# V2 Core Bot Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Replace the dead V1 dispatcher copilot with a working Q&A WhatsApp helpline bot: Postgres + pg-boss worker, Claude Sonnet 5 through the owner's Max plan via the Agent SDK, token-based phone-number rendering with a scrubber, a no-LLM fallback path, an admin transcript page, the test-chat harness, and an eval suite. Runs locally against Docker Postgres; the Azure rollout, media polish and take-over UI are a second plan.

**Architecture:** The Next.js app only ingests Interakt webhooks (verify, dedup, store, enqueue) and serves admin pages. A separate long-running worker consumes `turn` jobs from a Postgres-backed pg-boss queue, gathers every unanswered message of a conversation, runs one Claude turn with three in-process tools that return opaque handles instead of phone numbers, substitutes `{{AMB:handle}}` tokens with real numbers in code, scrubs any stray digits, sends via Interakt and records a `turns` row. When Claude is paused or fails, a pure-code fallback still delivers directory numbers.

**Tech Stack:** Next.js 16.2 (App Router, Node runtime), TypeScript 5, Node 22+, Postgres 17, drizzle-orm 0.45 + drizzle-kit 0.31 + postgres.js 3.4, pg-boss 12, `@anthropic-ai/claude-agent-sdk` 0.3.282 with zod 4, pino 9, vitest 3, yaml 2, Docker Compose (Postgres only in this plan).

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-25-v2-qa-helpline-design.md` — read it first; every task below cites the section it implements.

## Global Constraints

- Phone numbers are never authored by the model: tools return handles, code substitutes numbers, the scrubber blocks any 7+ digit run code did not insert (spec §5.9, hard rule 1).
- No dispatching language ever reaches a reporter: "we're sending", "on the way", "dispatched" and the Hindi/Marathi/Gujarati equivalents are replaced by "Please call them directly and they will guide you." (spec §5.9 step 5).
- Languages: `en | hi | mr | gu`; cards, fallback lines, welcome and menu exist in all four (spec §5.14).
- Model: `claude-sonnet-5`, effort `medium`, max 6 tool rounds, 45 000 ms timeout, Agent SDK pinned to `0.3.282`, zod `^4` (spec §3, §5.4). Never bare mode.
- Queue: pg-boss queue `turn`, policy `singleton`, `singletonKey = conversationId`, `singletonSeconds = 3`, `startAfter = 2`, `retryLimit = 2`, `retryBackoff = true`, `expireInSeconds = 120` (spec §5.2).
- Webhook returns 200 within milliseconds; no LLM call, no outbound send, no `after()` in the request path (spec §5.1). Bodies over 256 KB are rejected (spec §10).
- Raw webhook bodies are logged only when `LOG_RAW_WEBHOOKS=1`; phones are masked to the last 4 digits in logs (spec §8).
- Test conversations (`is_test = true`, phone `+91TEST_…`) never reach Interakt (spec §5.16).
- Commit after every task; never squash history (owner's rule).

## Review Focus

Five inputs the spec implies but no task's tests fully exercise; each has a pinned test in the owning task:

1. **A reporter sends the same message twice within a second** (WhatsApp resend). Expected: one stored message, one reply. Pinned in Task 10 (duplicate `provider_message_id`) and Task 11 (coalescing gathers one message).
2. **Claude returns a reply that references a handle from an earlier turn** ("the number I gave you was {{AMB:amb_1a2b3c4d}}"). Expected: the token resolves from the persisted registry, not dropped. Pinned in Task 21.
3. **The directory has two units with the same city and no area** (Rajkot's partner NGOs). Expected: a multi-card with both, each labelled with its operator. Pinned in Task 3 and Task 13.
4. **A reporter writes the whole message in Gujarati script for a covered city** while Claude is paused. Expected: the fallback still finds the city via Indic aliases and sends a card in Gujarati. Pinned in Task 13.
5. **Interakt sends `message_received` with `chat_message_type: "PublicApiMessage"`** (an echo of our own outbound). Expected: ignored, never stored as a reporter message. Pinned in Task 7.

## File Structure

New code lives under `src/server/` (shared by the app and the worker), `src/app/` (Next.js routes and pages), `evals/`, `tests/`, `scripts/`, `docker/`. Everything Supabase/Vercel/cron/escalation-related is deleted in Task 1.

| Path | Responsibility |
|---|---|
| `src/server/types.ts` | Shared unions: `Language`, `MessageKind`, `MessageOrigin`, `Intent` |
| `src/server/config.ts` | Env parsing with zod; `getConfig()` |
| `src/server/log.ts` | pino logger; `maskPhone()` |
| `src/server/db/schema.ts` | drizzle tables (spec §6) |
| `src/server/db/client.ts` | postgres.js + drizzle instance; `getDb()`, `getSql()`, `closeDb()` |

| `src/server/messages/cards.ts` | ambulance/clinic/helpline cards, `formatIndianPhone`, partner suffix |
| `src/server/messages/welcome.ts` | welcome text, menu text, interactive list rows |
| `src/server/messages/system-replies.ts` | audio-not-supported, no-match, call-directly lines |
| `src/server/directory/aliases.ts` | city aliases incl. Devanagari/Gujarati spellings |
| `src/server/directory/fuzzy.ts` | Levenshtein |
| `src/server/directory/neighbors.ts` | neighbour map |
| `src/server/directory/matcher.ts` | `matchAmbulances()` tiers (spec §5.8) |
| `src/server/directory/repo.ts` | load active ambulances/clinics with operator |
| `src/server/directory/coverage.ts` | coverage summary for the prompt, cached 1 h |
| `src/server/interakt/client.ts` | `sendText`, `sendInteractiveList`, `splitPhone` |
| `src/server/interakt/webhook.ts` | signature verification, payload parsing (spec §5.1) |
| `src/server/queue/boss.ts` | pg-boss bootstrap, `enqueueTurn` |

| `src/server/worker/deliver.ts` | send (or skip for test) + persist assistant message |

| `src/server/worker/fallback.ts` | no-LLM turn (spec §5.10) |
| `src/server/worker/pause.ts` | `worker_state`: Claude pause, heartbeat |

| `src/server/worker/index.ts` | worker entrypoint |
| `src/server/agent/types.ts` | `LLMBackend`, `TurnInput`, `TurnResult`, `StructuredReply` |
| `src/server/agent/schema.ts` | JSON schema + zod for the structured reply (spec §5.6) |
| `src/server/agent/handles.ts` | handle generation + registry (spec §5.7) |
| `src/server/agent/tools.ts` | `find_ambulances`, `find_clinics`, `get_info` as SDK MCP tools |
| `src/server/agent/transcript.ts` | render messages for the model |
| `src/server/agent/prompt.ts` | system prompt + per-turn prompt (spec §5.5) |
| `src/server/agent/render.ts` | token substitution, card fallback, format cleanup |
| `src/server/agent/scrub.ts` | digit-run scrubber, dispatch-language guard |
| `src/server/agent/claude-code-backend.ts` | Agent SDK backend (spec §5.4) |
| `src/server/agent/fake-backend.ts` | scripted backend for tests and evals |
| `src/server/agent/index.ts` | `getBackend()` |

- [ ] **Step 7: Implement `src/server/config.ts`**

```ts
import { z } from "zod";

const e164 = z.string().regex(/^\+[1-9]\d{7,14}$/, "must be E.164 like +919876543210");

const envSchema = z.object({
  NODE_ENV: z.string().default("development"),
  DATABASE_URL: z.string().min(1, "DATABASE_URL is required"),
  INTERAKT_API_KEY: z.string().min(1),
  INTERAKT_WEBHOOK_SECRET: z.string().default(""),
  INTERAKT_WEBHOOK_VERIFY_MODE: z.enum(["hmac", "off"]).default("hmac"),
  INTERAKT_API_BASE: z.string().url().default("https://api.interakt.ai/v1/public/message/"),
  LLM_BACKEND: z.enum(["claude-code", "fake"]).default("claude-code"),
  CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN: z.string().optional(),
  CLAUDE_TOKEN_ISSUED_AT: z.string().optional(),
  CLAUDE_MODEL: z.string().default("claude-sonnet-5"),
  CLAUDE_EFFORT: z.enum(["low", "medium", "high", "xhigh", "max"]).default("medium"),
  CLAUDE_MAX_TURNS: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(20).default(6),
  CLAUDE_TURN_TIMEOUT_MS: z.coerce.number().int().min(5_000).default(45_000),
  CLAUDE_HOME_DIR: z.string().default("/tmp/arham-claude-home"),
  HELPLINE_PHONE: e164.describe("HELPLINE_PHONE"),
  WEBSITE_URL: z.string().url().default("https://arhamalwayscare.org"),
  ADMIN_PASSWORD: z.string().min(6),
  SESSION_SECRET: z.string().min(32),
  WORKER_CONCURRENCY: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(16).default(4),
  LOG_LEVEL: z.string().default("info"),
  LOG_RAW_WEBHOOKS: z.string().default("0"),
});

export interface AppConfig {
  nodeEnv: string;
  isProduction: boolean;
  databaseUrl: string;
  interakt: { apiKey: string; webhookSecret: string; verifyMode: "hmac" | "off"; apiBase: string };
  llmBackend: "claude-code" | "fake";
  claude: {
    oauthToken?: string;
    tokenIssuedAt?: string;
    model: string;
    effort: "low" | "medium" | "high" | "xhigh" | "max";
    maxTurns: number;
    turnTimeoutMs: number;
    homeDir: string;
  };
  helplinePhone: string;
  websiteUrl: string;
  admin: { password: string; sessionSecret: string };
  workerConcurrency: number;
  logLevel: string;
  logRawWebhooks: boolean;
}

let cached: AppConfig | null = null;

export function getConfig(): AppConfig {
  if (cached) return cached;
  const parsed = envSchema.safeParse(process.env);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    const issues = parsed.error.issues.map((i) => `${i.path.join(".")}: ${i.message}`).join("; ");
    throw new Error(`Invalid environment: ${issues}`);
  }
  const e = parsed.data;
  cached = {
    nodeEnv: e.NODE_ENV,
    isProduction: e.NODE_ENV === "production",
    databaseUrl: e.DATABASE_URL,
    interakt: {
      apiKey: e.INTERAKT_API_KEY,
      webhookSecret: e.INTERAKT_WEBHOOK_SECRET,
      verifyMode: e.INTERAKT_WEBHOOK_VERIFY_MODE,
      apiBase: e.INTERAKT_API_BASE,
    },
    llmBackend: e.LLM_BACKEND,
    claude: {
      oauthToken: e.CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN,
      tokenIssuedAt: e.CLAUDE_TOKEN_ISSUED_AT,
      model: e.CLAUDE_MODEL,
      effort: e.CLAUDE_EFFORT,
      maxTurns: e.CLAUDE_MAX_TURNS,
      turnTimeoutMs: e.CLAUDE_TURN_TIMEOUT_MS,
      homeDir: e.CLAUDE_HOME_DIR,
    },
    helplinePhone: e.HELPLINE_PHONE,
    websiteUrl: e.WEBSITE_URL,
    admin: { password: e.ADMIN_PASSWORD, sessionSecret: e.SESSION_SECRET },
    workerConcurrency: e.WORKER_CONCURRENCY,
    logLevel: e.LOG_LEVEL,
    logRawWebhooks: e.LOG_RAW_WEBHOOKS === "1",
  };
  return cached;
}

/** Tests mutate process.env between cases; call this to drop the memo. */
export function resetConfigForTests(): void {
  cached = null;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 11: Implement `src/server/log.ts`**

```ts
import pino from "pino";

export const log = pino({
  level: process.env.LOG_LEVEL ?? "info",
  base: { service: process.env.SERVICE_NAME ?? "arham" },
  redact: { paths: ["*.body", "req.headers.authorization"], remove: true },
});

/** "+919876543210" → "+91xxxxxx3210". Non-numeric tails (test phones) are left alone. */
export function maskPhone(phone: string): string {
  if (!phone) return phone;
  const m = phone.match(/^(\+?\d{0,3})(\d+)(\d{4})$/);
  if (!m) return phone;
  return `${m[1]}${"x".repeat(m[2].length)}${m[3]}`;
}
```

### Task 2: Database schema, client, migrations, dev Postgres

Implements spec §6.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/server/types.ts` (rewrite of the moved file), `src/server/db/schema.ts`, `src/server/db/client.ts`, `drizzle.config.ts`, `scripts/migrate.ts`, `docker/docker-compose.dev.yml`, `docker/init/01-test-db.sql`, `tests/global-setup.ts` (replace), `tests/helpers/db.ts`, `tests/helpers/fixtures.ts`, `tests/db/schema.test.ts`
- Generated: `src/server/db/migrations/0000_*.sql` + `meta/`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: drizzle tables `operators, ambulances, clinics, conversations, turns, messages, events, workerState`; inferred row types `Conversation, Message, Turn, Ambulance, Clinic, Operator, NewMessage, NewTurn`; `getDb()`, `getSql()`, `closeDb()`; test helpers `resetDb()`, `seedDirectory()` returning `{ ghatkopar, malad, rajkotA, rajkotB, andheri, bandra, chembur, mulund, surat, bengaluru, puneA, puneB, clinicSurat }` ids and `createConversation({ phone?, language?, isTest? })`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write `src/server/types.ts`**

```ts
export type Language = "en" | "hi" | "mr" | "gu";
export const LANGUAGES: Language[] = ["en", "hi", "mr", "gu"];

export type MessageKind =
  | "text" | "audio" | "image" | "video" | "document" | "location" | "sticker" | "contact" | "interactive";

export type MessageOrigin = "reporter" | "agent" | "fallback" | "human" | "system";

export type Intent =
  | "ambulance" | "clinic" | "donate" | "volunteer" | "faq" | "suggestion" | "smalltalk" | "other";

export type TurnStatus = "ok" | "fallback" | "failed";
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write `src/server/db/schema.ts`**

```ts
import { sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import {
  pgTable, uuid, text, boolean, timestamp, jsonb, integer, doublePrecision, index, uniqueIndex,
} from "drizzle-orm/pg-core";
import type { Language, MessageKind, MessageOrigin, TurnStatus } from "../types";

const ts = (name: string) => timestamp(name, { withTimezone: true, mode: "date" });

export const operators = pgTable("operators", {
  id: uuid("id").primaryKey().defaultRandom(),
  name: text("name").notNull().unique(),
  isArham: boolean("is_arham").notNull().default(false),
  active: boolean("active").notNull().default(true),
  createdAt: ts("created_at").notNull().defaultNow(),
});

export const ambulances = pgTable(
  "ambulances",
  {
    id: uuid("id").primaryKey().defaultRandom(),
    operatorId: uuid("operator_id").notNull().references(() => operators.id),
    label: text("label").notNull().unique(),
    city: text("city").notNull(),
    area: text("area"),
    state: text("state").notNull(),
    phone: text("phone").notNull(),
    phoneRaw: text("phone_raw"),
    areasCovered: text("areas_covered").array().notNull().default(sql`'{}'::text[]`),
    category: text("category").notNull().default("Animal Ambulance"),
    active: boolean("active").notNull().default(true),
    updatedAt: ts("updated_at").notNull().defaultNow(),
    createdAt: ts("created_at").notNull().defaultNow(),
  },
  (t) => [index("ambulances_city_idx").on(t.city), index("ambulances_operator_idx").on(t.operatorId)]
);

export const clinics = pgTable("clinics", {
  id: uuid("id").primaryKey().defaultRandom(),
  operatorId: uuid("operator_id").notNull().references(() => operators.id),
  label: text("label").notNull().unique(),
  city: text("city").notNull(),
  area: text("area"),
  state: text("state").notNull(),
  phone: text("phone").notNull(),
  phoneRaw: text("phone_raw"),
  address: text("address"),
  hours: text("hours"),
  active: boolean("active").notNull().default(true),
  createdAt: ts("created_at").notNull().defaultNow(),
});

export const conversations = pgTable(
  "conversations",
  {
    id: uuid("id").primaryKey().defaultRandom(),
    phone: text("phone").notNull().unique(),
    name: text("name"),
    language: text("language").$type<Language>(),
    mode: text("mode").$type<"agent" | "human">().notNull().default("agent"),
    flagged: boolean("flagged").notNull().default(false),
    flagReason: text("flag_reason"),
    welcomedAt: ts("welcomed_at"),
    lastInboundAt: ts("last_inbound_at"),
    lastOutboundAt: ts("last_outbound_at"),
    isTest: boolean("is_test").notNull().default(false),
    createdAt: ts("created_at").notNull().defaultNow(),
    updatedAt: ts("updated_at").notNull().defaultNow(),
  },
  (t) => [
    index("conversations_updated_idx").on(t.updatedAt),
    index("conversations_flagged_idx").on(t.flagged),
    index("conversations_is_test_idx").on(t.isTest),
  ]
);

export const turns = pgTable(
  "turns",
  {
    id: uuid("id").primaryKey().defaultRandom(),
    conversationId: uuid("conversation_id").notNull().references(() => conversations.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    status: text("status").$type<TurnStatus>().notNull(),
    model: text("model"),
    effort: text("effort"),
    structuredOutput: jsonb("structured_output"),
    toolCalls: jsonb("tool_calls"),
    handles: jsonb("handles"),
    usage: jsonb("usage"),
    latencyMs: integer("latency_ms"),
    sessionId: text("session_id"),
    error: text("error"),
    scrubbed: boolean("scrubbed").notNull().default(false),
    createdAt: ts("created_at").notNull().defaultNow(),
  },
  (t) => [index("turns_conversation_idx").on(t.conversationId, t.createdAt)]
);

export const messages = pgTable(
  "messages",
  {
    id: uuid("id").primaryKey().defaultRandom(),
    conversationId: uuid("conversation_id").notNull().references(() => conversations.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    role: text("role").$type<"user" | "assistant">().notNull(),
    origin: text("origin").$type<MessageOrigin>().notNull(),
    content: text("content").notNull().default(""),
    contentTemplate: text("content_template"),
    kind: text("kind").$type<MessageKind>().notNull().default("text"),
    mediaUrl: text("media_url"),
    transcript: text("transcript"),
    lat: doublePrecision("lat"),
    lng: doublePrecision("lng"),
    placeLabel: text("place_label"),
    providerMessageId: text("provider_message_id"),
    interactiveId: text("interactive_id"),
    deliveryStatus: text("delivery_status"),
    error: text("error"),
    answeredTurnId: uuid("answered_turn_id").references(() => turns.id, { onDelete: "set null" }),
    createdAt: ts("created_at").notNull().defaultNow(),
  },
  (t) => [
    index("messages_conversation_idx").on(t.conversationId, t.createdAt),
    uniqueIndex("messages_provider_message_id_idx").on(t.providerMessageId),
    index("messages_pending_idx")
      .on(t.conversationId)
      .where(sql`${t.role} = 'user' and ${t.answeredTurnId} is null`),
  ]
);

export const events = pgTable(
  "events",
  {
    id: uuid("id").primaryKey().defaultRandom(),
    conversationId: uuid("conversation_id"),
    kind: text("kind").notNull(),
    payload: jsonb("payload"),
    createdAt: ts("created_at").notNull().defaultNow(),
  },
  (t) => [index("events_kind_idx").on(t.kind, t.createdAt)]
);

export const workerState = pgTable("worker_state", {
  singleton: boolean("singleton").primaryKey().default(true),
  claudePausedUntil: ts("claude_paused_until"),
  pauseReason: text("pause_reason"),
  lastHeartbeatAt: ts("last_heartbeat_at"),
  lastOkTurnAt: ts("last_ok_turn_at"),
});

export type Operator = typeof operators.$inferSelect;
export type Ambulance = typeof ambulances.$inferSelect;
export type Clinic = typeof clinics.$inferSelect;
export type Conversation = typeof conversations.$inferSelect;
export type Message = typeof messages.$inferSelect;
export type NewMessage = typeof messages.$inferInsert;
export type Turn = typeof turns.$inferSelect;
export type NewTurn = typeof turns.$inferInsert;
export type Event = typeof events.$inferSelect;
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/server/db/client.ts`**

```ts
import postgres from "postgres";
import { drizzle } from "drizzle-orm/postgres-js";
import * as schema from "./schema";
import { getConfig } from "../config";

let sqlClient: ReturnType<typeof postgres> | null = null;
let db: ReturnType<typeof drizzle<typeof schema>> | null = null;

export function getSql() {
  if (!sqlClient) {
    sqlClient = postgres(getConfig().databaseUrl, { max: 8, idle_timeout: 30, prepare: false });
  }
  return sqlClient;
}

export function getDb() {
  if (!db) db = drizzle(getSql(), { schema });
  return db;
}

export async function closeDb(): Promise<void> {
  if (sqlClient) await sqlClient.end({ timeout: 5 });
  sqlClient = null;
  db = null;
}

export type Db = ReturnType<typeof getDb>;
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `drizzle.config.ts` and `scripts/migrate.ts`**

`drizzle.config.ts`:

```ts
import { defineConfig } from "drizzle-kit";

export default defineConfig({
  schema: "./src/server/db/schema.ts",
  out: "./src/server/db/migrations",
  dialect: "postgresql",
  dbCredentials: { url: process.env.DATABASE_URL ?? "postgres://arham:arham@localhost:5432/arham" },
});
```

`scripts/migrate.ts`:

```ts
import { config as loadEnv } from "dotenv";
loadEnv({ path: ".env.local" });
loadEnv({ path: ".env" });
import { migrate } from "drizzle-orm/postgres-js/migrator";
import { getDb, closeDb } from "../src/server/db/client";

async function main() {
  const url = process.env.DATABASE_URL;
  if (!url) throw new Error("DATABASE_URL not set");
  console.log(`Migrating ${url.replace(/:\/\/.*@/, "://***@")}`);
  await migrate(getDb(), { migrationsFolder: "src/server/db/migrations" });
  console.log("Migrations applied.");
  await closeDb();
}

main().catch((e) => {
  console.error(e);
  process.exit(1);
});
```

```ts
import { sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { getDb, getSql } from "@/server/db/client";
import { conversations } from "@/server/db/schema";
import type { Language } from "@/server/types";

export async function resetDb(): Promise<void> {
  await getSql().unsafe(
    "truncate table messages, turns, events, conversations, clinics, ambulances, operators, worker_state restart identity cascade"
  );
  await getSql().unsafe("drop schema if exists pgboss cascade");
}

let counter = 0;
export async function createConversation(opts: {
  phone?: string;
  language?: Language | null;
  isTest?: boolean;
  mode?: "agent" | "human";
  welcomedAt?: Date | null;
} = {}) {
  counter += 1;
  const phone = opts.phone ?? `+9198765${String(10000 + counter).slice(-5)}`;
  const [row] = await getDb()
    .insert(conversations)
    .values({
      phone,
      language: opts.language ?? null,
      isTest: opts.isTest ?? false,
      mode: opts.mode ?? "agent",
      welcomedAt: opts.welcomedAt ?? null,
    })
    .returning();
  return row;
}

export const rawSql = sql;
```

```ts
import { getDb } from "@/server/db/client";

export interface DirectoryIds {

  hopeOperatorId: string;

  clinicSurat: string;

/** Minimal directory that exercises every matcher tier and both operator kinds. */
export async function seedDirectory(): Promise<DirectoryIds> {
  const db = getDb();
  const [arham] = await db.insert(operators).values({ name: "Arham Yuva Seva Group", isArham: true }).returning();
  const [blue] = await db.insert(operators).values({ name: "Blue Cross", isArham: false }).returning();
  const [hope] = await db.insert(operators).values({ name: "Hope for Indies Trust", isArham: false }).returning();

    label: string; city: string; area: string | null; state: string; phone: string; areas: string[]; op: string;

        phone: v.phone, phoneRaw: v.phone, areasCovered: v.areas,

  };

    arhamOperatorId: arham.id, blueCrossOperatorId: blue.id, hopeOperatorId: hope.id,
    ghatkopar: await amb({ label: "Mumbai - Ghatkopar", city: "Mumbai", area: "Ghatkopar", state: "Maharashtra", phone: "+917662005404", areas: ["Ghatkopar", "Vikhroli", "Kurla"], op: arham.id }),
```

`tests/db/schema.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from "vitest";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { getDb } from "@/server/db/client";
import { messages, conversations } from "@/server/db/schema";
import { resetDb, createConversation } from "../helpers/db";
import { seedDirectory } from "../helpers/fixtures";

describe("schema", () => {
  beforeEach(resetDb);

  it("seeds the directory fixture", async () => {
    const ids = await seedDirectory();
    expect(ids.ghatkopar).toMatch(/[0-9a-f-]{36}/);
  });

  it("rejects duplicate provider message ids", async () => {
    const c = await createConversation();
    await getDb().insert(messages).values({ conversationId: c.id, role: "user", origin: "reporter", content: "hi", providerMessageId: "m1" });
    await expect(
      getDb().insert(messages).values({ conversationId: c.id, role: "user", origin: "reporter", content: "hi again", providerMessageId: "m1" })
    ).rejects.toThrow(/duplicate key|unique/);
  });

  it("allows many messages without a provider id (test chat)", async () => {
    const c = await createConversation({ isTest: true });
    await getDb().insert(messages).values([
      { conversationId: c.id, role: "user", origin: "reporter", content: "a" },
      { conversationId: c.id, role: "user", origin: "reporter", content: "b" },
    ]);
    const rows = await getDb().select().from(messages).where(eq(messages.conversationId, c.id));
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it("defaults conversation mode to agent and flagged to false", async () => {
    const c = await createConversation();
    const [row] = await getDb().select().from(conversations).where(eq(conversations.id, c.id));
    expect(row.mode).toBe("agent");
    expect(row.flagged).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

```ts
  row({ id: "andheri", city: "Mumbai", area: "Andheri", areasCovered: ["Andheri", "Jogeshwari"] }),
  row({ id: "bandra", city: "Mumbai", area: "Bandra", areasCovered: ["Bandra", "Khar"] }),
  row({ id: "rajkotA", city: "Rajkot", areasCovered: ["Rajkot"], operatorName: "Blue Cross", operatorIsArham: false }),
  row({ id: "rajkotB", city: "Rajkot", areasCovered: ["Rajkot"], operatorName: "Hope for Indies Trust", operatorIsArham: false }),
  row({ id: "bengaluru", city: "Bengaluru", areasCovered: ["Bengaluru"] }),
  row({ id: "surat", city: "Surat", areasCovered: ["Surat", "Vesu"] }),
];
const ids = (r: { candidates: DirectoryAmbulance[] }) => r.candidates.map((c) => c.id).sort();

describe("normalize", () => {
  it("lowercases, strips punctuation, applies aliases", () => {
    expect(normalize("Munbai - Goregaon!")).toBe("munbai goregaon");
    expect(normalize("Bangalore Indiranagar")).toBe("bengaluru indiranagar");
    expect(normalize("मुंबई घाटकोपर")).toBe("mumbai ghatkopar");
    expect(normalize("સુરત")).toBe("surat");
  });
});

describe("matchAmbulances", () => {
  it("tier area: exact area wins over city", () => {
    const r = matchAmbulances(rows, { city: "Mumbai", area: "Ghatkopar", query: "ghatkopar" });
    expect(ids(r)).toEqual(["ghatkopar"]);
    expect(r.match).toBe("area");
  });

  it("tier city: city with many units returns all and the area list", () => {
    const r = matchAmbulances(rows, { city: "Mumbai", area: null, query: "mumbai" });
    expect(ids(r)).toEqual(["andheri", "bandra", "ghatkopar", "malad"]);
    expect(r.match).toBe("city");
    expect(r.cityAreas).toEqual(["Andheri", "Bandra", "Ghatkopar", "Malad"]);
  });

  it("city with two partner units and no areas returns both (Rajkot)", () => {
    const r = matchAmbulances(rows, { query: "rajkot" });
    expect(ids(r)).toEqual(["rajkotA", "rajkotB"]);
    expect(r.cityAreas).toBeNull();
  });

  it("alias: Bangalore resolves to Bengaluru", () => {
    expect(ids(matchAmbulances(rows, { query: "Bangalore" }))).toEqual(["bengaluru"]);
  });

  it("fuzzy: Munbai typo resolves to Mumbai units", () => {
    const r = matchAmbulances(rows, { query: "munbai" });
    expect(r.match).toBe("fuzzy");
    expect(ids(r)).toEqual(["andheri", "bandra", "ghatkopar", "malad"]);
  });

  it("neighbour: Goregaon maps to Malad/Andheri", () => {
    const r = matchAmbulances(rows, { city: "Mumbai", area: "Goregaon", query: "goregaon" });
    expect(r.match).toBe("neighbour");
    expect(ids(r)).toEqual(["andheri", "malad"]);
  });

  it("Devanagari area matches via aliases", () => {
    expect(ids(matchAmbulances(rows, { query: "घाटकोपर में कुत्ता" }))).toEqual(["ghatkopar"]);
  });

  it("Gujarati city matches via aliases", () => {
    expect(ids(matchAmbulances(rows, { query: "સુરતમાં ગાય ઘાયલ છે" }))).toEqual(["surat"]);
  });

  it("out of coverage returns empty", () => {
    const r = matchAmbulances(rows, { query: "Lucknow" });
    expect(r.candidates).toEqual([]);
    expect(r.outOfCoverage).toBe(true);
  });

  it("city restricts area search to that city", () => {
    const r = matchAmbulances(rows, { city: "Rajkot", area: "Ghatkopar", query: "ghatkopar rajkot" });
    expect(ids(r)).toEqual(["rajkotA", "rajkotB"]);
  });
});
```

```ts
/**
 * City aliases: colloquial / pre-rename / Indic-script spellings → canonical
 * Latin spelling used in the directory. Applied token-wise after lowercasing.
 */
const LATIN_ALIASES: Record<string, string> = {
  bangalore: "bengaluru", bombay: "mumbai", madras: "chennai", calcutta: "kolkata", mysore: "mysuru",
  mangalore: "mangaluru", gurgaon: "gurugram", baroda: "vadodara", cochin: "kochi", trivandrum: "thiruvananthapuram",
  pondicherry: "puducherry", poona: "pune", vizag: "visakhapatnam", belgaum: "belagavi", hubli: "hubballi",
  allahabad: "prayagraj", benares: "varanasi", banaras: "varanasi",
};

/** Devanagari (Hindi/Marathi) and Gujarati spellings of covered cities and common areas. */
const INDIC_ALIASES: Record<string, string> = {
  // cities — Devanagari
  "मुंबई": "mumbai", "मुम्बई": "mumbai", "पुणे": "pune", "नागपुर": "nagpur", "नागपूर": "nagpur", "सोलापुर": "solapur",
  "सोलापूर": "solapur", "अमरावती": "amravati", "दिल्ली": "delhi", "अहमदाबाद": "ahmedabad", "सूरत": "surat",
  "वडोदरा": "vadodara", "राजकोट": "rajkot", "जामनगर": "jamnagar", "भावनगर": "bhavnagar", "गांधीनगर": "gandhinagar",
  "इंदौर": "indore", "कोलकाता": "kolkata", "चेन्नई": "chennai", "हैदराबाद": "hyderabad", "बेंगलुरु": "bengaluru",
  "बैंगलोर": "bengaluru", "गुरुग्राम": "gurugram", "गुड़गांव": "gurugram",
  // areas — Devanagari
  "घाटकोपर": "ghatkopar", "अंधेरी": "andheri", "बांद्रा": "bandra", "मलाड": "malad", "कांदिवली": "kandivali",
  "बोरीवली": "borivali", "गोरेगांव": "goregaon", "गोरेगाव": "goregaon", "चेंबूर": "chembur", "मुलुंड": "mulund",
  "दादर": "dadar", "वडाला": "wadala", "ठाणे": "thane", "कल्याण": "kalyan", "डोंबिवली": "dombivali",
  "कोथरूड": "kothrud", "पिंपरी": "pimpri", "चिंचवड": "chinchwad", "हिंजवडी": "hinjewadi", "शिवाजीनगर": "shivajinagar",
  // cities — Gujarati
  "મુંબઈ": "mumbai", "અમદાવાદ": "ahmedabad", "સુરત": "surat", "વડોદરા": "vadodara", "રાજકોટ": "rajkot",
  "જામનગર": "jamnagar", "ભાવનગર": "bhavnagar", "ગાંધીનગર": "gandhinagar", "જૂનાગઢ": "junagadh", "મોરબી": "morbi",
  "ગોંડલ": "gondal", "વેરાવળ": "veraval", "પુણે": "pune", "દિલ્હી": "delhi",
  // areas — Gujarati
  "વેસુ": "vesu", "વરાછા": "varachha", "ઘાટકોપર": "ghatkopar", "અંધેરી": "andheri",
};

/** Strip common Indic case suffixes glued to place names ("सुरतमां", "મુંબઈમાં", "पुण्यात"). */
const INDIC_SUFFIXES = ["माँ", "मां", "में", "मे", "ला", "ात", "मध्ये", "માં", "મા", "ના", "થી"];

function stripIndicSuffix(token: string): string {
  for (const suf of INDIC_SUFFIXES) {
    if (token.length > suf.length + 1 && token.endsWith(suf)) return token.slice(0, -suf.length);
  }
  return token;
}

/**
 * Replace aliased tokens in an already lowercased, punctuation-free string.
 * Indic tokens are tried raw, then with a case suffix stripped.
 */
export function applyCityAliases(s: string): string {
  return s
    .split(" ")
    .map((token) => {
      if (LATIN_ALIASES[token]) return LATIN_ALIASES[token];
      if (INDIC_ALIASES[token]) return INDIC_ALIASES[token];
      const stripped = stripIndicSuffix(token);
      if (INDIC_ALIASES[stripped]) return INDIC_ALIASES[stripped];
      // Marathi locative "पुण्यात" → "पुणे" is irregular; handle the covered cities explicitly.
      if (token === "पुण्यात") return "pune";
      if (token === "मुंबईत" || token === "मुंबईला") return "mumbai";
      if (token === "नागपुरात") return "nagpur";
      return token;
    })
    .join(" ");
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/server/directory/matcher.ts`**

```ts
import { applyCityAliases } from "./aliases";
import { fuzzyEqual } from "./fuzzy";
import { neighborCandidates } from "./neighbors";

export interface DirectoryAmbulance {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  city: string;
  area: string | null;
  state: string;
  areasCovered: string[];
  category: string;
  phone: string;
  operatorName: string;
  operatorIsArham: boolean;
}

export type MatchType = "area" | "city" | "substring" | "token" | "fuzzy" | "neighbour";

export interface MatchResult {
  candidates: DirectoryAmbulance[];
  match: MatchType | null;
  /** Distinct areas when a city matched with more than three units, so the model can ask. */
  cityAreas: string[] | null;
  outOfCoverage: boolean;
}

export interface MatchInput {
  city?: string | null;
  area?: string | null;
  query: string;
}

/** Lowercase, strip punctuation, collapse spaces, apply city aliases (Latin + Indic). */
export function normalize(s: string): string {
  return applyCityAliases(
    (s ?? "")
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}\s]/gu, " ")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .trim()
  );
}

const tokensOf = (q: string) => q.split(" ").filter((t) => t.length >= 4);

function cityMatches(rowCity: string, wanted: string): boolean {
  const c = normalize(rowCity);
  if (c === wanted) return true;
  return fuzzyEqual(c, wanted);
}

function finish(candidates: DirectoryAmbulance[], match: MatchType): MatchResult {
  const sorted = [...candidates].sort((a, b) => a.label.localeCompare(b.label));
  let cityAreas: string[] | null = null;
  if (sorted.length > 3) {
    const areas = Array.from(new Set(sorted.map((r) => r.area).filter((a): a is string => !!a))).sort();
    if (areas.length > 0) cityAreas = areas;
  }
  return { candidates: sorted, match, cityAreas, outOfCoverage: false };
}

/**
 * Tiered lookup: area exact → city exact → substring → token → fuzzy → neighbour.
 * `city` restricts the row set first; `area` is searched before the free-text query.
 */
export function matchAmbulances(rows: DirectoryAmbulance[], input: MatchInput): MatchResult {
  const q = normalize(input.query);
  const wantedCity = input.city ? normalize(input.city) : "";
  const wantedArea = input.area ? normalize(input.area) : "";

  let pool = rows;
  if (wantedCity) {
    const restricted = rows.filter((r) => cityMatches(r.city, wantedCity));
    if (restricted.length > 0) pool = restricted;
  }

  const needles = [wantedArea, q].filter((n) => n.length > 0);

  // Tier 1 — area exact (row.area or any areas_covered equals the needle or one of its tokens).
  for (const needle of needles) {
    const toks = tokensOf(needle);
    const hit = pool.filter((r) => {
      const areas = [r.area ?? "", ...r.areasCovered].map(normalize).filter(Boolean);
      return areas.some((a) => a === needle || toks.includes(a));
    });
    if (hit.length > 0) return finish(hit, "area");
  }

  // Tier 2 — city exact (whole needle or a single token equals the city).
  for (const needle of needles) {
    const toks = tokensOf(needle);
    const hit = pool.filter((r) => {
      const c = normalize(r.city);
      return c === needle || toks.includes(c);
    });
    if (hit.length > 0) return finish(hit, "city");
  }

  // Tier 3 — substring (needle inside label/city/area/areas_covered).
  for (const needle of needles) {
    if (needle.length < 4) continue;
    const hit = pool.filter((r) =>
      [r.label, r.city, r.area ?? "", ...r.areasCovered].map(normalize).some((h) => h.length >= 4 && h.includes(needle))
    );
    if (hit.length > 0) return finish(hit, "substring");
  }

  // Tier 4 — token containment.
  for (const needle of needles) {
    const toks = tokensOf(needle);
    const hit = pool.filter((r) =>
      [r.label, r.city, r.area ?? "", ...r.areasCovered].map(normalize).some((h) => toks.some((t) => h === t || h.includes(t)))
    );
    if (hit.length > 0) return finish(hit, "token");
  }

  // Tier 5 — fuzzy token vs city/area.
  for (const needle of needles) {
    const toks = tokensOf(needle);
    const hit = pool.filter((r) => {
      const hay = [r.city, r.area ?? "", ...r.areasCovered].map(normalize).filter((h) => h.length >= 4);
      return toks.some((t) => hay.some((h) => fuzzyEqual(t, h)));
    });
    if (hit.length > 0) return finish(hit, "fuzzy");
  }

  // Tier 6 — neighbour map, on the whole needle and each token.
  const neighbourQueries = new Set<string>();
  for (const needle of needles) for (const k of [needle, ...tokensOf(needle)]) for (const n of neighborCandidates(k)) neighbourQueries.add(n);
  for (const nq of neighbourQueries) {
    const nn = normalize(nq);
    const hit = pool.filter((r) =>
      [r.area ?? "", ...r.areasCovered, r.city].map(normalize).some((h) => h === nn || (nn.length >= 4 && h.includes(nn)))
    );
    if (hit.length > 0) return finish(hit, "neighbour");
  }

  return { candidates: [], match: null, cityAreas: null, outOfCoverage: true };
}
```

Keep `fuzzy.ts` and `neighbors.ts` exactly as moved (they already export `levenshtein`, `fuzzyEqual`, `neighborCandidates`).

- [ ] **Step 3: Rewrite `src/server/messages/cards.ts`**

```ts
import type { Language } from "../types";

export interface CardAmbulance {
  city: string;
  area: string | null;
  phone: string;
  operatorName: string;
  operatorIsArham: boolean;
}

export interface CardClinic {
  label: string;
  city: string;
  area: string | null;
  phone: string;
  address?: string | null;
  hours?: string | null;
}

/** "+917662005404" → "+91 76620 05404". Non-Indian numbers are returned as-is with a "+". */
export function formatIndianPhone(e164: string): string {
  if (!e164) return e164;
  const digits = e164.replace(/\D/g, "");
  if (digits.length === 12 && digits.startsWith("91")) {
    const local = digits.slice(2);
    return `+91 ${local.slice(0, 5)} ${local.slice(5)}`;
  }
  if (digits.length === 10) return `+91 ${digits.slice(0, 5)} ${digits.slice(5)}`;
  return e164.startsWith("+") ? e164 : `+${e164}`;
}

const CALL_PROMPT: Record<Language, string> = {
  en: "Please call them right away.",
  hi: "कृपया उन्हें तुरंत कॉल करें।",
  mr: "कृपया त्यांना लगेच कॉल करा.",
  gu: "કૃપા કરીને તેમને તરત જ કૉલ કરો.",
};

const OPERATED_BY: Record<Language, (op: string) => string> = {
  en: (op) => `(operated by ${op})`,
  hi: (op) => `(${op} द्वारा संचालित)`,
  mr: (op) => `(${op} द्वारे चालवले जाते)`,
  gu: (op) => `(${op} દ્વારા સંચાલિત)`,
};

export function partnerSuffix(operatorName: string, lang: Language): string {
  return (OPERATED_BY[lang] ?? OPERATED_BY.en)(operatorName);
}

export function ambulanceDisplayName(row: CardAmbulance): string {
  const place = row.area && row.area.trim().length > 0 ? row.area : row.city;
  return row.operatorIsArham ? `Arham Animal Ambulance, ${place}` : `Animal Ambulance, ${place}`;
}

/** What replaces an {{AMB:handle}} token inside the model's sentence. */
export function renderNumberForToken(row: CardAmbulance, lang: Language): string {
  const phone = formatIndianPhone(row.phone);
  return row.operatorIsArham ? phone : `${phone} ${partnerSuffix(row.operatorName, lang)}`;
}

export function buildAmbulanceCard(row: CardAmbulance, lang: Language = "en"): string {
  const lines = [ambulanceDisplayName(row), formatIndianPhone(row.phone)];
  if (!row.operatorIsArham) lines.push(partnerSuffix(row.operatorName, lang));
  lines.push(CALL_PROMPT[lang] ?? CALL_PROMPT.en);
  return lines.join("\n");
}

const MULTI_HEADER: Record<Language, (n: number, place: string) => string> = {
  en: (n, p) => `${n} ambulances are available in ${p}. Please call any one of them:`,
  hi: (n, p) => `${p} में ${n} एम्बुलेंस उपलब्ध हैं। कृपया इनमें से किसी एक को कॉल करें:`,
  mr: (n, p) => `${p} मध्ये ${n} एम्बुलन्स उपलब्ध आहेत. कृपया यांपैकी कोणालाही कॉल करा:`,
  gu: (n, p) => `${p} માં ${n} એમ્બ્યુલન્સ ઉપલબ્ધ છે. કૃપા કરીને કોઈ પણ એકને કૉલ કરો:`,
};

export function buildMultiAmbulanceCard(rows: CardAmbulance[], lang: Language = "en"): string {
  if (rows.length === 0) return "";
  if (rows.length === 1) return buildAmbulanceCard(rows[0], lang);
  const lines: string[] = [(MULTI_HEADER[lang] ?? MULTI_HEADER.en)(rows.length, rows[0].city), ""];
  rows.forEach((row, i) => {
    lines.push(`${i + 1}. ${ambulanceDisplayName(row)}`);
    lines.push(`   ${formatIndianPhone(row.phone)}`);
    if (!row.operatorIsArham) lines.push(`   ${partnerSuffix(row.operatorName, lang)}`);
    lines.push("");
  });
  return lines.join("\n").trimEnd();
}

const HELPLINE_INTRO: Record<Language, string> = {
  en: "Please call the Arham Always Care helpline and our team will help you:",
  hi: "कृपया Arham Always Care हेल्पलाइन पर कॉल करें, हमारी टीम आपकी मदद करेगी:",
  mr: "कृपया Arham Always Care हेल्पलाइनला कॉल करा, आमची टीम तुम्हाला मदत करेल:",
  gu: "કૃપા કરીને Arham Always Care હેલ્પલાઇન પર કૉલ કરો, અમારી ટીમ તમને મદદ કરશે:",
};

export function buildHelplineCard(phone: string, lang: Language = "en"): string {
  return `${HELPLINE_INTRO[lang] ?? HELPLINE_INTRO.en}\n${formatIndianPhone(phone)}`;
}

const CLINIC_HEADER: Record<Language, string> = { en: "Our clinics", hi: "हमारे क्लिनिक", mr: "आमचे क्लिनिक", gu: "અમારા ક્લિનિક" };
const NO_CLINICS: Record<Language, string> = {
  en: "We don't have a clinic in that city yet. Visit alwayscare.org/clinics for the full list.",
  hi: "उस शहर में अभी हमारा क्लिनिक नहीं है। पूरी सूची के लिए alwayscare.org/clinics देखें।",
  mr: "त्या शहरात अजून आमचे क्लिनिक नाही. संपूर्ण यादीसाठी alwayscare.org/clinics पहा.",
  gu: "તે શહેરમાં હજી અમારું ક્લિનિક નથી. સંપૂર્ણ યાદી માટે alwayscare.org/clinics જુઓ.",
};

export function buildClinicCard(rows: CardClinic[], lang: Language = "en"): string {
  if (rows.length === 0) return NO_CLINICS[lang] ?? NO_CLINICS.en;
  const lines: string[] = [CLINIC_HEADER[lang] ?? CLINIC_HEADER.en, ""];
  for (const c of rows.slice(0, 5)) {
    lines.push(`*${c.label}* (${c.area ? `${c.city} — ${c.area}` : c.city})`);
    lines.push(`📞 ${formatIndianPhone(c.phone)}`);
    if (c.address) lines.push(`📍 ${c.address}`);
    if (c.hours) lines.push(`🕒 ${c.hours}`);
    lines.push("");
  }
  return lines.join("\n").trimEnd();
}
```

```ts
  hi: { "menu:ambulance": "एनिमल एम्बुलेंस", "menu:clinic": "क्लिनिक जानकारी", "menu:donate": "दान", "menu:volunteer": "वालंटियर", "menu:suggestion": "सुझाव" },
  mr: { "menu:ambulance": "एनिमल एम्बुलन्स", "menu:clinic": "क्लिनिक माहिती", "menu:donate": "दान", "menu:volunteer": "स्वयंसेवक", "menu:suggestion": "सूचना" },
  gu: { "menu:ambulance": "એનિમલ એમ્બ્યુલન્સ", "menu:clinic": "ક્લિનિક માહિતી", "menu:donate": "દાન", "menu:volunteer": "સ્વયંસેવક", "menu:suggestion": "સૂચન" },
};

const MENU_ORDER: MenuId[] = ["menu:ambulance", "menu:clinic", "menu:donate", "menu:volunteer", "menu:suggestion"];

/** Rows for the Interakt InteractiveList (titles ≤ 24 chars per WhatsApp). */
export function menuRows(lang: Language = "en"): { id: MenuId; title: string }[] {
  const titles = ROW_TITLES[lang] ?? ROW_TITLES.en;
  return MENU_ORDER.map((id) => ({ id, title: titles[id] }));
}

/** "menu:ambulance" → "ambulance" for the transcript; null for unknown ids. */
export function menuChoiceLabel(id: string): string | null {
  return (MENU_ORDER as string[]).includes(id) ? id.slice("menu:".length) : null;
}
```

```ts
import type { Language } from "../types";

const pick = (m: Record<Language, string>, lang: Language) => m[lang] ?? m.en;

export function audioNotSupported(lang: Language): string {
  return pick({
    en: "Sorry, I can't listen to voice notes yet. Please type your message and I'll help right away.",
    hi: "माफ़ कीजिए, मैं अभी वॉइस नोट नहीं सुन सकता। कृपया अपना संदेश लिखकर भेजें, मैं तुरंत मदद करूँगा।",
    mr: "क्षमस्व, मी अजून व्हॉइस नोट ऐकू शकत नाही. कृपया तुमचा संदेश लिहून पाठवा, मी लगेच मदत करेन.",
    gu: "માફ કરશો, હું હજી વૉઇસ નોટ સાંભળી શકતો નથી. કૃપા કરીને તમારો સંદેશ લખીને મોકલો, હું તરત મદદ કરીશ.",
  }, lang);
}

/** First line of a fallback reply when the directory matched. */
export function fallbackFoundIntro(lang: Language): string {
  return pick({
    en: "Here is the animal ambulance for your area:",
    hi: "आपके इलाके की एनिमल एम्बुलेंस यह रही:",
    mr: "तुमच्या भागातील एनिमल एम्बुलन्स ही आहे:",
    gu: "તમારા વિસ્તારની એનિમલ એમ્બ્યુલન્સ આ રહી:",
  }, lang);
}

/** First line of a fallback reply when nothing matched. */
export function fallbackNoMatch(lang: Language): string {
  return pick({
    en: "I couldn't find that area right now.",
    hi: "मुझे अभी वह इलाका नहीं मिल पाया।",
    mr: "मला आत्ता तो भाग सापडला नाही.",
    gu: "મને હાલ તે વિસ્તાર મળ્યો નહીં.",
  }, lang);
}

/** Replacement for a sentence that contained dispatch language. */
export function callDirectlyLine(lang: Language): string {
  return pick({
    en: "Please call them directly and they will guide you.",
    hi: "कृपया उन्हें सीधे कॉल करें, वे आपका मार्गदर्शन करेंगे।",
    mr: "कृपया त्यांना थेट कॉल करा, ते तुम्हाला मार्गदर्शन करतील.",
    gu: "કૃપા કરીને તેમને સીધો કૉલ કરો, તેઓ તમને માર્ગદર્શન આપશે.",
  }, lang);
}

export const TOKEN_CORRECTION_NOTE =
  "SYSTEM CORRECTION: your previous draft typed a phone number as digits. Phone numbers may only appear as tokens: {{AMB:<handle>}}, {{CLINIC:<handle>}} or {{PHONE:helpline}}. Rewrite the reply using tokens only.";

export const DISPATCH_CORRECTION_NOTE =
  "SYSTEM CORRECTION: your previous draft implied that Arham sends or dispatches the ambulance. We never dispatch; the reporter calls the driver directly. Rewrite the reply without any sending/dispatching/on-the-way language.";
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Rewrite `src/server/lang.ts`**

```ts
import type { Language } from "./types";

const SCRIPT_START = "(?:^|[\\s।,.!?;:\"'(\\[{])";
const SCRIPT_END = "(?:$|[\\s।,.!?;:\"')\\]}])";

/** Script-based detection with a small Hinglish keyword list. Defaults to English. */
export function detectLanguage(text: string): Language {
  if (!text) return "en";
  if (/[ऀ-ॿ]/.test(text)) {
    const marathi = new RegExp(`${SCRIPT_START}(आहे|आहेत|आहोत|नाही|काय|कुठे|कुत्रा|जखमी|मांजर|बघितल|पाहिल|करायच)${SCRIPT_END}`);
    return marathi.test(text) ? "mr" : "hi";
  }
  if (/[઀-૿]/.test(text)) return "gu";
  const hinglish = /\b(hai|hain|kaise|kaha|kahaan|kya|nahi|nahin|theek|jald|jaldi|krpya|kripaya|madad|ghayal|jakhmi|kutta|billi|janwar|bhai|bhaiya|didi|aap|hum|tum)\b/i;
  return hinglish.test(text) ? "hi" : "en";
}

const MENU_WORDS = /^(menu|options?|help|sahaay|मेन्यू|पर्याय|मेनू|menyu|મેનુ|વિકલ્પ)$/i;

export function isMenuWord(text: string): boolean {
  return MENU_WORDS.test((text ?? "").trim());
}

const GREETING_WORDS = /^(hi+|hello+|hey+|hii+|helo|namaste|namaskar|namaskaram|नमस्ते|नमस्कार|નમસ્તે|નમસ્કાર|good (morning|afternoon|evening)|ok|okay|thanks?|thank you|there|ji|hai)$/i;

/** True when the text carries no request: greetings, punctuation, emoji, menu words, empty. */
export function isGreetingOnly(text: string): boolean {
  const cleaned = (text ?? "")
    .replace(/[\p{Extended_Pictographic}\p{Emoji_Component}‍️]/gu, " ")
    .replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}\s]/gu, " ")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase();
  if (cleaned.length === 0) return true;
  if (isMenuWord(cleaned)) return true;
  return cleaned.split(" ").every((w) => GREETING_WORDS.test(w));
}
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import { getStaticInfo, getStaticPhones } from "@/server/content/static";

describe("getStaticInfo", () => {
  it("returns English donate content without TODO fields", () => {
    const c = getStaticInfo("donate", "en");
    expect(c.summary).toMatch(/donation/i);
    expect(c.url).toBe("https://alwayscare.org/donate");
    expect(JSON.stringify(c)).not.toMatch(/TODO/);
    expect(c.upi).toBeUndefined();
  });
  it("falls back to English for unfilled languages", () => {
    const c = getStaticInfo("donate", "gu");
    expect(c.summary).toMatch(/donation/i);
  });
  it("returns FAQ as question/answer pairs", () => {
    const faq = getStaticInfo("faq", "hi").faq as { question: string; answer: string }[];
    expect(faq.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3);
    expect(faq[0].question).toMatch(/free/i);
  });
  it("tokenizes phones in volunteer content and never exposes digits", () => {
    const c = getStaticInfo("volunteer", "en");
    expect(JSON.stringify(c)).not.toMatch(/\d{7,}/);
    const phones = getStaticPhones();
    expect(typeof phones).toBe("object");
  });
  it("returns the helpline token for the helpline topic", () => {
    const c = getStaticInfo("helpline", "mr");
    expect(c.phone).toBe("{{PHONE:helpline}}");
  });
  it("returns human emergency text with 112/108", () => {
    expect(getStaticInfo("human_emergency", "en").text).toMatch(/112|108/);
  });
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Rename the JSON key and rewrite the loader**

In `src/content/static.json`, rename the top-level key `"human_emergency_referral"` to `"human_emergency"`.

`src/server/content/static.ts`:

```ts
import raw from "@/content/static.json";
import type { Language } from "../types";

export type InfoTopic = "donate" | "volunteer" | "about" | "faq" | "human_emergency" | "helpline";
export type InfoContent = Record<string, string | { question: string; answer: string }[]>;

type Localized = Record<string, Record<string, string> | string>;
const content = raw as unknown as Record<string, unknown>;

const isFilled = (v: unknown): v is string => typeof v === "string" && v.trim().length > 0 && !v.trim().toUpperCase().startsWith("TODO");
const PHONE_RE = /^\+?\d[\d\s-]{6,}\d$/;

/** Real phones found in content, keyed by token name; filled lazily. */
const phoneRegistry: Record<string, string> = {};

export function getStaticPhones(): Record<string, string> {
  getStaticInfo("volunteer", "en");
  return { ...phoneRegistry };
}

function localizedObject(topic: string, lang: Language): Record<string, string> {
  const node = (content[topic] ?? {}) as Localized;
  const en = (typeof node.en === "object" ? node.en : {}) as Record<string, string>;
  const target = (typeof node[lang] === "object" ? node[lang] : {}) as Record<string, string>;
  const out: Record<string, string> = {};
  for (const key of new Set([...Object.keys(en), ...Object.keys(target)])) {
    const v = isFilled(target[key]) ? target[key] : isFilled(en[key]) ? en[key] : undefined;
    if (v === undefined) continue;
    if (key.includes("phone") && PHONE_RE.test(v)) {
      const tokenName = `${topic}`;
      phoneRegistry[tokenName] = v.replace(/[\s-]/g, "");
      out[key] = `{{PHONE:${tokenName}}}`;
    } else {
      out[key] = v;
    }
  }
  return out;
}

function localizedString(topic: string, lang: Language): string {
  const node = (content[topic] ?? {}) as Record<string, string>;
  return isFilled(node[lang]) ? node[lang] : isFilled(node.en) ? node.en : "";
}

export function getStaticInfo(topic: InfoTopic, lang: Language): InfoContent {
  switch (topic) {
    case "donate":
    case "volunteer":
      return localizedObject(topic, lang);
    case "about":
      return { text: localizedString("about", lang) };
    case "human_emergency":
      return { text: localizedString("human_emergency", lang) };
    case "faq": {
      const list = (content.faq ?? []) as Record<string, string>[];
      return {
        faq: list.map((e) => ({
          question: isFilled(e[`q_${lang}`]) ? e[`q_${lang}`] : e.q_en,
          answer: isFilled(e[`a_${lang}`]) ? e[`a_${lang}`] : e.a_en,
        })),
      };
    }
    case "helpline":
      return {
        phone: "{{PHONE:helpline}}",
        note: localizedString("about", lang),
      };
  }
}
```

Ensure `tsconfig.json` has `"resolveJsonModule": true` (Next.js default) so the JSON import compiles.

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach } from "vitest";
import { splitPhone, isTestPhone, sendText, sendInteractiveList, setFetchForTests } from "@/server/interakt/client";

const okFetch = (captured: { url?: string; init?: RequestInit }) =>
  (async (url: string | URL | Request, init?: RequestInit) => {
    captured.url = String(url);
    captured.init = init;
    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ result: true, id: "msg-1", message: "queued" }), { status: 201, headers: { "content-type": "application/json" } });
  }) as typeof fetch;

describe("splitPhone", () => {
  it("handles Indian and international shapes", () => {
    expect(splitPhone("+919096820338")).toEqual({ countryCode: "+91", phoneNumber: "9096820338" });
    expect(splitPhone("9096820338")).toEqual({ countryCode: "+91", phoneNumber: "9096820338" });
    expect(splitPhone("09096820338")).toEqual({ countryCode: "+91", phoneNumber: "9096820338" });
    expect(splitPhone("+447911123456")).toEqual({ countryCode: "+44", phoneNumber: "7911123456" });
  });
  it("recognises test phones", () => {
    expect(isTestPhone("+91TEST_abc123")).toBe(true);
    expect(isTestPhone("+919096820338")).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("send functions", () => {
  afterEach(() => setFetchForTests(null));

  it("posts a Text payload with Basic auth and returns the message id", async () => {
    const cap: { url?: string; init?: RequestInit } = {};
    setFetchForTests(okFetch(cap));
    const r = await sendText("+919096820338", "hello");
    expect(r.ok).toBe(true);

    expect(JSON.parse(String(cap.init!.body))).toEqual({
      countryCode: "+91", phoneNumber: "9096820338", type: "Text", callbackData: "agent", data: { message: "hello" },
    });
  });

    const cap: { url?: string; init?: RequestInit } = {};
    setFetchForTests(okFetch(cap));
    await sendInteractiveList("+919096820338", { body: "Pick one", buttonLabel: "Options", rows: [{ id: "menu:ambulance", title: "Animal ambulance" }] });
    const body = JSON.parse(String(cap.init!.body));
    expect(body.type).toBe("InteractiveList");
    expect(body.data.message.type).toBe("list");
    expect(body.data.message.action.button).toBe("Options");
    expect(body.data.message.action.sections[0].rows[0]).toEqual({ id: "menu:ambulance", title: "Animal ambulance" });
  });

  it("refuses to send to a test phone without calling fetch", async () => {
    let called = false;

    const r = await sendText("+91TEST_abc", "hi");
    expect(r.ok).toBe(false);
    expect(r.error).toMatch(/test phone/);

    const r = await sendText("+919096820338", "hello");
    expect(r.ok).toBe(false);

    const r2 = await sendText("+919096820338", "hello");
    expect(r2.ok).toBe(false);
    expect(r2.error).toMatch(/ECONNRESET/);
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Rewrite `src/server/interakt/client.ts`**

```ts
import { getConfig } from "../config";
import { log, maskPhone } from "../log";

export interface InteraktResult {
  ok: boolean;
  status: number;
  body: unknown;
  error?: string;
  messageId?: string;
}

export interface InteractiveListSpec {
  body: string;
  buttonLabel: string;
  sectionTitle?: string;
  rows: { id: string; title: string; description?: string }[];
}

let fetchImpl: typeof fetch | null = null;
export function setFetchForTests(fn: typeof fetch | null): void {
  fetchImpl = fn;
}

export function isTestPhone(phone: string): boolean {
  return phone.startsWith("+91TEST_");
}

/** Length-based split; a greedy regex once produced "+919" + 9 digits and silently dropped messages. */
export function splitPhone(phone: string): { countryCode: string; phoneNumber: string } {
  const digits = phone.replace(/\D/g, "");
  if (digits.length === 10) return { countryCode: "+91", phoneNumber: digits };
  if (digits.length === 12 && digits.startsWith("91")) return { countryCode: "+91", phoneNumber: digits.slice(2) };
  if (digits.length === 11 && digits.startsWith("0")) return { countryCode: "+91", phoneNumber: digits.slice(1) };
  if (digits.length > 10) return { countryCode: `+${digits.slice(0, -10)}`, phoneNumber: digits.slice(-10) };
  return { countryCode: "+91", phoneNumber: digits };
}

async function post(payload: Record<string, unknown>, to: string): Promise<InteraktResult> {
  if (isTestPhone(to)) {
    return { ok: false, status: 0, body: null, error: "refusing to send to a test phone" };
  }
  const cfg = getConfig();
  const doFetch = fetchImpl ?? fetch;
  try {
    const res = await doFetch(cfg.interakt.apiBase, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { Authorization: `Basic ${cfg.interakt.apiKey}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify(payload),
    });
    const body = await res.json().catch(() => null);
    const messageId = body && typeof body === "object" && typeof (body as { id?: unknown }).id === "string" ? (body as { id: string }).id : undefined;
    const result: InteraktResult = { ok: res.ok, status: res.status, body, messageId };
    if (!res.ok) {
      result.error = `interakt ${res.status}: ${JSON.stringify(body).slice(0, 300)}`;
      log.warn({ to: maskPhone(to), status: res.status }, "interakt send failed");
    }
    return result;
  } catch (e) {
    const error = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);
    log.error({ to: maskPhone(to), error }, "interakt send threw");
    return { ok: false, status: 0, body: null, error };
  }
}

/** Free-form text; valid only inside the 24 h customer-service window. */
export async function sendText(to: string, body: string): Promise<InteraktResult> {
  const { countryCode, phoneNumber } = splitPhone(to);
  return post({ countryCode, phoneNumber, type: "Text", callbackData: "agent", data: { message: body } }, to);
}

/** WhatsApp list message (≤10 rows, titles ≤24 chars, button ≤20 chars). */
export async function sendInteractiveList(to: string, list: InteractiveListSpec): Promise<InteraktResult> {
  const { countryCode, phoneNumber } = splitPhone(to);
  return post(
    {
      countryCode,
      phoneNumber,
      type: "InteractiveList",
      callbackData: "agent",
      data: {
        message: {
          type: "list",
          body: { text: list.body },
          action: {
            button: list.buttonLabel,
            sections: [{ title: list.sectionTitle ?? "Options", rows: list.rows }],
          },
        },
      },
    },
    to
  );
}
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import { createHmac } from "node:crypto";
import { parseInteraktPayload, verifyInteraktSignature } from "@/server/interakt/webhook";

const base = (message: Record<string, unknown>, customer: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => ({
  version: "1.0",
  timestamp: "2026-09-25T10:00:00Z",
  type: "message_received",
  data: {
    customer: { id: "c1", channel_phone_number: "917003705584", traits: { name: "Asha" }, ...customer },
    message: { id: "m-1", chat_message_type: "CustomerMessage", received_at_utc: "2026-09-25T10:00:00Z", ...message },
  },
});

describe("parseInteraktPayload", () => {
  it("parses text", () => {
    const r = parseInteraktPayload(base({ message_content_type: "Text", message: "dog injured" }));
    expect(r.kind).toBe("inbound");
    if (r.kind !== "inbound") return;
    expect(r.message).toMatchObject({ providerMessageId: "m-1", fromPhone: "+917003705584", fromName: "Asha", kind: "text", content: "dog injured", mediaUrl: null });
  });
  it("parses image with caption and media url", () => {
    const r = parseInteraktPayload(base({ message_content_type: "Image", message: "see this", media_url: "https://x/y.jpg" }));
    expect(r.kind === "inbound" && r.message).toMatchObject({ kind: "image", content: "see this", mediaUrl: "https://x/y.jpg" });
  });
  it("parses audio as kind audio with empty content", () => {
    const r = parseInteraktPayload(base({ message_content_type: "Audio", message: "", media_url: "https://x/a.ogg" }));
    expect(r.kind === "inbound" && r.message).toMatchObject({ kind: "audio", content: "", mediaUrl: "https://x/a.ogg" });
  });
  it("parses a location JSON string", () => {
    const r = parseInteraktPayload(base({ message_content_type: "Location", message: JSON.stringify({ latitude: 18.5074, longitude: 73.8077, name: "Karve Nagar" }) }));
    expect(r.kind === "inbound" && r.message).toMatchObject({ kind: "location", lat: 18.5074, lng: 73.8077, placeHint: "Karve Nagar" });
  });
  it("parses list and button replies", () => {
    const list = parseInteraktPayload(base({ message_content_type: "InteractiveListReply", message: JSON.stringify({ type: "list_reply", list_reply: { id: "menu:ambulance", title: "Animal ambulance" } }) }));
    expect(list.kind === "inbound" && list.message).toMatchObject({ kind: "interactive", interactiveId: "menu:ambulance", content: "Animal ambulance" });
    const btn = parseInteraktPayload(base({ message_content_type: "InteractiveButtonReply", message: JSON.stringify({ type: "button_reply", button_reply: { id: "yes", title: "Yes" } }) }));
    expect(btn.kind === "inbound" && btn.message).toMatchObject({ kind: "interactive", interactiveId: "yes", content: "Yes" });
  });
  it("ignores our own outbound echoes", () => {
    const r = parseInteraktPayload(base({ message_content_type: "Text", message: "hi", chat_message_type: "PublicApiMessage" }));
    expect(r).toEqual({ kind: "ignored", reason: "not_customer_message", eventType: "message_received" });
  });
  it("ignores status events and the webhook test ping", () => {
    expect(parseInteraktPayload({ type: "message_api_delivered", data: {} }).kind).toBe("ignored");
    expect(parseInteraktPayload({ type: "Webhook Test", data: { customer: {}, message: {} } }).kind).toBe("ignored");
    expect(parseInteraktPayload(null).kind).toBe("ignored");
  });
  it("keeps unknown content types as text and records the raw type", () => {
    const r = parseInteraktPayload(base({ message_content_type: "Contacts", message: "" }));
    expect(r.kind === "inbound" && r.message).toMatchObject({ kind: "contact", rawContentType: "Contacts" });
  });
});

describe("verifyInteraktSignature", () => {
  const secret = "s3cret";
  const body = '{"a":1}';
  const sig = "sha256=" + createHmac("sha256", secret).update(body).digest("hex");
  it("accepts a valid signature and rejects a wrong or missing one", async () => {
    expect(await verifyInteraktSignature(body, sig, { secret, mode: "hmac" })).toBe(true);
    expect(await verifyInteraktSignature(body, "sha256=deadbeef", { secret, mode: "hmac" })).toBe(false);
    expect(await verifyInteraktSignature(body, null, { secret, mode: "hmac" })).toBe(false);
  });
  it("accepts anything when mode is off", async () => {
    expect(await verifyInteraktSignature(body, null, { secret, mode: "off" })).toBe(true);
  });
  it("rejects when hmac mode has no secret", async () => {
    expect(await verifyInteraktSignature(body, sig, { secret: "", mode: "hmac" })).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Rewrite `src/server/interakt/webhook.ts`**

```ts
import { createHmac, timingSafeEqual } from "node:crypto";
import type { MessageKind } from "../types";

export interface ParsedInbound {
  providerMessageId: string;
  fromPhone: string;
  fromName: string | null;
  kind: MessageKind;
  content: string;
  mediaUrl: string | null;
  lat: number | null;
  lng: number | null;
  placeHint: string | null;
  interactiveId: string | null;
  receivedAt: Date;
  rawContentType: string;
}

export type ParseOutcome =
  | { kind: "inbound"; message: ParsedInbound }
  | { kind: "ignored"; reason: string; eventType: string };

interface InteraktPayload {
  type?: string;
  timestamp?: string;
  data?: {
    customer?: { channel_phone_number?: string; phone_number?: string; country_code?: string; traits?: Record<string, unknown> };
    message?: Record<string, unknown>;
  };
}

const INBOUND_TYPES = [/^message[_\s]received$/i, /^messages[_\s]received$/i, /^message[_\s]received[_\s]from[_\s]customers?$/i];

function toE164(raw: string | undefined, cc?: string): string | null {
  if (!raw) return null;
  const cleaned = raw.replace(/[^\d+]/g, "");
  if (cleaned.startsWith("+")) return cleaned;
  if (cleaned.length >= 11 && cleaned.length <= 15) return `+${cleaned}`;
  if (cleaned.length === 10) {
    const prefix = (cc ?? "+91").replace(/[^\d+]/g, "");
    return `${prefix.startsWith("+") ? prefix : `+${prefix || "91"}`}${cleaned}`;
  }
  return cleaned ? `+${cleaned}` : null;
}

function mapKind(contentType: string): MessageKind {
  switch (contentType.toLowerCase()) {
    case "text": return "text";
    case "image": return "image";
    case "video": return "video";
    case "audio": case "voice": return "audio";
    case "document": case "file": return "document";
    case "location": return "location";
    case "sticker": return "sticker";
    case "contact": case "contacts": return "contact";
    case "interactivebuttonreply": case "interactivelistreply": return "interactive";
    default: return "text";
  }
}

function parseJsonString(v: unknown): Record<string, unknown> | null {
  if (typeof v !== "string") return null;
  try {
    const o = JSON.parse(v);
    return o && typeof o === "object" ? (o as Record<string, unknown>) : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export function parseInteraktPayload(raw: unknown): ParseOutcome {
  if (typeof raw !== "object" || raw === null) return { kind: "ignored", reason: "non_object", eventType: "(none)" };
  const p = raw as InteraktPayload;
  const eventType = p.type ?? "(none)";
  if (!INBOUND_TYPES.some((re) => re.test(eventType.trim()))) return { kind: "ignored", reason: "event_type", eventType };

  const customer = p.data?.customer;
  const message = p.data?.message;
  if (!customer || !message || typeof message.id !== "string") return { kind: "ignored", reason: "missing_fields", eventType };
  if (typeof message.chat_message_type === "string" && message.chat_message_type !== "CustomerMessage") {
    return { kind: "ignored", reason: "not_customer_message", eventType };
  }
  const fromPhone = toE164(customer.channel_phone_number ?? customer.phone_number, customer.country_code);
  if (!fromPhone) return { kind: "ignored", reason: "no_phone", eventType };

  const traits = customer.traits ?? {};
  const fromName = [traits.whatsappName, traits.firstName, traits.name].find((v): v is string => typeof v === "string" && v.length > 0) ?? null;

  const rawContentType = typeof message.message_content_type === "string" ? message.message_content_type : "Text";
  const kind = mapKind(rawContentType);
  const body = typeof message.message === "string" ? message.message : "";
  const mediaUrl = typeof message.media_url === "string" && message.media_url.length > 0 ? message.media_url : null;

  let content = "";
  let lat: number | null = null;
  let lng: number | null = null;
  let placeHint: string | null = null;
  let interactiveId: string | null = null;

  if (kind === "location") {
    const loc = parseJsonString(body) ?? (message.location as Record<string, unknown> | undefined) ?? {};
    lat = typeof loc.latitude === "number" ? loc.latitude : Number(loc.latitude) || null;
    lng = typeof loc.longitude === "number" ? loc.longitude : Number(loc.longitude) || null;
    const parts = [loc.name, loc.address].filter((v): v is string => typeof v === "string" && v.length > 0);
    placeHint = parts.length ? parts.join(", ") : null;
    content = placeHint ?? "";
  } else if (kind === "interactive") {
    const obj = parseJsonString(body) ?? {};
    const reply = (obj.list_reply ?? obj.button_reply ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
    interactiveId = typeof reply.id === "string" ? reply.id : null;
    content = typeof reply.title === "string" ? reply.title : body;
  } else {
    content = body;
  }

  const tsStr = (message.received_at_utc as string | undefined) ?? p.timestamp;
  const parsedDate = tsStr ? new Date(tsStr) : new Date();

  return {
    kind: "inbound",
    message: {
      providerMessageId: message.id,
      fromPhone,
      fromName,
      kind,
      content,
      mediaUrl: kind === "text" ? null : mediaUrl,
      lat,
      lng,
      placeHint,
      interactiveId,
      receivedAt: isNaN(parsedDate.getTime()) ? new Date() : parsedDate,
      rawContentType,
    },
  };
}

/** `Interakt-Signature: sha256=<hex>` = HMAC-SHA256(secret, raw body bytes). */
export async function verifyInteraktSignature(
  rawBody: string,
  signatureHeader: string | null,
  opts: { secret: string; mode: "hmac" | "off" }
): Promise<boolean> {
  if (opts.mode === "off") return true;
  if (!opts.secret) return false;
  if (!signatureHeader) return false;
  const expected = "sha256=" + createHmac("sha256", opts.secret).update(rawBody, "utf8").digest("hex");
  const a = Buffer.from(signatureHeader);
  const b = Buffer.from(expected);
  return a.length === b.length && timingSafeEqual(a, b);
}
```

```ts
import { eq, inArray, and } from "drizzle-orm";
import { getDb } from "../db/client";
import { ambulances, clinics, operators } from "../db/schema";
import type { DirectoryAmbulance } from "./matcher";

export interface DirectoryClinic {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  city: string;
  area: string | null;
  state: string;
  phone: string;
  address: string | null;
  hours: string | null;
}

function toDirectoryAmbulance(r: {
  id: string; label: string; city: string; area: string | null; state: string; areasCovered: string[];
  category: string; phone: string; operatorName: string | null; operatorIsArham: boolean | null;
}): DirectoryAmbulance {
  return {
    id: r.id, label: r.label, city: r.city, area: r.area, state: r.state, areasCovered: r.areasCovered ?? [],
    category: r.category, phone: r.phone,
    operatorName: r.operatorName ?? "Arham Yuva Seva Group", operatorIsArham: r.operatorIsArham ?? true,
  };
}

const ambulanceSelect = {
  id: ambulances.id, label: ambulances.label, city: ambulances.city, area: ambulances.area, state: ambulances.state,
  areasCovered: ambulances.areasCovered, category: ambulances.category, phone: ambulances.phone,
  operatorName: operators.name, operatorIsArham: operators.isArham,
};

export async function loadActiveAmbulances(): Promise<DirectoryAmbulance[]> {
  const rows = await getDb()
    .select(ambulanceSelect)
    .from(ambulances)
    .leftJoin(operators, eq(ambulances.operatorId, operators.id))
    .where(eq(ambulances.active, true));
  return rows.map(toDirectoryAmbulance);
}

export async function getAmbulancesByIds(ids: string[]): Promise<Map<string, DirectoryAmbulance>> {
  if (ids.length === 0) return new Map();
  const rows = await getDb()
    .select(ambulanceSelect)
    .from(ambulances)
    .leftJoin(operators, eq(ambulances.operatorId, operators.id))
    .where(inArray(ambulances.id, ids));
  return new Map(rows.map((r) => [r.id, toDirectoryAmbulance(r)]));
}

const clinicSelect = {
  id: clinics.id, label: clinics.label, city: clinics.city, area: clinics.area, state: clinics.state,
  phone: clinics.phone, address: clinics.address, hours: clinics.hours,
};

export async function loadActiveClinics(): Promise<DirectoryClinic[]> {
  return getDb().select(clinicSelect).from(clinics).where(eq(clinics.active, true));
}

export async function getClinicsByIds(ids: string[]): Promise<Map<string, DirectoryClinic>> {
  if (ids.length === 0) return new Map();
  const rows = await getDb().select(clinicSelect).from(clinics).where(and(inArray(clinics.id, ids)));
  return new Map(rows.map((r) => [r.id, r]));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/server/directory/coverage.ts`**

```ts
import type { DirectoryAmbulance } from "./matcher";
import { loadActiveAmbulances, loadActiveClinics, type DirectoryClinic } from "./repo";

/** Plain-text coverage list for the system prompt. Contains no phone numbers. */
export function buildCoverageSummary(ambs: DirectoryAmbulance[], clinicRows: DirectoryClinic[]): string {
  const byCity = new Map<string, DirectoryAmbulance[]>();
  for (const a of ambs) byCity.set(a.city, [...(byCity.get(a.city) ?? []), a]);

  const lines = ["Ambulance coverage (city: areas with a unit; also covers = nearby localities served):"];
  for (const city of [...byCity.keys()].sort()) {
    const units = byCity.get(city)!;
    const areas = [...new Set(units.map((u) => u.area).filter((a): a is string => !!a))].sort();
    const covered = [...new Set(units.flatMap((u) => u.areasCovered))]
      .filter((c) => c.toLowerCase() !== city.toLowerCase() && !areas.some((a) => a.toLowerCase() === c.toLowerCase()))
      .sort();
    const partnerOnly = units.every((u) => !u.operatorIsArham);
    let line: string;
    if (areas.length === 0) {
      line = `- ${city}: whole city (${units.length} unit${units.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}${partnerOnly ? ", partner-operated" : ""})`;
      if (covered.length) line += ` (also covers ${covered.join(", ")})`;
    } else {
      line = `- ${city}: ${areas.join(", ")}`;
      if (covered.length) line += ` (also covers ${covered.join(", ")})`;
    }
    lines.push(line);
  }
  const clinicCities = [...new Set(clinicRows.map((c) => (c.area ? `${c.city} (${c.area})` : c.city)))].sort();
  lines.push(clinicCities.length ? `Clinics: ${clinicCities.join(", ")}` : "Clinics: none listed");
  return lines.join("\n");
}

let cache: { text: string; at: number } | null = null;
const TTL_MS = 60 * 60 * 1000;

export async function getCoverageSummary(now: number = Date.now()): Promise<string> {
  if (cache && now - cache.at < TTL_MS) return cache.text;
  const [ambs, cls] = await Promise.all([loadActiveAmbulances(), loadActiveClinics()]);
  cache = { text: buildCoverageSummary(ambs, cls), at: now };
  return cache.text;
}

export function resetCoverageCache(): void {
  cache = null;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `src/server/directory/ingest.ts`**

```ts
import { parse } from "csv-parse/sync";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { getDb } from "../db/client";
import { ambulances, clinics, operators } from "../db/schema";

export interface CsvRow {
  Type: string; Name: string; City: string; Area: string; State: string; Phone: string;
  "Area of Operation": string; Category: string; "Operated By": string;
}

export const ARHAM_NAME = "Arham Yuva Seva Group";

export function normalizePhone(raw: string): string {
  const digits = (raw ?? "").replace(/\D/g, "");
  if (digits.length === 10) return `+91${digits}`;
  if (digits.length === 12 && digits.startsWith("91")) return `+${digits}`;
  if (digits.length === 11 && digits.startsWith("0")) return `+91${digits.slice(1)}`;
  return `+${digits}`;
}

export function parseAreasCovered(value: string, city: string, area: string): string[] {
  const v = (value ?? "").trim();
  if (!v) return area ? [area] : [];
  if (/^entire city$/i.test(v)) return [city];
  return v.split(",").map((s) => s.trim()).filter((s) => s.length > 0);
}

/** "ALAI Trust" in the Area column is the operator, not a place. */
export function isPartnerNameLeak(area: string, operator: string): boolean {
  if (!area || !operator || operator === ARHAM_NAME) return false;
  const a = area.trim().toLowerCase();
  const o = operator.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (a.length < 4 || o.length < 4) return false;
  return a === o || a.includes(o) || o.includes(a);
}

export function parseDirectoryCsv(text: string): { ambulances: CsvRow[]; clinics: CsvRow[] } {
  const records = parse(text, { columns: true, skip_empty_lines: true, trim: true }) as CsvRow[];
  return {
    ambulances: records.filter((r) => r.Type === "Ambulance"),
    clinics: records.filter((r) => r.Type === "Clinic"),
  };
}

export async function ingestDirectory(rows: { ambulances: CsvRow[]; clinics: CsvRow[] }) {
  const db = getDb();
  const names = new Set([...rows.ambulances, ...rows.clinics].map((r) => r["Operated By"].trim()).filter(Boolean));
  const operatorIdByName = new Map<string, string>();
  for (const name of names) {
    const [existing] = await db.select({ id: operators.id }).from(operators).where(eq(operators.name, name));
    if (existing) { operatorIdByName.set(name, existing.id); continue; }
    const [inserted] = await db.insert(operators).values({ name, isArham: name === ARHAM_NAME }).returning({ id: operators.id });
    operatorIdByName.set(name, inserted.id);
  }

  let ambulancesInserted = 0, ambulancesUpdated = 0, clinicsInserted = 0, clinicsUpdated = 0;

  for (const r of rows.ambulances) {
    const operatorName = r["Operated By"].trim();
    const operatorId = operatorIdByName.get(operatorName);
    if (!operatorId) throw new Error(`Missing operator for ${r.Name}`);
    const rawArea = (r.Area ?? "").trim();
    const leak = isPartnerNameLeak(rawArea, operatorName);
    const payload = {
      operatorId, label: r.Name.trim(), city: r.City.trim(), area: leak ? null : rawArea || null, state: r.State.trim(),
      phone: normalizePhone(r.Phone), phoneRaw: (r.Phone ?? "").trim(),
      areasCovered: leak ? [r.City.trim()] : parseAreasCovered(r["Area of Operation"], r.City.trim(), rawArea),
      category: (r.Category ?? "").trim() || "Animal Ambulance", active: true, updatedAt: new Date(),
    };
    const [existing] = await db.select({ id: ambulances.id }).from(ambulances).where(eq(ambulances.label, payload.label));
    if (existing) { await db.update(ambulances).set(payload).where(eq(ambulances.id, existing.id)); ambulancesUpdated++; }
    else { await db.insert(ambulances).values(payload); ambulancesInserted++; }
  }

  for (const r of rows.clinics) {
    const operatorId = operatorIdByName.get(r["Operated By"].trim());
    if (!operatorId) throw new Error(`Missing operator for ${r.Name}`);
    const payload = {
      operatorId, label: r.Name.trim(), city: r.City.replace(/,.*$/, "").trim(), area: (r.Area ?? "").trim() || null,
      state: r.State.trim(), phone: normalizePhone(r.Phone), phoneRaw: (r.Phone ?? "").trim(), active: true,
    };
    const [existing] = await db.select({ id: clinics.id }).from(clinics).where(eq(clinics.label, payload.label));
    if (existing) { await db.update(clinics).set(payload).where(eq(clinics.id, existing.id)); clinicsUpdated++; }
    else { await db.insert(clinics).values(payload); clinicsInserted++; }
  }

  return { operators: names.size, ambulancesInserted, ambulancesUpdated, clinicsInserted, clinicsUpdated };
}
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach } from "vitest";
import { resetDb, createConversation } from "../helpers/db";
import { getBoss, stopBoss, enqueueTurn, TURN_QUEUE, type TurnJobData } from "@/server/queue/boss";

describe("queue", () => {
  beforeEach(async () => { await stopBoss(); await resetDb(); });
  afterEach(async () => { await stopBoss(); });

  it("creates the turn queue with singleton policy", async () => {
    const boss = await getBoss("worker");
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/server/queue/boss.ts`**

```ts
import PgBoss from "pg-boss";
import { getConfig } from "../config";
import { log } from "../log";

export const TURN_QUEUE = "turn";

export interface TurnJobData {
  conversationId: string;
}

let instance: PgBoss | null = null;
let starting: Promise<PgBoss> | null = null;

/**
 * One pg-boss per process. The app only sends; the worker also supervises
 * (maintenance, expiration). Both create the queue idempotently.
 */
export function getBoss(role: "app" | "worker" = "app"): Promise<PgBoss> {
  if (instance) return Promise.resolve(instance);
  if (starting) return starting;
  starting = (async () => {
    const boss = new PgBoss({
      connectionString: getConfig().databaseUrl,
      schema: "pgboss",
      supervise: role === "worker",
      schedule: false,
      max: role === "worker" ? 4 : 2,
    });
    boss.on("error", (err) => log.error({ err }, "pg-boss error"));
    await boss.start();
    const existing = await boss.getQueue(TURN_QUEUE);
    if (!existing) {
      await boss.createQueue(TURN_QUEUE, {
        policy: "singleton",
        retryLimit: 2,
        retryBackoff: true,
        retryDelay: 5,
        expireInSeconds: 120,
      });
    }
    instance = boss;
    starting = null;
    return boss;
  })();
  return starting;
}

export async function stopBoss(): Promise<void> {
  if (starting) await starting.catch(() => undefined);
  if (instance) {
    await instance.stop({ graceful: true, timeout: 5000 });
    instance = null;
  }
}

/**
 * Debounced per conversation: a burst of messages produces one turn that
 * starts 2 s after the first. Returns null when pg-boss deduplicated it.
 */
export async function enqueueTurn(conversationId: string, opts: { immediate?: boolean } = {}): Promise<string | null> {
  const boss = await getBoss();
  return boss.send(
    TURN_QUEUE,
    { conversationId } satisfies TurnJobData,
    {
      singletonKey: conversationId,
      singletonSeconds: 3,
      startAfter: opts.immediate ? 0 : 2,
    }
  );
}
```
