```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from "vitest";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { resetDb } from "../helpers/db";
import { getDb } from "@/server/db/client";
import { conversations, messages } from "@/server/db/schema";
import { upsertConversationByPhone, storeReporterMessage } from "@/server/inbound/store";

describe("inbound store", () => {
  beforeEach(resetDb);

  it("upserts by phone, keeps the first name when a later one is missing", async () => {
    const a = await upsertConversationByPhone({ phone: "+919876543210", name: "Asha" });
    const b = await upsertConversationByPhone({ phone: "+919876543210", name: null });
    expect(b.id).toBe(a.id);
    expect(b.name).toBe("Asha");
    expect(b.lastInboundAt).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it("stores a reporter message once per provider id", async () => {
    const c = await upsertConversationByPhone({ phone: "+919876543210", name: null });
    const first = await storeReporterMessage(c.id, { providerMessageId: "m1", kind: "text", content: "hi" });
    const dup = await storeReporterMessage(c.id, { providerMessageId: "m1", kind: "text", content: "hi" });
    expect(first.inserted).toBe(true);
    expect(dup).toEqual({ inserted: false, id: null });
    const rows = await getDb().select().from(messages).where(eq(messages.conversationId, c.id));
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(rows[0].origin).toBe("reporter");
    expect(rows[0].answeredTurnId).toBeNull();
  });

  it("does not require a provider id", async () => {
    const c = await upsertConversationByPhone({ phone: "+91TEST_x1", name: null });
    await getDb().update(conversations).set({ isTest: true }).where(eq(conversations.id, c.id));
    const r1 = await storeReporterMessage(c.id, { kind: "text", content: "a" });
    const r2 = await storeReporterMessage(c.id, { kind: "text", content: "b" });
    expect(r1.inserted && r2.inserted).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach } from "vitest";
import { createHmac } from "node:crypto";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { resetDb, createConversation } from "../helpers/db";
import { getDb } from "@/server/db/client";
import { conversations, messages } from "@/server/db/schema";
import { getBoss, stopBoss, TURN_QUEUE } from "@/server/queue/boss";
import { POST, GET } from "@/app/api/webhook/route";

const secret = process.env.INTERAKT_WEBHOOK_SECRET!;

function signed(body: unknown, opts: { sign?: boolean } = { sign: true }) {
  const raw = JSON.stringify(body);
  const headers: Record<string, string> = { "content-type": "application/json" };
  if (opts.sign) headers["interakt-signature"] = "sha256=" + createHmac("sha256", secret).update(raw).digest("hex");
  return new Request("http://localhost/api/webhook", { method: "POST", headers, body: raw });
}

const inbound = (id: string, text: string, phone = "917003705584") => ({
  version: "1.0", timestamp: "2026-09-25T10:00:00Z", type: "message_received",
  data: {
    customer: { id: "c1", channel_phone_number: phone, traits: { name: "Asha" } },
    message: { id, chat_message_type: "CustomerMessage", message_content_type: "Text", message: text, received_at_utc: "2026-09-25T10:00:00Z" },
  },
});

describe("POST /api/webhook", () => {
  beforeEach(async () => { await stopBoss(); await resetDb(); });
  afterEach(async () => { await stopBoss(); });

  it("GET answers ok", async () => {
    expect(await (await GET()).text()).toBe("ok");
  });

  it("rejects a bad signature", async () => {
    const res = await POST(signed(inbound("m1", "hi"), { sign: false }));
    expect(res.status).toBe(403);
  });

  it("stores the message, upserts the conversation and enqueues a turn", async () => {
    const res = await POST(signed(inbound("m1", "dog injured in ghatkopar")));
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    const json = await res.json();
    expect(json.status).toBe("queued");
    const [c] = await getDb().select().from(conversations).where(eq(conversations.phone, "+917003705584"));
    expect(c.name).toBe("Asha");
    const rows = await getDb().select().from(messages).where(eq(messages.conversationId, c.id));
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(1);
    const q = await (await getBoss()).getQueue(TURN_QUEUE);
    expect((q?.queuedCount ?? 0) + (q?.activeCount ?? 0) + (q?.deferredCount ?? 0)).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
  });

  it("answers duplicate for a resent message id", async () => {
    await POST(signed(inbound("m1", "hi")));
    const res = await POST(signed(inbound("m1", "hi")));
    expect((await res.json()).status).toBe("duplicate");
    const rows = await getDb().select().from(messages);
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it("ignores status events and echoes", async () => {
    const res = await POST(signed({ type: "message_api_delivered", data: {} }));
    expect((await res.json()).status).toBe("ignored");
  });

  it("refuses phones that belong to a test conversation", async () => {
    await createConversation({ phone: "+917003705584", isTest: true });
    const res = await POST(signed(inbound("m9", "hi")));
    expect((await res.json()).status).toBe("ignored_test_conv");
  });

  it("rejects oversized bodies", async () => {
    const big = { ...inbound("m2", "x".repeat(300_000)) };
    const res = await POST(signed(big));
    expect(res.status).toBe(413);
  });

  it("answers bad_json for malformed bodies", async () => {
    const raw = "{not json";
    const req = new Request("http://localhost/api/webhook", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "content-type": "application/json", "interakt-signature": "sha256=" + createHmac("sha256", secret).update(raw).digest("hex") },
      body: raw,
    });
    expect((await POST(req)).status).toBe(400);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { getDb } from "../db/client";
import { conversations, events, messages, type Conversation } from "../db/schema";
import type { MessageKind } from "../types";

export async function upsertConversationByPhone(input: { phone: string; name: string | null }): Promise<Conversation> {
  const now = new Date();
  const [row] = await getDb()
    .insert(conversations)
    .values({ phone: input.phone, name: input.name, lastInboundAt: now, updatedAt: now })
    .onConflictDoUpdate({
      target: conversations.phone,
      set: {
        name: sql`coalesce(${conversations.name}, excluded.name)`,
        lastInboundAt: now,
        updatedAt: now,
      },
    })
    .returning();
  return row;
}

export interface ReporterMessageInput {
  providerMessageId?: string | null;
  kind: MessageKind;
  content: string;
  mediaUrl?: string | null;
  lat?: number | null;
  lng?: number | null;
  placeLabel?: string | null;
  interactiveId?: string | null;
}

/** Inserts a reporter message; `inserted: false` when the provider id already exists. */
export async function storeReporterMessage(
  conversationId: string,
  m: ReporterMessageInput
): Promise<{ inserted: boolean; id: string | null }> {
  const rows = await getDb()
    .insert(messages)
    .values({
      conversationId,
      role: "user",
      origin: "reporter",
      kind: m.kind,
      content: m.content ?? "",
      mediaUrl: m.mediaUrl ?? null,
      lat: m.lat ?? null,
      lng: m.lng ?? null,
      placeLabel: m.placeLabel ?? null,
      providerMessageId: m.providerMessageId ?? null,
      interactiveId: m.interactiveId ?? null,
    })
    .onConflictDoNothing({ target: messages.providerMessageId })
    .returning({ id: messages.id });
  if (rows.length === 0) return { inserted: false, id: null };
  return { inserted: true, id: rows[0].id };
}

export async function recordEvent(kind: string, payload: unknown, conversationId: string | null = null): Promise<void> {
  await getDb().insert(events).values({ kind, payload: payload as object, conversationId });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Rewrite `src/app/api/webhook/route.ts`**

```ts
import { getConfig } from "@/server/config";
import { log, maskPhone } from "@/server/log";
import { parseInteraktPayload, verifyInteraktSignature } from "@/server/interakt/webhook";
import { recordEvent, storeReporterMessage, upsertConversationByPhone } from "@/server/inbound/store";
import { enqueueTurn } from "@/server/queue/boss";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

const MAX_BODY_BYTES = 256 * 1024;
const seenUnknownTypes = new Set<string>();

export async function GET() {
  return new Response("ok", { status: 200 });
}

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  const cfg = getConfig();
  const rawBody = await request.text();
  if (Buffer.byteLength(rawBody, "utf8") > MAX_BODY_BYTES) {
    return Response.json({ status: "too_large" }, { status: 413 });
  }
  if (cfg.logRawWebhooks) log.debug({ body: rawBody.slice(0, 4096) }, "raw webhook");

  const sigHeader = request.headers.get("interakt-signature") ?? request.headers.get("x-interakt-signature");
  const verified = await verifyInteraktSignature(rawBody, sigHeader, { secret: cfg.interakt.webhookSecret, mode: cfg.interakt.verifyMode });
  if (!verified) {
    log.warn("webhook signature verification failed");
    return Response.json({ status: "forbidden" }, { status: 403 });
  }

  let payload: unknown;
  try {
    payload = JSON.parse(rawBody);
  } catch {
    return Response.json({ status: "bad_json" }, { status: 400 });
  }

  const parsed = parseInteraktPayload(payload);
  if (parsed.kind === "ignored") {
    log.info({ eventType: parsed.eventType, reason: parsed.reason }, "webhook ignored");
    return Response.json({ status: "ignored", reason: parsed.reason, event_type: parsed.eventType });
  }
  const m = parsed.message;

  const known = ["Text", "Image", "Video", "Audio", "Document", "Location", "Sticker", "InteractiveButtonReply", "InteractiveListReply"];
  if (!known.includes(m.rawContentType) && !seenUnknownTypes.has(m.rawContentType)) {
    seenUnknownTypes.add(m.rawContentType);
    await recordEvent("unknown_payload", { contentType: m.rawContentType, keys: Object.keys((payload as { data?: { message?: object } }).data?.message ?? {}) });
  }

  const conversation = await upsertConversationByPhone({ phone: m.fromPhone, name: m.fromName });
  if (conversation.isTest) {
    log.error({ phone: maskPhone(m.fromPhone) }, "webhook phone collides with a test conversation");
    return Response.json({ status: "ignored_test_conv" });
  }

  const stored = await storeReporterMessage(conversation.id, {
    providerMessageId: m.providerMessageId,
    kind: m.kind,
    content: m.content,
    mediaUrl: m.mediaUrl,
    lat: m.lat,
    lng: m.lng,
    placeLabel: m.placeHint,
    interactiveId: m.interactiveId,
  });
  if (!stored.inserted) return Response.json({ status: "duplicate" });

  await enqueueTurn(conversation.id);
  log.info({ conversationId: conversation.id, kind: m.kind, phone: maskPhone(m.fromPhone) }, "inbound queued");
  return Response.json({ status: "queued", conversationId: conversation.id, messageId: stored.id });
}
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach } from "vitest";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { resetDb, createConversation } from "../helpers/db";
import { getDb } from "@/server/db/client";

import { storeReporterMessage } from "@/server/inbound/store";
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/server/worker/gather.ts`**

```ts
import { and, asc, desc, eq, isNull } from "drizzle-orm";
import { getDb } from "../db/client";
import { conversations, messages, type Conversation, type Message } from "../db/schema";

export async function gatherPending(conversationId: string): Promise<{ conversation: Conversation; pending: Message[] } | null> {
  const db = getDb();
  const [conversation] = await db.select().from(conversations).where(eq(conversations.id, conversationId));
  if (!conversation) return null;
  const pending = await db
    .select()
    .from(messages)
    .where(and(eq(messages.conversationId, conversationId), eq(messages.role, "user"), isNull(messages.answeredTurnId)))
    .orderBy(asc(messages.createdAt));
  return { conversation, pending };
}

/** Last `limit` messages, oldest first. */
export async function loadHistory(conversationId: string, limit = 30): Promise<Message[]> {
  const rows = await getDb()
    .select()
    .from(messages)
    .where(eq(messages.conversationId, conversationId))
    .orderBy(desc(messages.createdAt))
    .limit(limit);
  return rows.reverse();
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/server/worker/persist.ts`**

```ts
import { desc, eq, inArray } from "drizzle-orm";
import { getDb } from "../db/client";
import { conversations, messages, turns, type Conversation, type NewTurn, type Turn } from "../db/schema";
import type { TurnStatus } from "../types";

export async function createTurn(input: { conversationId: string; status: TurnStatus; model?: string | null; effort?: string | null }): Promise<Turn> {
  const [row] = await getDb()
    .insert(turns)
    .values({ conversationId: input.conversationId, status: input.status, model: input.model ?? null, effort: input.effort ?? null })
    .returning();
  return row;
}

export async function finishTurn(id: string, patch: Partial<NewTurn>): Promise<void> {
  await getDb().update(turns).set(patch).where(eq(turns.id, id));
}

export async function markAnswered(messageIds: string[], turnId: string | null): Promise<void> {
  if (messageIds.length === 0) return;
  await getDb().update(messages).set({ answeredTurnId: turnId }).where(inArray(messages.id, messageIds));
}

export async function updateConversation(id: string, patch: Partial<Conversation>): Promise<void> {
  await getDb().update(conversations).set({ ...patch, updatedAt: new Date() }).where(eq(conversations.id, id));
}

export type HandleMap = Record<string, { kind: "ambulance" | "clinic"; id: string }>;

/** Union of the handle maps stored on the last 20 turns, newest winning. */
export async function loadConversationHandles(conversationId: string): Promise<HandleMap> {
  const rows = await getDb()
    .select({ handles: turns.handles })
    .from(turns)
    .where(eq(turns.conversationId, conversationId))
    .orderBy(desc(turns.createdAt))
    .limit(20);
  const merged: HandleMap = {};
  for (const r of rows.reverse()) Object.assign(merged, (r.handles ?? {}) as HandleMap);
  return merged;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `src/server/worker/deliver.ts`**

```ts
import { getDb } from "../db/client";
import { messages, type Conversation, type Message } from "../db/schema";
import { sendInteractiveList, sendText, type InteractiveListSpec, type InteraktResult } from "../interakt/client";
import { updateConversation } from "./persist";
import type { MessageOrigin } from "../types";
import { log } from "../log";

async function persistOutbound(
  conversation: Conversation,
  input: { text: string; template?: string | null; origin: MessageOrigin; turnId?: string | null; kind?: "text" | "interactive" },
  send: InteraktResult | null
): Promise<Message> {
  const [row] = await getDb()
    .insert(messages)
    .values({
      conversationId: conversation.id,
      role: "assistant",
      origin: input.origin,
      kind: input.kind ?? "text",
      content: input.text,
      contentTemplate: input.template ?? null,
      providerMessageId: send?.messageId ? `out:${send.messageId}` : null,
      deliveryStatus: send === null ? "test_skipped" : send.ok ? "sent" : "failed",
      error: send && !send.ok ? send.error ?? `status=${send.status}` : null,
    })
    .returning();
  await updateConversation(conversation.id, { lastOutboundAt: new Date() });
  return row;
}

/** Sends free-form text (skipped for test conversations) and persists the assistant message. */
export async function deliverText(
  conversation: Conversation,
  input: { text: string; template?: string | null; origin: MessageOrigin; turnId?: string | null }
): Promise<Message> {
  const send = conversation.isTest ? null : await sendText(conversation.phone, input.text);
  if (send && !send.ok) log.warn({ conversationId: conversation.id, error: send.error }, "outbound failed");
  return persistOutbound(conversation, input, send);
}

/** Sends a WhatsApp list; falls back to plain text when the list is rejected. */
export async function deliverList(
  conversation: Conversation,
  input: { list: InteractiveListSpec; fallbackText: string; origin: MessageOrigin }
): Promise<{ message: Message; usedList: boolean }> {
  if (conversation.isTest) {
    const message = await persistOutbound(conversation, { text: input.fallbackText, origin: input.origin, kind: "interactive" }, null);
    return { message, usedList: true };
  }
  const listSend = await sendInteractiveList(conversation.phone, input.list);
  if (listSend.ok) {
    const message = await persistOutbound(conversation, { text: input.fallbackText, origin: input.origin, kind: "interactive" }, listSend);
    return { message, usedList: true };
  }
  log.warn({ conversationId: conversation.id, error: listSend.error }, "list send failed, falling back to text");
  const textSend = await sendText(conversation.phone, input.fallbackText);
  const message = await persistOutbound(conversation, { text: input.fallbackText, origin: input.origin }, textSend);
  return { message, usedList: false };
}
```

### Task 12: Claude pause state and the no-LLM fallback turn

Implements spec §5.10 and the `worker_state` part of §5.4. Covers Review Focus items 3 and 4.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/server/worker/pause.ts`, `src/server/worker/fallback.ts`, `tests/db/fallback.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (`pause.ts`): `getWorkerState(): Promise<{ claudePausedUntil: Date | null; pauseReason: string | null; lastHeartbeatAt: Date | null; lastOkTurnAt: Date | null }>`, `isClaudePaused(now?: Date): Promise<boolean>`, `pauseClaude(until: Date, reason: string): Promise<void>`, `clearClaudePause(): Promise<void>`, `heartbeat(): Promise<void>`, `recordOkTurn(): Promise<void>`.
- Produces (`fallback.ts`): `runFallbackTurn(input: { conversation: Conversation; pending: Message[]; reason: string }): Promise<{ turn: Turn; message: Message }>`, `pendingText(pending: Message[]): string`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/server/worker/pause.ts`**

```ts
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { getDb } from "../db/client";
import { workerState } from "../db/schema";

async function ensureRow() {
  await getDb().insert(workerState).values({ singleton: true }).onConflictDoNothing();
}

export async function getWorkerState() {
  await ensureRow();
  const [row] = await getDb().select().from(workerState).where(eq(workerState.singleton, true));
  return {
    claudePausedUntil: row.claudePausedUntil,
    pauseReason: row.pauseReason,
    lastHeartbeatAt: row.lastHeartbeatAt,
    lastOkTurnAt: row.lastOkTurnAt,
  };
}

export async function isClaudePaused(now: Date = new Date()): Promise<boolean> {
  const s = await getWorkerState();
  return !!s.claudePausedUntil && s.claudePausedUntil.getTime() > now.getTime();
}

export async function pauseClaude(until: Date, reason: string): Promise<void> {
  await ensureRow();
  await getDb().update(workerState).set({ claudePausedUntil: until, pauseReason: reason }).where(eq(workerState.singleton, true));
}

export async function clearClaudePause(): Promise<void> {
  await ensureRow();
  await getDb().update(workerState).set({ claudePausedUntil: null, pauseReason: null }).where(eq(workerState.singleton, true));
}

export async function heartbeat(): Promise<void> {
  await ensureRow();
  await getDb().update(workerState).set({ lastHeartbeatAt: new Date() }).where(eq(workerState.singleton, true));
}

export async function recordOkTurn(): Promise<void> {
  await ensureRow();
  await getDb().update(workerState).set({ lastOkTurnAt: new Date() }).where(eq(workerState.singleton, true));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/server/worker/fallback.ts`**

```ts
import type { Conversation, Message, Turn } from "../db/schema";
import { getConfig } from "../config";
import { detectLanguage } from "../lang";
import { loadActiveAmbulances } from "../directory/repo";
import { matchAmbulances } from "../directory/matcher";
import { buildAmbulanceCard, buildHelplineCard, buildMultiAmbulanceCard } from "../messages/cards";
import { fallbackFoundIntro, fallbackNoMatch } from "../messages/system-replies";
import { deliverText } from "./deliver";
import { createTurn, finishTurn, markAnswered, updateConversation } from "./persist";
import { recordEvent } from "../inbound/store";
import type { Language } from "../types";

/** Text the matcher sees: message bodies plus resolved place labels for pins. */
export function pendingText(pending: Message[]): string {
  return pending
    .map((m) => (m.kind === "location" ? m.placeLabel ?? "" : m.content))
    .filter((t) => t && t.trim().length > 0)
    .join(" ");
}

/**
 * Pure-code answer used whenever Claude is paused or failed. Never asks a
 * question, never promises a callback; always ends with a real number.
 */
export async function runFallbackTurn(input: { conversation: Conversation; pending: Message[]; reason: string }) {
  const { conversation, pending, reason } = input;
  const text = pendingText(pending);
  const lang: Language = conversation.language ?? detectLanguage(text);
  const turn = await createTurn({ conversationId: conversation.id, status: "fallback", model: "fallback" });

  const rows = await loadActiveAmbulances();
  const match = matchAmbulances(rows, { query: text });
  let reply: string;
  if (match.candidates.length === 1) {
    reply = `${fallbackFoundIntro(lang)}\n\n${buildAmbulanceCard(match.candidates[0], lang)}`;
  } else if (match.candidates.length >= 2 && match.candidates.length <= 3) {
    reply = `${fallbackFoundIntro(lang)}\n\n${buildMultiAmbulanceCard(match.candidates, lang)}`;
  } else {
    reply = `${fallbackNoMatch(lang)}\n${buildHelplineCard(getConfig().helplinePhone, lang)}`;
  }

  const message = await deliverText(conversation, { text: reply, origin: "fallback", turnId: turn.id });
  await markAnswered(pending.map((m) => m.id), turn.id);
  await finishTurn(turn.id, {
    error: reason,
    structuredOutput: { matched: match.candidates.length, matchType: match.match },
    latencyMs: 0,
  });
  await updateConversation(conversation.id, {
    language: lang,
    flagged: true,
    flagReason: conversation.flagged ? conversation.flagReason : "claude_unavailable",
  });
  await recordEvent("fallback_turn", { reason, matched: match.candidates.length }, conversation.id);
  const [finished] = [{ ...turn, status: "fallback" as const, error: reason }];
  return { turn: finished as Turn, message };
}
```

```ts
export async function enqueueTurn(
  conversationId: string,
  opts: { immediate?: boolean; nextSlot?: boolean } = {}
): Promise<string | null> {
  const boss = await getBoss();
  return boss.send(
    TURN_QUEUE,
    { conversationId } satisfies TurnJobData,
    {
      singletonKey: conversationId,
      singletonSeconds: 3,
      singletonNextSlot: opts.nextSlot ?? false,
      startAfter: opts.immediate ? 0 : 2,
    }
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/server/worker/first-contact.ts`**

```ts
import { and, eq, isNull, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { getDb } from "../db/client";
import { conversations, type Conversation, type Message } from "../db/schema";
import { isGreetingOnly, isMenuWord } from "../lang";
import { MENU_BUTTON_LABEL, menuRows, menuText, welcomeText } from "../messages/welcome";
import { deliverList } from "./deliver";
import type { Language } from "../types";

/** Atomic first-contact claim: true for exactly one caller per conversation. */
export async function claimWelcome(conversationId: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const rows = await getDb()
    .update(conversations)
    .set({ welcomedAt: sql`now()`, updatedAt: sql`now()` })
    .where(and(eq(conversations.id, conversationId), isNull(conversations.welcomedAt)))
    .returning({ id: conversations.id });
  return rows.length === 1;
}

export async function sendWelcome(conversation: Conversation, lang: Language): Promise<Message> {
  const r = await deliverList(conversation, {
    list: { body: welcomeText(lang), buttonLabel: MENU_BUTTON_LABEL[lang], rows: menuRows(lang) },
    fallbackText: welcomeText(lang),
    origin: "system",
  });
  return r.message;
}

export async function sendMenu(conversation: Conversation, lang: Language): Promise<Message> {
  const r = await deliverList(conversation, {
    list: { body: menuText(lang), buttonLabel: MENU_BUTTON_LABEL[lang], rows: menuRows(lang) },
    fallbackText: menuText(lang),
    origin: "system",
  });
  return r.message;
}

/** Classifies a burst so the runner can short-circuit without Claude. */
export function classifyPending(pending: Message[]): { allGreeting: boolean; allMenu: boolean; allAudio: boolean } {
  const isMenuTap = (m: Message) => m.kind === "interactive" && !!m.interactiveId?.startsWith("menu:");
  const isTrivial = (m: Message) =>
    m.kind === "sticker" || (m.kind === "interactive" && !isMenuTap(m) && isGreetingOnly(m.content)) || (m.kind === "text" && isGreetingOnly(m.content));
  return {
    allGreeting: pending.length > 0 && pending.every(isTrivial),
    allMenu: pending.length > 0 && pending.every((m) => m.kind === "text" && isMenuWord(m.content)),
    allAudio: pending.length > 0 && pending.every((m) => m.kind === "audio"),
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `src/server/worker/turn-runner.ts` (v1)**

```ts
import { getSql } from "../db/client";
import { detectLanguage } from "../lang";
import { audioNotSupported } from "../messages/system-replies";
import { enqueueTurn } from "../queue/boss";
import { log } from "../log";
import { gatherPending } from "./gather";
import { claimWelcome, classifyPending, sendMenu, sendWelcome } from "./first-contact";
import { runFallbackTurn, pendingText } from "./fallback";
import { deliverText } from "./deliver";
import { createTurn, markAnswered, updateConversation } from "./persist";
import type { Language } from "../types";

export interface TurnDeps {
  backend?: unknown; // replaced by LLMBackend in Task 21
}

export type TurnOutcome = {
  kind: "noop" | "locked" | "human_mode" | "welcome" | "menu" | "audio_only" | "fallback" | "ok" | "failed";
  turnId?: string;
  requeued?: boolean;
};

/** Session-level advisory lock on a reserved connection; released in `finally`. */
async function withConversationLock<T>(conversationId: string, fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T | "locked"> {
  const reserved = await getSql().reserve();
  try {
    const [{ locked }] = await reserved<{ locked: boolean }[]>`select pg_try_advisory_lock(hashtext(${conversationId})::bigint) as locked`;
    if (!locked) return "locked";
    try {
      return await fn();
    } finally {
      await reserved`select pg_advisory_unlock(hashtext(${conversationId})::bigint)`;
    }
  } finally {
    reserved.release();
  }
}

export async function runTurn(conversationId: string, _deps: TurnDeps = {}): Promise<TurnOutcome> {
  const result = await withConversationLock(conversationId, () => runTurnLocked(conversationId));
  if (result === "locked") {
    await enqueueTurn(conversationId, { nextSlot: true });
    return { kind: "locked", requeued: true };
  }
  return result;
}

async function requeueIfPending(conversationId: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const again = await gatherPending(conversationId);
  if (again && again.pending.length > 0 && again.conversation.mode === "agent") {
    await enqueueTurn(conversationId, { immediate: true, nextSlot: true });
    return true;
  }
  return false;
}

async function runTurnLocked(conversationId: string): Promise<TurnOutcome> {
  const gathered = await gatherPending(conversationId);
  if (!gathered || gathered.pending.length === 0) return { kind: "noop" };
  let { conversation } = gathered;
  const { pending } = gathered;
  if (conversation.mode === "human") return { kind: "human_mode" };

  const text = pendingText(pending);
  const lang: Language = conversation.language ?? detectLanguage(text);
  const cls = classifyPending(pending);
  let welcomeJustSent = false;

  if (await claimWelcome(conversation.id)) {
    await sendWelcome(conversation, lang);
    await updateConversation(conversation.id, { language: lang });
    conversation = { ...conversation, language: lang, welcomedAt: new Date() };
    welcomeJustSent = true;
    if (cls.allGreeting) {
      const turn = await createTurn({ conversationId: conversation.id, status: "ok", model: "system:welcome" });
      await markAnswered(pending.map((m) => m.id), turn.id);
      return { kind: "welcome", turnId: turn.id, requeued: await requeueIfPending(conversation.id) };
    }
  }

  if (cls.allMenu) {
    await sendMenu(conversation, lang);
    const turn = await createTurn({ conversationId: conversation.id, status: "ok", model: "system:menu" });
    await markAnswered(pending.map((m) => m.id), turn.id);
    return { kind: "menu", turnId: turn.id, requeued: await requeueIfPending(conversation.id) };
  }

  if (cls.allAudio) {
    const turn = await createTurn({ conversationId: conversation.id, status: "ok", model: "system:audio" });
    await deliverText(conversation, { text: audioNotSupported(lang), origin: "system", turnId: turn.id });
    await markAnswered(pending.map((m) => m.id), turn.id);
    return { kind: "audio_only", turnId: turn.id, requeued: await requeueIfPending(conversation.id) };
  }

  if (cls.allGreeting && !welcomeJustSent) {
    // Already welcomed; a bare greeting later gets a short line from Claude in Task 21. Until then, the menu.
    await sendMenu(conversation, lang);
    const turn = await createTurn({ conversationId: conversation.id, status: "ok", model: "system:menu" });
    await markAnswered(pending.map((m) => m.id), turn.id);
    return { kind: "menu", turnId: turn.id, requeued: await requeueIfPending(conversation.id) };
  }

  log.info({ conversationId, welcomeJustSent }, "running fallback turn (no backend yet)");
  const r = await runFallbackTurn({ conversation, pending, reason: "no_backend" });
  return { kind: "fallback", turnId: r.turn.id, requeued: await requeueIfPending(conversation.id) };
}
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach } from "vitest";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { resetDb, createConversation } from "../helpers/db";
import { seedDirectory } from "../helpers/fixtures";
import { getDb } from "@/server/db/client";
import { conversations, messages } from "@/server/db/schema";
import { storeReporterMessage } from "@/server/inbound/store";
import { enqueueTurn, stopBoss } from "@/server/queue/boss";
import { startWorker } from "@/server/worker/start";
import { getWorkerState } from "@/server/worker/pause";

describe("startWorker", () => {
  beforeEach(async () => { await stopBoss(); await resetDb(); await seedDirectory(); });
  afterEach(async () => { await stopBoss(); });

  it("processes a queued turn end to end and heartbeats", async () => {
    const c = await createConversation({ isTest: true, phone: "+91TEST_worker" });
    await getDb().update(conversations).set({ welcomedAt: new Date() }).where(eq(conversations.id, c.id));
    await storeReporterMessage(c.id, { kind: "text", content: "Surat" });
    const worker = await startWorker({ concurrency: 1, heartbeatMs: 200 });
    try {
      await enqueueTurn(c.id, { immediate: true });
      const deadline = Date.now() + 15_000;
      let out: { content: string }[] = [];
      while (Date.now() < deadline) {
        out = (await getDb().select().from(messages).where(eq(messages.conversationId, c.id))).filter((m) => m.role === "assistant");
        if (out.length > 0) break;
        await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 200));
      }
      expect(out.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
      expect(out[0].content.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 400));
      expect((await getWorkerState()).lastHeartbeatAt).not.toBeNull();
    } finally {
      await worker.stop();
    }
  });
});
```

Expected: FAIL.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/server/worker/start.ts`**

```ts
import { getConfig } from "../config";
import { log } from "../log";
import { getBoss, stopBoss, TURN_QUEUE, type TurnJobData } from "../queue/boss";
import { setWebsiteUrl } from "../messages/welcome";
import { heartbeat } from "./pause";
import { runTurn } from "./turn-runner";
import { closeDb } from "../db/client";

export async function startWorker(opts: { concurrency?: number; heartbeatMs?: number } = {}) {
  const cfg = getConfig();
  setWebsiteUrl(cfg.websiteUrl);
  const boss = await getBoss("worker");

  await boss.work<TurnJobData>(
    TURN_QUEUE,
    { batchSize: 1, localConcurrency: opts.concurrency ?? cfg.workerConcurrency, pollingIntervalSeconds: 1 },
    async ([job]) => {
      const started = Date.now();
      const outcome = await runTurn(job.data.conversationId);
      log.info({ conversationId: job.data.conversationId, outcome: outcome.kind, ms: Date.now() - started }, "turn done");
    }
  );

  await heartbeat();
  const timer = setInterval(() => {
    heartbeat().catch((err) => log.warn({ err }, "heartbeat failed"));
  }, opts.heartbeatMs ?? 30_000);

  log.info({ concurrency: opts.concurrency ?? cfg.workerConcurrency }, "worker started");
  return {
    async stop() {
      clearInterval(timer);
      await stopBoss();
    },
  };
}

export async function runWorkerProcess(): Promise<void> {
  const worker = await startWorker();
  const shutdown = async (signal: string) => {
    log.info({ signal }, "worker shutting down");
    await worker.stop();
    await closeDb();
    process.exit(0);
  };
  process.on("SIGTERM", () => void shutdown("SIGTERM"));
  process.on("SIGINT", () => void shutdown("SIGINT"));
}
```

```ts
runWorkerProcess().catch((err) => {
  console.error("worker failed to start", err);
  process.exit(1);
});
```

```ts
import type { McpSdkServerConfigWithInstance } from "@anthropic-ai/claude-agent-sdk";
import type { Intent, Language } from "../types";

export interface StructuredReply {
  reply: string;
  language: Language;
  intent: Intent;
  location: { city: string | null; area: string | null };
  needs_human: boolean;
  notes: string | null;
}

export interface TurnInput {
  conversationId: string;
  systemPrompt: string;
  prompt: string;
  toolServer: McpSdkServerConfigWithInstance | null;
  allowedToolNames: string[];
  timeoutMs: number;
}

export type BackendErrorKind = "LIMITED" | "AUTH" | "TRANSIENT" | "INVALID_OUTPUT";

export type TurnResult =
  | {
      ok: true;
      structured: StructuredReply;
      usage: Record<string, unknown> | null;
      latencyMs: number;
      sessionId: string | null;
      model: string | null;
      costUsd: number | null;
    }
  | { ok: false; kind: BackendErrorKind; message: string; retryAfter: Date | null; latencyMs: number };

export interface LLMBackend {
  readonly name: string;
  runTurn(input: TurnInput): Promise<TurnResult>;
}
```

```ts
import { z } from "zod";
import type { StructuredReply } from "./types";

export const REPLY_JSON_SCHEMA = {
  type: "object",
  properties: {
    reply: { type: "string", minLength: 1, maxLength: 1500, description: "The exact WhatsApp message to send, in the reporter's language. Phone numbers only as {{AMB:handle}}, {{CLINIC:handle}} or {{PHONE:helpline}} tokens." },
    language: { type: "string", enum: ["en", "hi", "mr", "gu"] },
    intent: { type: "string", enum: ["ambulance", "clinic", "donate", "volunteer", "faq", "suggestion", "smalltalk", "other"] },
    location: {
      type: "object",
      properties: { city: { type: ["string", "null"] }, area: { type: ["string", "null"] } },
      required: ["city", "area"],
      additionalProperties: false,
    },
    needs_human: { type: "boolean", description: "True when a human should look at this conversation." },
    notes: { type: ["string", "null"], description: "One internal line for the admin page, never sent." },
  },
  required: ["reply", "language", "intent", "location", "needs_human", "notes"],
  additionalProperties: false,
} as const;

export const replySchema = z.object({
  reply: z.string().min(1).max(1500),
  language: z.enum(["en", "hi", "mr", "gu"]),
  intent: z.enum(["ambulance", "clinic", "donate", "volunteer", "faq", "suggestion", "smalltalk", "other"]),
  location: z.object({ city: z.string().nullable(), area: z.string().nullable() }),
  needs_human: z.boolean(),
  notes: z.string().nullable(),
});

export function parseStructuredReply(u: unknown): StructuredReply | null {
  const r = replySchema.safeParse(u);
  return r.success ? r.data : null;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `src/server/agent/handles.ts` and update `persist.ts`**

```ts
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";

export type HandleKind = "ambulance" | "clinic";
export type HandleMap = Record<string, { kind: HandleKind; id: string }>;

/** Stable within a conversation, opaque across conversations. */
export function makeHandle(kind: HandleKind, conversationId: string, entityId: string): string {
  const hex = createHash("sha256").update(`${conversationId}:${kind}:${entityId}`).digest("hex").slice(0, 8);
  return `${kind === "ambulance" ? "amb" : "cli"}_${hex}`;
}

export class HandleRegistry {
  private map: HandleMap;
  constructor(private readonly conversationId: string, initial: HandleMap = {}) {
    this.map = { ...initial };
  }
  ambulance(id: string): string {
    const h = makeHandle("ambulance", this.conversationId, id);
    this.map[h] = { kind: "ambulance", id };
    return h;
  }
  clinic(id: string): string {
    const h = makeHandle("clinic", this.conversationId, id);
    this.map[h] = { kind: "clinic", id };
    return h;
  }
  resolve(handle: string): { kind: HandleKind; id: string } | undefined {
    return this.map[handle];
  }
  handles(): string[] {
    return Object.keys(this.map);
  }
  toJSON(): HandleMap {
    return { ...this.map };
  }
}

export const TOKEN_RE = /\{\{(AMB|CLINIC|PHONE):([A-Za-z0-9_-]+)\}\}/g;

export function extractTokens(text: string): { kind: "AMB" | "CLINIC" | "PHONE"; value: string; raw: string }[] {
  const out: { kind: "AMB" | "CLINIC" | "PHONE"; value: string; raw: string }[] = [];
  for (const m of text.matchAll(TOKEN_RE)) out.push({ kind: m[1] as "AMB" | "CLINIC" | "PHONE", value: m[2], raw: m[0] });
  return out;
}
```

In `src/server/worker/persist.ts`, replace the local `export type HandleMap = ...` line with `import type { HandleMap } from "../agent/handles"; export type { HandleMap };`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/server/agent/tools.ts`**

```ts
import { createSdkMcpServer, tool, type McpSdkServerConfigWithInstance } from "@anthropic-ai/claude-agent-sdk";
import { z } from "zod";
import type { Language } from "../types";
import { HandleRegistry } from "./handles";
import { loadActiveAmbulances, loadActiveClinics } from "../directory/repo";
import { matchAmbulances, normalize, type DirectoryAmbulance, type MatchType } from "../directory/matcher";
import { fuzzyEqual } from "../directory/fuzzy";
import { ambulanceDisplayName } from "../messages/cards";
import { getStaticInfo, type InfoContent, type InfoTopic } from "../content/static";

export interface ToolCallRecord {
  name: string;
  input: unknown;
  output: unknown;
  durationMs: number;
  error?: string;
}

export interface ToolContext {
  conversationId: string;
  language: Language;
  registry: HandleRegistry;
  calls: ToolCallRecord[];
  lastAmbulanceCandidates: DirectoryAmbulance[] | null;
}

export const SERVER_NAME = "arham";
export const TOOL_NAMES = [`mcp__${SERVER_NAME}__find_ambulances`, `mcp__${SERVER_NAME}__find_clinics`, `mcp__${SERVER_NAME}__get_info`];

export interface FindAmbulancesOutput {
  candidates: {
    handle: string; display_name: string; city: string; area: string | null; areas_covered: string[];
    category: string; operator_name: string; operator_is_arham: boolean;
  }[];
  match: MatchType | null;
  city_areas: string[] | null;
  out_of_coverage: boolean;
}

async function timed<T>(ctx: ToolContext, name: string, input: unknown, fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
  const started = Date.now();
  try {
    const output = await fn();
    ctx.calls.push({ name, input, output, durationMs: Date.now() - started });
    return output;
  } catch (e) {
    const error = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);
    ctx.calls.push({ name, input, output: null, durationMs: Date.now() - started, error });
    throw e;
  }
}

export async function findAmbulancesImpl(
  ctx: ToolContext,
  input: { city?: string | null; area?: string | null; query: string }
): Promise<FindAmbulancesOutput> {
  return timed(ctx, "find_ambulances", input, async () => {
    const rows = await loadActiveAmbulances();
    const result = matchAmbulances(rows, { city: input.city ?? null, area: input.area ?? null, query: input.query });
    ctx.lastAmbulanceCandidates = result.candidates;
    return {
      candidates: result.candidates.map((r) => ({
        handle: ctx.registry.ambulance(r.id),
        display_name: ambulanceDisplayName(r),
        city: r.city,
        area: r.area,
        areas_covered: r.areasCovered,
        category: r.category,
        operator_name: r.operatorName,
        operator_is_arham: r.operatorIsArham,
      })),
      match: result.match,
      city_areas: result.cityAreas,
      out_of_coverage: result.outOfCoverage,
    };
  });
}

export async function findClinicsImpl(ctx: ToolContext, input: { city?: string | null }) {
  return timed(ctx, "find_clinics", input, async () => {
    const rows = await loadActiveClinics();
    const wanted = input.city ? normalize(input.city) : "";
    const filtered = wanted ? rows.filter((c) => normalize(c.city) === wanted || fuzzyEqual(normalize(c.city), wanted)) : rows;
    return {
      clinics: filtered.map((c) => ({
        handle: ctx.registry.clinic(c.id),
        label: c.label, city: c.city, area: c.area, address: c.address, hours: c.hours,
      })),
    };
  });
}

export function getInfoImpl(ctx: ToolContext, input: { topic: InfoTopic }): InfoContent {
  const started = Date.now();
  const output = getStaticInfo(input.topic, ctx.language);
  ctx.calls.push({ name: "get_info", input, output, durationMs: Date.now() - started });
  return output;
}

const text = (o: unknown) => ({ content: [{ type: "text" as const, text: JSON.stringify(o) }] });
const errorResult = (e: unknown) => ({ content: [{ type: "text" as const, text: JSON.stringify({ error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e) }) }], isError: true });

export function createArhamTools(ctx: ToolContext): { server: McpSdkServerConfigWithInstance; allowedToolNames: string[] } {
  const server = createSdkMcpServer({
    name: SERVER_NAME,
    version: "2.0.0",
    tools: [
      tool(
        "find_ambulances",
        "Find animal ambulances covering a location. Pass the city and area you understood from the reporter (normalize typos and Indic spellings yourself using the coverage list) plus the reporter's own words as query. Returns candidates with an opaque handle each; to give a number write {{AMB:<handle>}} in your reply. Never returns phone digits.",
        {
          city: z.string().nullable().optional().describe("City as spelled in the coverage list, or null if unknown"),
          area: z.string().nullable().optional().describe("Area/suburb/landmark as spelled in the coverage list, or null"),
          query: z.string().describe("The reporter's own location words"),
        },
        async (args) => {
          try {
            return text(await findAmbulancesImpl(ctx, { city: args.city ?? null, area: args.area ?? null, query: args.query }));
          } catch (e) {
            return errorResult(e);
          }
        }
      ),
      tool(
        "find_clinics",
        "List Arham clinics, optionally filtered by city. Returns handles; write {{CLINIC:<handle>}} to give a clinic's number.",
        { city: z.string().nullable().optional() },
        async (args) => {
          try {
            return text(await findClinicsImpl(ctx, { city: args.city ?? null }));
          } catch (e) {
            return errorResult(e);
          }
        }
      ),
      tool(
        "get_info",
        "Static information: donate, volunteer, about, faq, human_emergency (112/108 text), helpline (the {{PHONE:helpline}} token to hand over when you cannot help).",
        { topic: z.enum(["donate", "volunteer", "about", "faq", "human_emergency", "helpline"]) },
        async (args) => {
          try {
            return text(getInfoImpl(ctx, { topic: args.topic }));
          } catch (e) {
            return errorResult(e);
          }
        }
      ),
    ],
  });
  return { server, allowedToolNames: TOOL_NAMES };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/server/agent/transcript.ts`**

```ts
import type { Message } from "../db/schema";
import { menuChoiceLabel } from "../messages/welcome";

type Renderable = Pick<Message, "role" | "kind" | "content" | "contentTemplate" | "placeLabel" | "interactiveId" | "createdAt">;

const timeFmt = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-IN", { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit", hour12: false, timeZone: "Asia/Kolkata" });

export function renderMessageForModel(m: Renderable): string {
  const time = timeFmt.format(m.createdAt);
  if (m.role === "assistant") return `[${time}] Assistant: ${m.contentTemplate ?? m.content}`;
  let body: string;
  switch (m.kind) {
    case "audio": body = "[voice note received — not transcribed]"; break;
    case "image": body = `[photo received]${m.content ? ` ${m.content}` : ""}`; break;
    case "video": body = `[video received]${m.content ? ` ${m.content}` : ""}`; break;
    case "document": body = `[document received]${m.content ? ` ${m.content}` : ""}`; break;
    case "sticker": body = "[sticker]"; break;
    case "contact": body = "[contact card received]"; break;
    case "location": body = m.placeLabel ? `[location pin: ${m.placeLabel}]` : "[location pin received]"; break;
    case "interactive": {
      const label = m.interactiveId ? menuChoiceLabel(m.interactiveId) : null;
      body = label ? `[menu choice: ${label}]` : m.content;
      break;
    }
    default: body = m.content;
  }
  return `[${time}] Reporter: ${body}`;
}

export function renderTranscript(history: Message[], pending: Message[]): string {
  const pendingIds = new Set(pending.map((m) => m.id));
  const past = history.filter((m) => !pendingIds.has(m.id)).map(renderMessageForModel);
  const fresh = pending.map(renderMessageForModel);
  return `${past.join("\n")}\n\n--- NEW MESSAGES (answer these) ---\n${fresh.join("\n")}`.trim();
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/server/agent/prompt.ts`**

```ts
import type { Language } from "../types";

export function formatIst(d: Date): string {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-IN", {
    weekday: "short", day: "2-digit", month: "short", year: "numeric", hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit", hour12: false, timeZone: "Asia/Kolkata",
  }).format(d) + " IST";
}

/** Byte-stable per process except for the coverage block (refreshed hourly). */
export function buildSystemPrompt(input: { coverage: string; websiteUrl: string }): string {
  return `You are the WhatsApp helpline assistant for Arham Always Care, the animal-rescue project of Arham Yuva Seva Group, an Indian non-profit. Website: ${input.websiteUrl}.

WHAT WE DO
Arham and partner NGOs run free animal ambulances and clinics in the cities listed below for injured or sick stray animals. Our job on this chat is to give the reporter the right ambulance driver's number quickly and kindly. The reporter calls the driver themselves. We do not dispatch: never say "we are sending", "they are on the way", "dispatched" or anything implying we control the ambulance. Partner-operated units are labelled by the system automatically when you give their number.

COVERAGE (from the live directory; use it to normalise typos, transliterate Devanagari/Gujarati place names, and recognise neighbouring suburbs)
${input.coverage}
If the reporter's place is clearly outside every listed city, say we don't cover it yet, suggest contacting a local rescue group, and offer {{PHONE:helpline}}.

HOW TO ANSWER AN AMBULANCE REQUEST
1. Work out the city and area from the whole conversation (never re-ask for a location you already have).
2. Call find_ambulances with city, area and the reporter's own words. Do this as soon as you have a city; ask for the area only when the tool returns city_areas.
3. One candidate: give it. Two or three: give them all. More: ask which area, naming real areas from city_areas. None: out-of-coverage reply.
4. Every number you give is a token: {{AMB:<handle>}} from the tool result. Never type phone digits yourself, never invent a handle.
5. If the animal is in immediate danger, add one short practical line (keep it warm, don't move a badly injured animal, keep it warm and calm) — never medication advice.

OTHER REQUESTS
- Clinics: call find_clinics (optionally with city) and give {{CLINIC:<handle>}} tokens.
- Donations, volunteering, about, FAQ: call get_info and answer from it; include the URL it returns.
- Medication or treatment questions: don't advise; offer the nearest clinic.
- A human emergency: get_info("human_emergency") gives the 112/108 text to relay.
- Requests for a human, complaints, or anything you cannot handle: say so plainly, include {{PHONE:helpline}}, and set needs_human to true.
- Short acknowledgements ("ok", "thanks", "🙏"): one short warm line. No menu, no "anything else?" every time.

STYLE
- Reply in the reporter's language and script. Hinglish stays Hinglish (Latin script). Mirror switches.
- Two to five short sentences. WhatsApp formatting only: *bold*, _italic_, line breaks. No markdown headings, no bullet lists, no **double asterisks**. At most one emoji.
- Warm, calm, practical. Ask at most one clarifying question per reply.
- Remember everything said earlier in this chat; the transcript is your memory.

TOKENS (the only way a phone number may appear)
{{AMB:<handle>}}  {{CLINIC:<handle>}}  {{PHONE:helpline}}

OUTPUT
Fill every field of the JSON schema. "reply" is the exact message to send. "location" is the city/area you now believe the reporter is in (null if unknown). "notes" is one internal line for the admin page.`;
}

export interface TurnContext {
  nowIst: string;
  language: Language | null;
  knownLocation: { city: string | null; area: string | null } | null;
  handlesShared: string[];
  welcomeJustSent: boolean;
  transcript: string;
  correction: string | null;
}

export function buildTurnPrompt(ctx: TurnContext): string {
  const loc = ctx.knownLocation && (ctx.knownLocation.city || ctx.knownLocation.area)
    ? [ctx.knownLocation.area, ctx.knownLocation.city].filter(Boolean).join(", ")
    : "unknown";
  const lines = [
    "CONTEXT",
    `Current time: ${ctx.nowIst}`,
    `Language so far: ${ctx.language ?? "unknown"}`,
    `Location known so far: ${loc}`,
    `Handles already shared in this chat: ${ctx.handlesShared.length ? ctx.handlesShared.join(", ") : "none"}`,
  ];
  if (ctx.welcomeJustSent) lines.push("The welcome menu was just sent by the system. Do not greet again; answer the reporter's message directly.");
  if (ctx.correction) lines.push("", ctx.correction);
  lines.push("", "TRANSCRIPT", ctx.transcript, "", "Write the next assistant reply now.");
  return lines.join("\n");
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/server/agent/render.ts`**

```ts
import type { Intent, Language } from "../types";
import type { DirectoryAmbulance } from "../directory/matcher";
import type { DirectoryClinic } from "../directory/repo";
import { HandleRegistry, TOKEN_RE } from "./handles";
import { buildAmbulanceCard, buildMultiAmbulanceCard, formatIndianPhone, renderNumberForToken } from "../messages/cards";

export interface RenderLookups {
  ambulance(id: string): Promise<DirectoryAmbulance | undefined>;
  clinic(id: string): Promise<DirectoryClinic | undefined>;
}

export interface RenderInput {
  replyTemplate: string;
  lang: Language;
  intent: Intent;
  registry: HandleRegistry;
  lookups: RenderLookups;
  helplinePhone: string;
  staticPhones: Record<string, string>;
  lastAmbulanceCandidates: DirectoryAmbulance[] | null;
}

export interface RenderResult {
  text: string;
  template: string;
  inserted: string[];
  unknownHandles: string[];
  appendedCard: boolean;
}

export function cleanupFormatting(text: string): string {
  return text
    .replace(/\*\*(.+?)\*\*/g, "*$1*")
    .replace(/^#{1,6}\s+/gm, "")
    .replace(/^\s*[-*]\s+/gm, "• ")
    .replace(/\n{3,}/g, "\n\n")
    .trim()
    .slice(0, 4000);
}

export async function renderReply(input: RenderInput): Promise<RenderResult> {
  const inserted: string[] = [];
  const unknownHandles: string[] = [];
  const template = input.replyTemplate;

  const replacements = new Map<string, string>();
  for (const m of template.matchAll(TOKEN_RE)) {
    const [raw, kind, value] = m;
    if (replacements.has(raw)) continue;
    if (kind === "PHONE") {
      const phone = value === "helpline" ? input.helplinePhone : input.staticPhones[value];
      if (!phone) { unknownHandles.push(value); replacements.set(raw, ""); continue; }
      inserted.push(phone);
      replacements.set(raw, formatIndianPhone(phone));
      continue;
    }
    const ref = input.registry.resolve(value);
    if (!ref || (kind === "AMB" && ref.kind !== "ambulance") || (kind === "CLINIC" && ref.kind !== "clinic")) {
      unknownHandles.push(value);
      replacements.set(raw, "");
      continue;
    }
    if (ref.kind === "ambulance") {
      const row = await input.lookups.ambulance(ref.id);
      if (!row) { unknownHandles.push(value); replacements.set(raw, ""); continue; }
      inserted.push(row.phone);
      replacements.set(raw, renderNumberForToken(row, input.lang));
    } else {
      const row = await input.lookups.clinic(ref.id);
      if (!row) { unknownHandles.push(value); replacements.set(raw, ""); continue; }
      inserted.push(row.phone);
      replacements.set(raw, formatIndianPhone(row.phone));
    }
  }

  let text = template;
  for (const [raw, rep] of replacements) text = text.split(raw).join(rep);
  text = text.replace(/[ \t]{2,}/g, " ").replace(/ +([.,!?])/g, "$1");

  let appendedCard = false;
  const hasAmbToken = /\{\{AMB:/.test(template);
  const cands = input.lastAmbulanceCandidates ?? [];
  if (input.intent === "ambulance" && !hasAmbToken && cands.length >= 1 && cands.length <= 3) {
    const card = cands.length === 1 ? buildAmbulanceCard(cands[0], input.lang) : buildMultiAmbulanceCard(cands, input.lang);
    text = `${text.trim()}\n\n${card}`;
    for (const c of cands) inserted.push(c.phone);
    appendedCard = true;
  }

  return { text: cleanupFormatting(text), template, inserted, unknownHandles, appendedCard };
}
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import { findDigitRuns, foreignDigitRuns, stripRuns, hasDispatchLanguage, replaceDispatchSentences } from "@/server/agent/scrub";

describe("digit scrubber", () => {
  it("finds runs of 7+ digits with spaces, dashes and plus", () => {
    expect(findDigitRuns("call +91 76620 05404 or 98765-43210 not 112 or 2026")).toEqual(["+91 76620 05404", "98765-43210"]);
  });
  it("allows numbers code inserted, with or without the country code", () => {
    const text = "A +91 76620 05404 B 7662005404 C +91 91671 19135";
    expect(foreignDigitRuns(text, ["+917662005404"])).toEqual(["+91 91671 19135"]);
    expect(foreignDigitRuns(text, ["+917662005404", "919167119135"])).toEqual([]);
  });
  it("strips foreign runs cleanly", () => {
    expect(stripRuns("Call 9876543210 now.", ["9876543210"])).toBe("Call now.");
  });
});

describe("dispatch guard", () => {
  it("detects dispatch language in four languages", () => {
    for (const t of ["We are sending an ambulance", "They're on the way!", "Ambulance dispatched.", "हम एम्बुलेंस भेज रहे हैं", "आम्ही पाठवत आहोत", "અમે મોકલી રહ્યા છીએ"]) expect(hasDispatchLanguage(t)).toBe(true);
    expect(hasDispatchLanguage("Please call them directly.")).toBe(false);
  });
  it("replaces only the offending sentence", () => {
    const out = replaceDispatchSentences("Here is the number. We are sending help now! Stay calm.", "Please call them directly and they will guide you.");
    expect(out).toBe("Here is the number. Please call them directly and they will guide you. Stay calm.");
    expect(replaceDispatchSentences("हम एम्बुलेंस भेज रहे हैं। शांत रहें।", "कृपया उन्हें सीधे कॉल करें।")).toBe("कृपया उन्हें सीधे कॉल करें। शांत रहें।");
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/server/agent/scrub.ts`**

```ts
export const DIGIT_RUN_RE = /\+?\d(?:[\s-]?\d){6,}/g;

export function findDigitRuns(text: string): string[] {
  return Array.from(text.matchAll(DIGIT_RUN_RE), (m) => m[0]);
}

export function digitsOf(run: string): string {
  return run.replace(/\D/g, "");
}

function sameNumber(a: string, b: string): boolean {
  if (a === b) return true;
  const [long, short] = a.length >= b.length ? [a, b] : [b, a];
  return short.length >= 10 && long.endsWith(short);
}

/** Runs whose digits do not match any allowed number (allowed may carry a 91 prefix or not). */
export function foreignDigitRuns(text: string, allowedNumbers: string[]): string[] {
  const allowed = allowedNumbers.map(digitsOf).filter(Boolean);
  return findDigitRuns(text).filter((run) => !allowed.some((a) => sameNumber(digitsOf(run), a)));
}

export function stripRuns(text: string, runs: string[]): string {
  let out = text;
  for (const run of runs) out = out.split(run).join("");
  return out.replace(/[ \t]{2,}/g, " ").replace(/ +([.,!?।])/g, "$1").trim();
}

export const DISPATCH_RE = /we(?:'| a)?re sending|on (?:the|their|its) way|dispatch(?:ed|ing)?\b|भेज रहे|पाठवत आहोत|મોકલી રહ્યા/i;

export function hasDispatchLanguage(text: string): boolean {
  return DISPATCH_RE.test(text);
}

/** Splits on sentence terminators (., !, ?, । and newlines) and replaces sentences that match. */
export function replaceDispatchSentences(text: string, replacement: string): string {
  const parts = text.match(/[^.!?।\n]+[.!?।]*\s*|\n+/g) ?? [text];
  return parts
    .map((p) => {
      if (!DISPATCH_RE.test(p)) return p;
      const trailingSpace = /\s$/.test(p) ? " " : "";
      return replacement + trailingSpace;
    })
    .join("")
    .replace(/[ \t]{2,}/g, " ")
    .trim();
}
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import { classifyOutcome } from "@/server/agent/claude-code-backend";

const success = (structured_output: unknown, extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => ({
  type: "result", subtype: "success", is_error: false, duration_ms: 1000, duration_api_ms: 900, num_turns: 2, result: "",
  stop_reason: "end_turn", total_cost_usd: 0.01, usage: { input_tokens: 1, output_tokens: 1 }, modelUsage: { "claude-sonnet-5": { costUSD: 0.01 } },
  permission_denials: [], uuid: "u", session_id: "s", structured_output, ...extra,
});
const good = { reply: "hi", language: "en", intent: "smalltalk", location: { city: null, area: null }, needs_human: false, notes: null };
const base = { rejectedLimit: null, assistantError: null, result: null, aborted: false, thrown: null, latencyMs: 5 };

describe("classifyOutcome", () => {
  it("returns ok with the parsed reply, session id, model and cost", () => {
    const r = classifyOutcome({ ...base, result: success(good) as never });
    expect(r.ok && r.structured.reply).toBe("hi");
    expect(r.ok && r.sessionId).toBe("s");
    expect(r.ok && r.model).toBe("claude-sonnet-5");
    expect(r.ok && r.costUsd).toBe(0.01);
  });
  it("flags invalid structured output", () => {
    expect(classifyOutcome({ ...base, result: success({ reply: "" }) as never })).toMatchObject({ ok: false, kind: "INVALID_OUTPUT" });
    expect(classifyOutcome({ ...base, result: { ...success(good), subtype: "error_max_structured_output_retries", errors: [] } as never })).toMatchObject({ ok: false, kind: "INVALID_OUTPUT" });
  });
  it("maps a rejected rate limit to LIMITED with the reset time", () => {
    const r = classifyOutcome({ ...base, rejectedLimit: { resetsAt: 1_800_000_000 }, result: success(good) as never });
    expect(r).toMatchObject({ ok: false, kind: "LIMITED" });
    expect(!r.ok && r.retryAfter?.getTime()).toBe(1_800_000_000 * 1000);
  });
  it("maps assistant errors", () => {
    expect(classifyOutcome({ ...base, assistantError: "rate_limit" })).toMatchObject({ ok: false, kind: "LIMITED" });
    expect(classifyOutcome({ ...base, assistantError: "authentication_failed" })).toMatchObject({ ok: false, kind: "AUTH" });
    expect(classifyOutcome({ ...base, assistantError: "billing_error" })).toMatchObject({ ok: false, kind: "AUTH" });
    expect(classifyOutcome({ ...base, assistantError: "overloaded" })).toMatchObject({ ok: false, kind: "TRANSIENT" });
  });
  it("maps error_during_execution by message and api status", () => {
    const err = (errors: string[]) => ({ ...success(good), subtype: "error_during_execution", is_error: true, errors }) as never;
    expect(classifyOutcome({ ...base, result: err(["You've hit your weekly limit · resets 3pm"]) })).toMatchObject({ ok: false, kind: "LIMITED" });
    expect(classifyOutcome({ ...base, result: err(["Not logged in. Run claude login"]) })).toMatchObject({ ok: false, kind: "AUTH" });
    expect(classifyOutcome({ ...base, result: err(["socket hang up"]) })).toMatchObject({ ok: false, kind: "TRANSIENT" });
    expect(classifyOutcome({ ...base, result: success(good, { is_error: true, api_error_status: 429 }) as never })).toMatchObject({ ok: false, kind: "LIMITED" });
    expect(classifyOutcome({ ...base, result: success(good, { is_error: true, api_error_status: 401 }) as never })).toMatchObject({ ok: false, kind: "AUTH" });
  });
  it("treats abort and thrown errors as transient, and no result as transient", () => {
    expect(classifyOutcome({ ...base, aborted: true })).toMatchObject({ ok: false, kind: "TRANSIENT" });
    expect(classifyOutcome({ ...base, thrown: "spawn failed" })).toMatchObject({ ok: false, kind: "TRANSIENT" });
    expect(classifyOutcome(base)).toMatchObject({ ok: false, kind: "TRANSIENT" });
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/server/agent/claude-code-backend.ts`**

```ts
import { mkdirSync } from "node:fs";
import { query, type EffortLevel, type SDKResultMessage } from "@anthropic-ai/claude-agent-sdk";
import { REPLY_JSON_SCHEMA, parseStructuredReply } from "./schema";
import type { BackendErrorKind, LLMBackend, TurnInput, TurnResult } from "./types";
import { log } from "../log";

export interface StreamObservation {
  rejectedLimit: { resetsAt?: number } | null;
  assistantError: string | null;
  result: SDKResultMessage | null;
  aborted: boolean;
  thrown: string | null;
  latencyMs: number;
}

const BUILTIN_TOOLS = ["Bash", "Read", "Write", "Edit", "MultiEdit", "Glob", "Grep", "WebFetch", "WebSearch", "Task", "NotebookEdit", "TodoWrite", "KillShell", "BashOutput"];
const AUTH_ERRORS = new Set(["authentication_failed", "oauth_org_not_allowed", "account_on_hold", "verification_required", "billing_error", "cloud_credential_error"]);

function resetDate(resetsAt?: number): Date | null {
  if (!resetsAt) return null;
  return new Date(resetsAt > 1e12 ? resetsAt : resetsAt * 1000);
}

/** Pure mapping from what the SDK stream showed to a TurnResult (spec §5.4 table). */
export function classifyOutcome(obs: StreamObservation): TurnResult {
  const fail = (kind: BackendErrorKind, message: string, retryAfter: Date | null = null): TurnResult =>
    ({ ok: false, kind, message, retryAfter, latencyMs: obs.latencyMs });

  if (obs.rejectedLimit) return fail("LIMITED", "subscription usage limit reached", resetDate(obs.rejectedLimit.resetsAt));
  if (obs.assistantError) {
    if (obs.assistantError === "rate_limit") return fail("LIMITED", "assistant error: rate_limit");
    if (AUTH_ERRORS.has(obs.assistantError)) return fail("AUTH", `assistant error: ${obs.assistantError}`);
    return fail("TRANSIENT", `assistant error: ${obs.assistantError}`);
  }
  if (obs.thrown) return fail("TRANSIENT", obs.thrown);
  if (obs.aborted) return fail("TRANSIENT", "turn timed out");
  const r = obs.result;
  if (!r) return fail("TRANSIENT", "no result message from the SDK");

  if (r.subtype !== "success") {
    const text = ("errors" in r ? r.errors : []).join(" | ");
    if (r.subtype === "error_max_structured_output_retries" || r.subtype === "error_max_turns") return fail("INVALID_OUTPUT", `${r.subtype}: ${text}`);
    if (/limit|too many requests|rate/i.test(text)) return fail("LIMITED", text);
    if (/not logged in|login|invalid.*token|authentication|unauthorized|401/i.test(text)) return fail("AUTH", text);
    return fail("TRANSIENT", `${r.subtype}: ${text}`);
  }
  if (r.is_error) {
    const status = r.api_error_status ?? 0;
    if (status === 429) return fail("LIMITED", `api status ${status}`);
    if (status === 401 || status === 403) return fail("AUTH", `api status ${status}`);
    return fail("TRANSIENT", `result is_error (status ${status}): ${r.result.slice(0, 200)}`);
  }
  const structured = parseStructuredReply(r.structured_output);
  if (!structured) return fail("INVALID_OUTPUT", "structured output failed validation");
  const modelNames = Object.keys(r.modelUsage ?? {});
  const primary = modelNames.find((m) => !/haiku/i.test(m)) ?? modelNames[0] ?? null;
  return {
    ok: true,
    structured,
    usage: (r.usage as unknown as Record<string, unknown>) ?? null,
    latencyMs: obs.latencyMs,
    sessionId: r.session_id ?? null,
    model: primary,
    costUsd: typeof r.total_cost_usd === "number" ? r.total_cost_usd : null,
  };
}

export class ClaudeCodeBackend implements LLMBackend {
  readonly name = "claude-code";

  constructor(private readonly opts: { model: string; effort: EffortLevel; maxTurns: number; oauthToken?: string; homeDir: string }) {
    mkdirSync(`${opts.homeDir}/config`, { recursive: true });
  }

  async runTurn(input: TurnInput): Promise<TurnResult> {
    const started = Date.now();
    const abort = new AbortController();
    const timer = setTimeout(() => abort.abort(), input.timeoutMs);
    const env: Record<string, string | undefined> = {
      ...process.env,
      HOME: this.opts.homeDir,
      CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR: `${this.opts.homeDir}/config`,
      ANTHROPIC_API_KEY: undefined,
      CLAUDECODE: undefined,
      CLAUDE_CODE_ENTRYPOINT: undefined,
    };
    if (this.opts.oauthToken) env.CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN = this.opts.oauthToken;

    const obs: StreamObservation = { rejectedLimit: null, assistantError: null, result: null, aborted: false, thrown: null, latencyMs: 0 };
    try {
      const q = query({
        prompt: input.prompt,
        options: {
          systemPrompt: input.systemPrompt,
          model: this.opts.model,
          effort: this.opts.effort,
          maxTurns: this.opts.maxTurns,
          mcpServers: input.toolServer ? { arham: input.toolServer } : {},
          allowedTools: input.allowedToolNames,
          disallowedTools: BUILTIN_TOOLS,
          tools: [],
          permissionMode: "bypassPermissions",
          allowDangerouslySkipPermissions: true,
          settingSources: [],
          persistSession: false,
          strictMcpConfig: true,
          outputFormat: { type: "json_schema", schema: REPLY_JSON_SCHEMA },
          cwd: this.opts.homeDir,
          env,
          abortController: abort,
          stderr: (data) => log.debug({ data: data.slice(0, 500) }, "claude stderr"),
        },
      });
      for await (const msg of q) {
        if (msg.type === "rate_limit_event") {
          const info = msg.rate_limit_info;
          if (info.status === "rejected") obs.rejectedLimit = { resetsAt: info.resetsAt };
          else if (info.status === "allowed_warning") log.warn({ info }, "claude usage nearing limit");
        } else if (msg.type === "assistant" && msg.error) {
          obs.assistantError = msg.error;
        } else if (msg.type === "result") {
          obs.result = msg;
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (abort.signal.aborted) obs.aborted = true;
      else obs.thrown = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timer);
    }
    obs.latencyMs = Date.now() - started;
    const outcome = classifyOutcome(obs);
    if (!outcome.ok) log.warn({ kind: outcome.kind, message: outcome.message, latencyMs: outcome.latencyMs }, "claude turn failed");
    return outcome;
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `src/server/agent/fake-backend.ts` and `src/server/agent/index.ts`**

`fake-backend.ts`:

```ts
import type { BackendErrorKind, LLMBackend, StructuredReply, TurnInput, TurnResult } from "./types";

export function okReply(partial: Partial<StructuredReply> & { reply: string }): TurnResult {
  return {
    ok: true,
    structured: { language: "en", intent: "other", location: { city: null, area: null }, needs_human: false, notes: null, ...partial },
    usage: { input_tokens: 10, output_tokens: 5 },
    latencyMs: 1,
    sessionId: "fake-session",
    model: "fake",
    costUsd: 0,
  };
}

export function errorResult(kind: BackendErrorKind, message = kind, retryAfter: Date | null = null): TurnResult {
  return { ok: false, kind, message, retryAfter, latencyMs: 1 };
}

type Step = TurnResult | ((input: TurnInput) => TurnResult | Promise<TurnResult>);

/** Scripted backend. Each call consumes the next step; the last step repeats. */
export class FakeBackend implements LLMBackend {
  readonly name = "fake";
  calls: TurnInput[] = [];
  private script: Step[];
  constructor(script: Step[] = []) {
    this.script = [...script];
  }
  async runTurn(input: TurnInput): Promise<TurnResult> {
    this.calls.push(input);
    const step = this.script.length > 1 ? this.script.shift()! : this.script[0];
    if (!step) {
      const last = input.prompt.split("\n").filter((l) => l.startsWith("[")).pop() ?? "";
      return okReply({ reply: `Fake backend reply to: ${last.slice(0, 80)}` });
    }
    return typeof step === "function" ? step(input) : step;
  }
}
```

`index.ts`:

```ts
import { getConfig } from "../config";
import { ClaudeCodeBackend } from "./claude-code-backend";
import { FakeBackend } from "./fake-backend";
import type { LLMBackend } from "./types";

let override: LLMBackend | null = null;
let cached: LLMBackend | null = null;

export function setBackendForTests(b: LLMBackend | null): void {
  override = b;
}

export function getBackend(): LLMBackend {
  if (override) return override;
  if (cached) return cached;
  const cfg = getConfig();
  cached = cfg.llmBackend === "fake"
    ? new FakeBackend()
    : new ClaudeCodeBackend({ model: cfg.claude.model, effort: cfg.claude.effort, maxTurns: cfg.claude.maxTurns, oauthToken: cfg.claude.oauthToken, homeDir: cfg.claude.homeDir });
  return cached;
}
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from "vitest";
import { resetDb } from "../helpers/db";
import { seedDirectory } from "../helpers/fixtures";
import { ClaudeCodeBackend } from "@/server/agent/claude-code-backend";
import { HandleRegistry } from "@/server/agent/handles";
import { createArhamTools, type ToolContext } from "@/server/agent/tools";
import { buildSystemPrompt, buildTurnPrompt, formatIst } from "@/server/agent/prompt";
import { getCoverageSummary, resetCoverageCache } from "@/server/directory/coverage";

const run = process.env.RUN_CLAUDE_SMOKE === "1" ? describe : describe.skip;

run("ClaudeCodeBackend smoke (spends Max usage)", () => {
  beforeEach(async () => { await resetDb(); resetCoverageCache(); await seedDirectory(); });

  it("answers a Ghatkopar request with a token and Hinglish", async () => {
    const ctx: ToolContext = { conversationId: "smoke", language: "hi", registry: new HandleRegistry("smoke"), calls: [], lastAmbulanceCandidates: null };
    const { server, allowedToolNames } = createArhamTools(ctx);
    const backend = new ClaudeCodeBackend({ model: "claude-sonnet-5", effort: "medium", maxTurns: 6, oauthToken: process.env.CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN, homeDir: "/tmp/arham-claude-home-test" });
    const res = await backend.runTurn({
      conversationId: "smoke",
      systemPrompt: buildSystemPrompt({ coverage: await getCoverageSummary(), websiteUrl: "https://arhamalwayscare.org" }),
      prompt: buildTurnPrompt({ nowIst: formatIst(new Date()), language: null, knownLocation: null, handlesShared: [], welcomeJustSent: true, correction: null,
        transcript: "--- NEW MESSAGES (answer these) ---\n[10:03] Reporter: ghatkopar station ke paas kutta ghayal hai please jaldi" }),
      toolServer: server, allowedToolNames, timeoutMs: 60_000,
    });
    expect(res.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!res.ok) return;
    expect(res.structured.intent).toBe("ambulance");
    expect(res.structured.reply).toMatch(/\{\{AMB:amb_[0-9a-f]{8}\}\}/);
    expect(res.structured.reply).not.toMatch(/\d{7,}/);
    expect(ctx.calls.some((c) => c.name === "find_ambulances")).toBe(true);
  }, 90_000);
});
```

```ts
    expect(m.contentTemplate).toMatch(/\{\{AMB:amb_[0-9a-f]{8}\}\}/);
    expect(m.turnId).toBe(r.turnId);
    const t = await lastTurn(c.id);
    expect(t.status).toBe("ok");
    expect((t.toolCalls as { name: string }[])[0].name).toBe("find_ambulances");
    expect(Object.keys(t.handles as object)).toHaveLength(1);
    const [row] = await getDb().select().from(conversations).where(eq(conversations.id, c.id));
    expect(row.language).toBe("hi");
    expect((await getWorkerState()).lastOkTurnAt).not.toBeNull();
    expect(backend.calls[0].prompt).toContain("NEW MESSAGES");
    expect(backend.calls[0].systemPrompt).toContain("- Mumbai:");
  });

  it("resolves a handle issued in an earlier turn", async () => {
    const c = await conv();
    const h = makeHandle("ambulance", c.id, ids.ghatkopar);
    const old = await createTurn({ conversationId: c.id, status: "ok" });
    await finishTurn(old.id, { handles: { [h]: { kind: "ambulance", id: ids.ghatkopar } } });
    await storeReporterMessage(c.id, { kind: "text", content: "number phir se bhejo" });
    await runTurn(c.id, { backend: new FakeBackend([okReply({ reply: `Number: {{AMB:${h}}}`, intent: "ambulance" })]) });
    const [m] = await outbound(c.id);
    expect(m.content).toBe("Number: +91 76620 05404");
  });

  it("strips digits the model typed after one correction attempt", async () => {
    const c = await conv();
    await storeReporterMessage(c.id, { kind: "text", content: "surat" });
    const backend = new FakeBackend([okReply({ reply: "Call 9876543210 now.", intent: "other" }), okReply({ reply: "Call 9876543210 now.", intent: "other" })]);
    await runTurn(c.id, { backend });
    expect(backend.calls).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(backend.calls[1].prompt).toContain("SYSTEM CORRECTION");
    const [m] = await outbound(c.id);
    expect(m.content).toBe("Call now.");
    expect((await lastTurn(c.id)).scrubbed).toBe(true);
    const ev = await getDb().select().from(events).where(eq(events.kind, "scrub_strip"));
    expect(ev).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it("accepts a corrected reply on the second attempt", async () => {
    const c = await conv();
    await storeReporterMessage(c.id, { kind: "text", content: "surat" });
    const backend = new FakeBackend([okReply({ reply: "Call 9876543210 now.", intent: "other" }), okReply({ reply: "Please call our helpline {{PHONE:helpline}}.", intent: "other" })]);
    await runTurn(c.id, { backend });
    const [m] = await outbound(c.id);
    expect(m.content).toBe("Please call our helpline +91 99999 00000.");
    expect((await lastTurn(c.id)).scrubbed).toBe(false);
  });

  it("replaces dispatch language", async () => {
    const c = await conv();
    await storeReporterMessage(c.id, { kind: "text", content: "surat" });
    const backend = new FakeBackend([okReply({ reply: "We are sending the ambulance now. Stay calm.", intent: "ambulance" }), okReply({ reply: "We are sending the ambulance now. Stay calm.", intent: "ambulance" })]);
    await runTurn(c.id, { backend });
    const [m] = await outbound(c.id);
    expect(m.content).toBe("Please call them directly and they will guide you. Stay calm.");
  });

  it("appends a card when the model forgot the token and one unit matched", async () => {
    const c = await conv();
    await storeReporterMessage(c.id, { kind: "text", content: "ghatkopar" });
    const backend = new FakeBackend([async (input: TurnInput) => {
      await findAmbulancesImpl(input.toolContext!, { city: "Mumbai", area: "Ghatkopar", query: "ghatkopar" });
      return okReply({ reply: "Please call the Ghatkopar ambulance.", intent: "ambulance" });
    }]);
    await runTurn(c.id, { backend });
    const [m] = await outbound(c.id);
    expect(m.content).toContain("Arham Animal Ambulance, Ghatkopar\n+91 76620 05404");
  });

  it("pauses Claude and falls back on LIMITED, then skips the backend while paused", async () => {
    const c = await conv();
    await storeReporterMessage(c.id, { kind: "text", content: "surat" });
    const backend = new FakeBackend([errorResult("LIMITED", "limit", new Date(Date.now() + 60_000)), ghatkoparStep]);
    const r = await runTurn(c.id, { backend });
    expect(r.kind).toBe("fallback");
    expect(await isClaudePaused()).toBe(true);
    const [m] = await outbound(c.id);
    expect(m.content).toContain("Arham Animal Ambulance, Surat");
    await storeReporterMessage(c.id, { kind: "text", content: "rajkot" });
    await runTurn(c.id, { backend });
    expect(backend.calls).toHaveLength(1);
    const out = await outbound(c.id);
    expect(out[1].content).toContain("2 ambulances are available in Rajkot");
  });

  it("records an auth event and falls back on AUTH", async () => {
    const c = await conv();
    await storeReporterMessage(c.id, { kind: "text", content: "surat" });
    await runTurn(c.id, { backend: new FakeBackend([errorResult("AUTH", "not logged in")]) });
    expect((await getDb().select().from(events).where(eq(events.kind, "claude_auth_error"))).length).toBe(1);
    expect((await lastTurn(c.id)).status).toBe("fallback");
  });

  it("retries once on INVALID_OUTPUT and TRANSIENT, then falls back", async () => {
    const c = await conv();
    await storeReporterMessage(c.id, { kind: "text", content: "surat" });
    const backend = new FakeBackend([errorResult("INVALID_OUTPUT"), errorResult("TRANSIENT"), errorResult("TRANSIENT")]);
    const r = await runTurn(c.id, { backend });
    expect(r.kind).toBe("fallback");
    expect(backend.calls.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2);
    expect(backend.calls.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(3);
    const c2 = await conv();
    await storeReporterMessage(c2.id, { kind: "text", content: "hello again" });
    const b2 = new FakeBackend([errorResult("TRANSIENT"), okReply({ reply: "Namaste! Kaise madad karun?", language: "hi", intent: "smalltalk" })]);
    expect((await runTurn(c2.id, { backend: b2 })).kind).toBe("ok");
  });

  it("flags the conversation when the model asks for a human", async () => {
    const c = await conv();
    await storeReporterMessage(c.id, { kind: "text", content: "I want to talk to a person" });
    await runTurn(c.id, { backend: new FakeBackend([okReply({ reply: "Sure, call {{PHONE:helpline}}.", intent: "other", needs_human: true, notes: "asked for human" })]) });
    const [row] = await getDb().select().from(conversations).where(eq(conversations.id, c.id));
    expect(row.flagged).toBe(true);
    expect(row.flagReason).toBe("needs_human");
    expect((await lastTurn(c.id)).structuredOutput).toMatchObject({ notes: "asked for human" });
  });
});
```
