- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/server/worker/claude-turn.ts`**

```ts
import type { Conversation, Message } from "../db/schema";
import { desc, eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { getDb } from "../db/client";
import { turns } from "../db/schema";
import { getConfig } from "../config";
import { log } from "../log";
import type { Language } from "../types";
import type { LLMBackend, StructuredReply, TurnResult } from "../agent/types";
import { HandleRegistry } from "../agent/handles";
import { createArhamTools, type ToolContext } from "../agent/tools";
import { buildSystemPrompt, buildTurnPrompt, formatIst } from "../agent/prompt";
import { renderTranscript } from "../agent/transcript";
import { renderReply } from "../agent/render";
import { findDigitRuns, foreignDigitRuns, hasDispatchLanguage, replaceDispatchSentences, stripRuns } from "../agent/scrub";
import { getCoverageSummary } from "../directory/coverage";
import { getAmbulancesByIds, getClinicsByIds } from "../directory/repo";
import { getStaticPhones } from "../content/static";
import { callDirectlyLine, DISPATCH_CORRECTION_NOTE, TOKEN_CORRECTION_NOTE } from "../messages/system-replies";
import { recordEvent } from "../inbound/store";
import { loadHistory } from "./gather";
import { deliverText } from "./deliver";
import { createTurn, finishTurn, loadConversationHandles, markAnswered, updateConversation } from "./persist";
import { isClaudePaused, pauseClaude, recordOkTurn } from "./pause";
import { runFallbackTurn } from "./fallback";

const PAUSE_FALLBACK_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000;

async function lastKnownLocation(conversationId: string): Promise<{ city: string | null; area: string | null } | null> {
  const [row] = await getDb()
    .select({ so: turns.structuredOutput })
    .from(turns)
    .where(eq(turns.conversationId, conversationId))
    .orderBy(desc(turns.createdAt))
    .limit(5);
  const loc = (row?.so as { location?: { city: string | null; area: string | null } } | null)?.location;
  return loc && (loc.city || loc.area) ? loc : null;
}

export async function runClaudeTurn(input: {
  conversation: Conversation;
  pending: Message[];
  lang: Language;
  welcomeJustSent: boolean;
  backend: LLMBackend;
  now: () => Date;
}): Promise<{ turnId: string; outcome: "ok" | "fallback" }> {
  const { conversation, pending, backend, now } = input;
  const cfg = getConfig();

  if (await isClaudePaused(now())) {
    const r = await runFallbackTurn({ conversation, pending, reason: "claude_paused" });
    return { turnId: r.turn.id, outcome: "fallback" };
  }

  const turn = await createTurn({ conversationId: conversation.id, status: "failed", model: cfg.claude.model, effort: cfg.claude.effort });
  const history = await loadHistory(conversation.id, 30);
  const registry = new HandleRegistry(conversation.id, await loadConversationHandles(conversation.id));
  const toolContext: ToolContext = { conversationId: conversation.id, language: input.lang, registry, calls: [], lastAmbulanceCandidates: null };
  const { server, allowedToolNames } = createArhamTools(toolContext);
  const systemPrompt = buildSystemPrompt({ coverage: await getCoverageSummary(), websiteUrl: cfg.websiteUrl });
  const knownLocation = await lastKnownLocation(conversation.id);
  const priorNumbers = history.filter((m) => m.role === "assistant").flatMap((m) => findDigitRuns(m.content));

  const promptFor = (opts: { transcriptLimit: number; correction: string | null }) =>
    buildTurnPrompt({
      nowIst: formatIst(now()),
      language: conversation.language,
      knownLocation,
      handlesShared: registry.handles(),
      welcomeJustSent: input.welcomeJustSent,
      transcript: renderTranscript(history.slice(-opts.transcriptLimit), pending),
      correction: opts.correction,
    });

  const call = (prompt: string): Promise<TurnResult> =>
    backend.runTurn({ conversationId: conversation.id, systemPrompt, prompt, toolServer: server, allowedToolNames, timeoutMs: cfg.claude.turnTimeoutMs, toolContext });

  const fail = async (reason: string, message: string) => {
    await finishTurn(turn.id, { status: "failed", error: `${reason}: ${message}`, toolCalls: toolContext.calls, handles: registry.toJSON() });
    const r = await runFallbackTurn({ conversation, pending, reason });
    return { turnId: r.turn.id, outcome: "fallback" as const };
  };

  // Attempt 1, with one retry for INVALID_OUTPUT (shorter transcript) or TRANSIENT.
  let res = await call(promptFor({ transcriptLimit: 30, correction: null }));
  if (!res.ok && (res.kind === "INVALID_OUTPUT" || res.kind === "TRANSIENT")) {
    res = await call(promptFor({ transcriptLimit: res.kind === "INVALID_OUTPUT" ? 10 : 30, correction: null }));
  }
  if (!res.ok) {
    if (res.kind === "LIMITED") {
      await pauseClaude(res.retryAfter ?? new Date(now().getTime() + PAUSE_FALLBACK_MS), "LIMITED");
      await recordEvent("claude_limited", { message: res.message, retryAfter: res.retryAfter }, conversation.id);
      return fail("claude_limited", res.message);
    }
    if (res.kind === "AUTH") {
      await pauseClaude(new Date(now().getTime() + PAUSE_FALLBACK_MS), "AUTH");
      await recordEvent("claude_auth_error", { message: res.message }, conversation.id);
      return fail("claude_auth", res.message);
    }
    return fail(res.kind === "INVALID_OUTPUT" ? "claude_invalid_output" : "claude_transient", res.message);
  }

  const render = async (structured: StructuredReply) =>
    renderReply({
      replyTemplate: structured.reply,
      lang: structured.language,
      intent: structured.intent,
      registry,
      lookups: {
        ambulance: async (id) => (await getAmbulancesByIds([id])).get(id),
        clinic: async (id) => (await getClinicsByIds([id])).get(id),
      },
      helplinePhone: cfg.helplinePhone,
      staticPhones: getStaticPhones(),
      lastAmbulanceCandidates: toolContext.lastAmbulanceCandidates,
    });

  let structured = res.structured;
  let rendered = await render(structured);
  let scrubbed = false;
  let text = rendered.text;

  // Digit guard: one corrective retry, then strip.
  let foreign = foreignDigitRuns(text, [...rendered.inserted, ...priorNumbers]);
  if (foreign.length > 0) {
    const retry = await call(promptFor({ transcriptLimit: 30, correction: TOKEN_CORRECTION_NOTE }));
    if (retry.ok) {
      structured = retry.structured;
      rendered = await render(structured);
      text = rendered.text;
      foreign = foreignDigitRuns(text, [...rendered.inserted, ...priorNumbers]);
    }
    if (foreign.length > 0) {
      text = stripRuns(text, foreign);
      scrubbed = true;
      await recordEvent("scrub_strip", { runs: foreign.length }, conversation.id);
      log.warn({ conversationId: conversation.id, runs: foreign.length }, "stripped model-typed digits");
    }
  }

  // Dispatch guard: one corrective retry, then sentence replacement.
  if (hasDispatchLanguage(text)) {
    const retry = await call(promptFor({ transcriptLimit: 30, correction: DISPATCH_CORRECTION_NOTE }));
    if (retry.ok) {
      structured = retry.structured;
      rendered = await render(structured);
      text = scrubbed ? stripRuns(rendered.text, foreignDigitRuns(rendered.text, [...rendered.inserted, ...priorNumbers])) : rendered.text;
    }
    if (hasDispatchLanguage(text)) {
      text = replaceDispatchSentences(text, callDirectlyLine(structured.language));
      await recordEvent("dispatch_language_strip", {}, conversation.id);
    }
  }

  if (rendered.unknownHandles.length > 0) await recordEvent("unknown_handle", { handles: rendered.unknownHandles }, conversation.id);

  await deliverText(conversation, { text, template: rendered.template, origin: "agent", turnId: turn.id });
  await markAnswered(pending.map((m) => m.id), turn.id);
  await finishTurn(turn.id, {
    status: "ok",
    structuredOutput: structured,
    toolCalls: toolContext.calls,
    handles: registry.toJSON(),
    usage: res.usage,
    latencyMs: res.latencyMs,
    sessionId: res.sessionId,
    model: res.model ?? cfg.claude.model,
    scrubbed,
  });
  await updateConversation(conversation.id, {
    language: structured.language,
    flagged: structured.needs_human ? true : conversation.flagged,
    flagReason: structured.needs_human ? "needs_human" : conversation.flagReason,
  });
  await recordOkTurn();
  return { turnId: turn.id, outcome: "ok" };
}
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import { signAdminSession, verifyAdminSession, checkPassword, loginLimiter, isAdminRequest, ADMIN_COOKIE } from "@/server/admin/auth";

describe("admin session", () => {
  it("round-trips a signed session and rejects expiry and tampering", () => {
    const token = signAdminSession(Date.now() + 60_000);
    expect(verifyAdminSession(token)).toBe(true);
    expect(verifyAdminSession(token, Date.now() + 120_000)).toBe(false);
    expect(verifyAdminSession(token.slice(0, -2) + "zz")).toBe(false);
    expect(verifyAdminSession("garbage")).toBe(false);
    expect(verifyAdminSession(null)).toBe(false);
  });
  it("checks the password in constant time", () => {
    expect(checkPassword("test-admin")).toBe(true);
    expect(checkPassword("nope")).toBe(false);
    expect(checkPassword("")).toBe(false);
  });
  it("rate limits five failures per 15 minutes per ip", () => {
    loginLimiter.reset("1.2.3.4");
    const t0 = 1_000_000;
    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) { expect(loginLimiter.allow("1.2.3.4", t0)).toBe(true); loginLimiter.recordFailure("1.2.3.4", t0); }
    expect(loginLimiter.allow("1.2.3.4", t0 + 1000)).toBe(false);
    expect(loginLimiter.allow("1.2.3.4", t0 + 16 * 60_000)).toBe(true);
  });
  it("reads the cookie from a Request", () => {
    const token = signAdminSession(Date.now() + 60_000);
    expect(isAdminRequest(new Request("http://x/", { headers: { cookie: `${ADMIN_COOKIE}=${token}; other=1` } }))).toBe(true);
    expect(isAdminRequest(new Request("http://x/"))).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

```ts
  });

});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/server/admin/auth.ts`**

```ts
import { createHmac, timingSafeEqual } from "node:crypto";
import { getConfig } from "../config";

export const ADMIN_COOKIE = "arham_admin";
const MAX_FAILURES = 5;
const WINDOW_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000;

function hmac(payload: string, secret: string): string {
  return createHmac("sha256", secret).update(payload).digest("hex");
}

export function signAdminSession(expiresAtMs: number): string {
  const exp = String(Math.floor(expiresAtMs));
  return `${exp}.${hmac(exp, getConfig().admin.sessionSecret)}`;
}

export function verifyAdminSession(value: string | null | undefined, nowMs: number = Date.now()): boolean {
  if (!value) return false;
  const dot = value.indexOf(".");
  if (dot <= 0) return false;
  const exp = value.slice(0, dot);
  const sig = value.slice(dot + 1);
  if (!/^\d+$/.test(exp) || Number(exp) < nowMs) return false;
  const expected = hmac(exp, getConfig().admin.sessionSecret);
  const a = Buffer.from(sig);
  const b = Buffer.from(expected);
  return a.length === b.length && timingSafeEqual(a, b);
}

export function checkPassword(candidate: string): boolean {
  const expected = getConfig().admin.password;
  const a = Buffer.from(candidate ?? "");
  const b = Buffer.from(expected);
  return a.length === b.length && a.length > 0 && timingSafeEqual(a, b);
}

const failures = new Map<string, number[]>();
export const loginLimiter = {
  allow(ip: string, nowMs: number = Date.now()): boolean {
    const recent = (failures.get(ip) ?? []).filter((t) => nowMs - t < WINDOW_MS);
    failures.set(ip, recent);
    return recent.length < MAX_FAILURES;
  },
  recordFailure(ip: string, nowMs: number = Date.now()): void {
    failures.set(ip, [...(failures.get(ip) ?? []), nowMs]);
  },
  reset(ip: string): void {
    failures.delete(ip);
  },
};

export function cookieHeaderValue(token: string, maxAgeSec: number): string {
  const secure = getConfig().isProduction ? "; Secure" : "";
  return `${ADMIN_COOKIE}=${token}; Path=/; HttpOnly; SameSite=Lax; Max-Age=${maxAgeSec}${secure}`;
}

function cookieFromHeader(header: string | null): string | null {
  if (!header) return null;
  for (const part of header.split(";")) {
    const [k, ...rest] = part.trim().split("=");
    if (k === ADMIN_COOKIE) return rest.join("=");
  }
  return null;
}

export function isAdminRequest(request: Request): boolean {
  return verifyAdminSession(cookieFromHeader(request.headers.get("cookie")));
}

export function unauthorized(): Response {
  return Response.json({ error: "unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
}

/** Server components: redirect to the login page when the cookie is missing or invalid. */
export async function requireAdmin(): Promise<void> {
  const { cookies } = await import("next/headers");
  const { redirect } = await import("next/navigation");
  const store = await cookies();
  if (!verifyAdminSession(store.get(ADMIN_COOKIE)?.value)) redirect("/admin/login");
}
```

```ts
import { checkPassword, cookieHeaderValue, loginLimiter, signAdminSession } from "@/server/admin/auth";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
const THIRTY_DAYS_SEC = 30 * 24 * 60 * 60;

function clientIp(request: Request): string {
  return request.headers.get("x-forwarded-for")?.split(",")[0].trim() || "unknown";
}

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  const ip = clientIp(request);
  if (!loginLimiter.allow(ip)) return Response.json({ error: "too many attempts, try later" }, { status: 429 });
  const body = (await request.json().catch(() => ({}))) as { password?: string };
  if (!checkPassword(body.password ?? "")) {
    loginLimiter.recordFailure(ip);
    return Response.json({ error: "wrong password" }, { status: 401 });
  }
  loginLimiter.reset(ip);
  const token = signAdminSession(Date.now() + THIRTY_DAYS_SEC * 1000);
  return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true }), {
    status: 200,
    headers: { "content-type": "application/json", "set-cookie": cookieHeaderValue(token, THIRTY_DAYS_SEC) },
  });
}

export async function DELETE() {
  return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true }), {
    status: 200,
    headers: { "content-type": "application/json", "set-cookie": cookieHeaderValue("", 0) },
  });
}
```

```tsx
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";

export default function LoginPage() {
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  async function submit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    const res = await fetch("/api/admin/login", { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify({ password }) });
    setBusy(false);
    if (res.ok) {
      window.location.href = "/admin";
      return;
    }
    setError(res.status === 429 ? "Too many attempts. Try again in 15 minutes." : "Wrong password.");
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-[#0f0f0f] text-white">
      <form onSubmit={submit} className="w-80 space-y-4 rounded-xl border border-white/10 bg-[#141414] p-6">
        <h1 className="text-lg font-semibold">Arham Helpline Admin</h1>
        <input
          type="password"
          value={password}
          onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Password"
          autoFocus
          className="w-full rounded-md border border-white/10 bg-[#0f0f0f] px-3 py-2 text-sm focus:border-emerald-500/50 focus:outline-none"
        />
        {error && <p className="text-xs text-red-400">{error}</p>}
        <button disabled={busy || !password} className="w-full rounded-md bg-emerald-500 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-black disabled:opacity-40">
          {busy ? "…" : "Sign in"}
        </button>
      </form>
    </main>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/server/admin/queries.ts`**

```ts
import { and, asc, desc, eq, ilike, or, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { getDb } from "../db/client";
import { conversations, messages, turns, type Conversation, type Message, type Turn } from "../db/schema";

export type ConversationFilter = "all" | "flagged" | "human" | "test";
export type ConversationSummary = Conversation & { lastMessage: string | null; lastMessageAt: Date | null; messageCount: number };

export async function listConversations(opts: { filter: ConversationFilter; search?: string; limit?: number }): Promise<ConversationSummary[]> {
  const db = getDb();
  const conds = [];
  if (opts.filter === "test") conds.push(eq(conversations.isTest, true));
  else conds.push(eq(conversations.isTest, false));
  if (opts.filter === "flagged") conds.push(eq(conversations.flagged, true));
  if (opts.filter === "human") conds.push(eq(conversations.mode, "human"));
  if (opts.search && opts.search.trim()) {
    const q = `%${opts.search.trim()}%`;
    conds.push(or(ilike(conversations.phone, q), ilike(conversations.name, q))!);
  }
  const rows = await db
    .select({
      conversation: conversations,
      lastMessage: sql<string | null>`(select m.content from messages m where m.conversation_id = ${conversations.id} order by m.created_at desc limit 1)`,
      lastMessageAt: sql<Date | null>`(select m.created_at from messages m where m.conversation_id = ${conversations.id} order by m.created_at desc limit 1)`,
      messageCount: sql<number>`(select count(*)::int from messages m where m.conversation_id = ${conversations.id})`,
    })
    .from(conversations)
    .where(and(...conds))
    .orderBy(desc(conversations.updatedAt))
    .limit(opts.limit ?? 100);
  return rows.map((r) => ({ ...r.conversation, lastMessage: r.lastMessage, lastMessageAt: r.lastMessageAt ? new Date(r.lastMessageAt) : null, messageCount: Number(r.messageCount) }));
}

export async function getConversationDetail(id: string): Promise<{ conversation: Conversation; messages: Message[]; turns: Turn[] } | null> {
  const db = getDb();
  const [conversation] = await db.select().from(conversations).where(eq(conversations.id, id));
  if (!conversation) return null;
  const [msgs, ts] = await Promise.all([
    db.select().from(messages).where(eq(messages.conversationId, id)).orderBy(asc(messages.createdAt)),
    db.select().from(turns).where(eq(turns.conversationId, id)).orderBy(asc(turns.createdAt)),
  ]);
  return { conversation, messages: msgs, turns: ts };
}
```

`src/app/api/admin/conversations/route.ts`:

```ts
import { isAdminRequest, unauthorized } from "@/server/admin/auth";
import { listConversations, type ConversationFilter } from "@/server/admin/queries";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export async function GET(request: Request) {
  if (!isAdminRequest(request)) return unauthorized();
  const url = new URL(request.url);
  const filter = (["all", "flagged", "human", "test"].includes(url.searchParams.get("filter") ?? "") ? url.searchParams.get("filter") : "all") as ConversationFilter;
  const rows = await listConversations({ filter, search: url.searchParams.get("q") ?? undefined });
  return Response.json(rows);
}
```

`src/app/api/admin/conversations/[id]/route.ts`:

```ts
import { isAdminRequest, unauthorized } from "@/server/admin/auth";
import { getConversationDetail } from "@/server/admin/queries";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

const UUID = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;

export async function GET(request: Request, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  if (!isAdminRequest(request)) return unauthorized();
  const { id } = await params;
  if (!UUID.test(id)) return Response.json({ error: "not found" }, { status: 404 });
  const detail = await getConversationDetail(id);
  if (!detail) return Response.json({ error: "not found" }, { status: 404 });
  return Response.json(detail);
}
```

```tsx
"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";

type Row = {
  id: string; phone: string; name: string | null; language: string | null; mode: "agent" | "human"; flagged: boolean;
  flagReason: string | null; isTest: boolean; updatedAt: string; lastMessage: string | null; messageCount: number;
};
const FILTERS = ["all", "flagged", "human", "test"] as const;

export function ConversationList() {
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<(typeof FILTERS)[number]>("all");
  const [q, setQ] = useState("");
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<Row[]>([]);

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/conversations?filter=${filter}&q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}`, { cache: "no-store" });
    if (res.status === 401) { window.location.href = "/admin/login"; return; }
    if (res.ok) setRows(await res.json());
  }, [filter, q]);

  useEffect(() => { load(); const t = setInterval(load, 5000); return () => clearInterval(t); }, [load]);

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-[#0f0f0f] text-white">
      <header className="flex items-center gap-3 border-b border-white/10 px-6 py-4">
        <h1 className="text-sm font-semibold">Arham Helpline Admin</h1>
        <nav className="ml-4 flex gap-1 text-xs">
          {FILTERS.map((f) => (
            <button key={f} onClick={() => setFilter(f)} className={`rounded px-2 py-1 ${filter === f ? "bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300" : "text-white/50 hover:text-white"}`}>{f}</button>
          ))}
        </nav>
        <input value={q} onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)} placeholder="Search phone or name" className="ml-auto w-56 rounded border border-white/10 bg-[#141414] px-2 py-1 text-xs" />
        <Link href="/test-chat" className="text-xs text-emerald-400">Test chat →</Link>
      </header>
      <ul className="divide-y divide-white/5">
        {rows.length === 0 && <li className="px-6 py-8 text-xs text-white/40">No conversations.</li>}
        {rows.map((r) => (
          <li key={r.id}>
            <Link href={`/admin/${r.id}`} className="flex items-center gap-4 px-6 py-3 hover:bg-white/[0.03]">
              <div className="w-44 truncate text-sm">{r.name ?? r.phone}<div className="text-[11px] text-white/40">{r.phone}</div></div>
              <div className="flex-1 truncate text-xs text-white/60">{r.lastMessage ?? "—"}</div>
              <div className="flex gap-1 text-[10px] uppercase">
                {r.flagged && <span className="rounded bg-red-500/20 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-red-300">{r.flagReason ?? "flagged"}</span>}
                {r.mode === "human" && <span className="rounded bg-amber-500/20 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-amber-300">human</span>}
                {r.language && <span className="rounded bg-white/10 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-white/60">{r.language}</span>}
              </div>
              <div className="w-24 text-right text-[11px] text-white/40">{r.messageCount} msgs<br />{new Date(r.updatedAt).toLocaleTimeString("en-IN", { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" })}</div>
            </Link>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </main>
  );
}
```

```tsx
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";

export type TurnRow = {
  id: string; status: string; model: string | null; effort: string | null; structuredOutput: unknown; toolCalls: unknown;
  usage: unknown; latencyMs: number | null; sessionId: string | null; error: string | null; scrubbed: boolean; createdAt: string;
};

export function TurnDetails({ turn }: { turn: TurnRow }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const so = (turn.structuredOutput ?? {}) as { intent?: string; language?: string; needs_human?: boolean; notes?: string | null; location?: { city: string | null; area: string | null } };
  return (
    <div className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-white/50">
      <button onClick={() => setOpen(!open)} className="hover:text-white">
        {open ? "▾" : "▸"} turn {turn.status}{turn.model ? ` · ${turn.model}` : ""}{turn.latencyMs != null ? ` · ${turn.latencyMs} ms` : ""}{turn.scrubbed ? " · scrubbed" : ""}{so.intent ? ` · ${so.intent}` : ""}{so.needs_human ? " · needs human" : ""}
      </button>
      {open && (
        <div className="mt-1 space-y-1 rounded border border-white/10 bg-black/30 p-2">
          {so.notes && <div>notes: {so.notes}</div>}
          {so.location && <div>location: {[so.location.area, so.location.city].filter(Boolean).join(", ") || "unknown"}</div>}
          {turn.error && <div className="text-red-300">error: {turn.error}</div>}
          <div>tool calls:</div>
          <pre className="max-h-64 overflow-auto whitespace-pre-wrap text-[10px]">{JSON.stringify(turn.toolCalls ?? [], null, 1)}</pre>
          <div>usage:</div>
          <pre className="max-h-32 overflow-auto whitespace-pre-wrap text-[10px]">{JSON.stringify(turn.usage ?? {}, null, 1)}</pre>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { TurnDetails, type TurnRow } from "./turn-details";

type Msg = { id: string; role: "user" | "assistant"; origin: string; kind: string; content: string; contentTemplate: string | null; deliveryStatus: string | null; error: string | null; turnId: string | null; createdAt: string; placeLabel: string | null; mediaUrl: string | null };
type Detail = { conversation: { id: string; phone: string; name: string | null; language: string | null; mode: string; flagged: boolean; flagReason: string | null; isTest: boolean }; messages: Msg[]; turns: TurnRow[] };

export function Transcript({ id, apiBase = "/api/admin/conversations", pollMs = 3000 }: { id: string; apiBase?: string; pollMs?: number }) {
  const [d, setD] = useState<Detail | null>(null);
  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    const res = await fetch(`${apiBase}/${id}`, { cache: "no-store" });
    if (res.status === 401) { window.location.href = "/admin/login"; return; }
    if (res.ok) setD(await res.json());
  }, [apiBase, id]);
  useEffect(() => { load(); const t = setInterval(load, pollMs); return () => clearInterval(t); }, [load, pollMs]);
  if (!d) return <div className="p-6 text-xs text-white/40">Loading…</div>;
  const turnsById = new Map(d.turns.map((t) => [t.id, t]));
  return (
    <div className="flex h-full flex-col">
      <header className="flex items-center gap-3 border-b border-white/10 px-6 py-3 text-sm">
        <Link href="/admin" className="text-white/40 hover:text-white">←</Link>
        <span className="font-medium">{d.conversation.name ?? d.conversation.phone}</span>
        <span className="text-xs text-white/40">{d.conversation.phone} · {d.conversation.language ?? "?"} · {d.conversation.mode}{d.conversation.flagged ? ` · flagged: ${d.conversation.flagReason}` : ""}</span>
      </header>
      <div className="flex-1 space-y-3 overflow-y-auto px-6 py-4">
        {d.messages.map((m) => {
          const user = m.role === "user";
          const turn = m.turnId ? turnsById.get(m.turnId) : undefined;
          return (
            <div key={m.id} className={`flex ${user ? "justify-start" : "justify-end"}`}>
              <div className={`max-w-[70%] rounded-2xl px-4 py-2 text-sm ${user ? "bg-white/[0.07]" : "bg-emerald-700/80"}`}>
                {m.kind !== "text" && <div className="mb-1 text-[10px] uppercase text-white/50">{m.kind}{m.placeLabel ? ` · ${m.placeLabel}` : ""}{m.mediaUrl ? " · media" : ""}</div>}
                <p className="whitespace-pre-wrap">{m.content}</p>
                <div className="mt-1 text-[10px] text-white/40">
                  {new Date(m.createdAt).toLocaleTimeString("en-IN", { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" })} · {m.origin}{m.deliveryStatus ? ` · ${m.deliveryStatus}` : ""}{m.error ? ` · ${m.error}` : ""}
                </div>
                {turn && <TurnDetails turn={turn} />}
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach } from "vitest";
import { resetDb } from "../helpers/db";
import { stopBoss, getBoss, TURN_QUEUE } from "@/server/queue/boss";
import { signAdminSession, ADMIN_COOKIE } from "@/server/admin/auth";
import { GET as list, POST as create } from "@/app/api/test-chat/conversations/route";
import { GET as detail } from "@/app/api/test-chat/conversations/[id]/route";
import { POST as send } from "@/app/api/test-chat/conversations/[id]/messages/route";

const cookie = () => ({ cookie: `${ADMIN_COOKIE}=${signAdminSession(Date.now() + 60_000)}` });
const req = (url: string, init: RequestInit = {}) => new Request(url, { ...init, headers: { "content-type": "application/json", ...cookie(), ...(init.headers ?? {}) } });

describe("test-chat routes", () => {
  beforeEach(async () => { await stopBoss(); await resetDb(); });
  afterEach(async () => { await stopBoss(); });

  it("requires auth", async () => {
    expect((await list(new Request("http://x/api/test-chat/conversations"))).status).toBe(401);
  });

  it("creates a test conversation, accepts a message and queues a turn", async () => {
    const created = await create(req("http://x/api/test-chat/conversations", { method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify({ language: "hi" }) }));
    expect(created.status).toBe(201);
    const c = await created.json();
    expect(c.phone).toMatch(/^\+91TEST_/);
    expect(c.isTest).toBe(true);
    expect(c.language).toBe("hi");

    const sent = await send(req(`http://x/api/test-chat/conversations/${c.id}/messages`, { method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify({ content: "ghatkopar" }) }), { params: Promise.resolve({ id: c.id }) });
    expect(sent.status).toBe(202);
    const q = await (await getBoss()).getQueue(TURN_QUEUE);
    expect((q?.queuedCount ?? 0) + (q?.deferredCount ?? 0) + (q?.activeCount ?? 0)).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);

    const d = await detail(req(`http://x/api/test-chat/conversations/${c.id}`), { params: Promise.resolve({ id: c.id }) });
    expect((await d.json()).messages).toHaveLength(1);
    const rows = await (await list(req("http://x/api/test-chat/conversations"))).json();
    expect(rows.map((r: { id: string }) => r.id)).toEqual([c.id]);
  });

  it("rejects empty content and non-test ids", async () => {
    const created = await (await create(req("http://x/api/test-chat/conversations", { method: "POST", body: "{}" }))).json();
    const bad = await send(req(`http://x/api/test-chat/conversations/${created.id}/messages`, { method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify({ content: "  " }) }), { params: Promise.resolve({ id: created.id }) });
    expect(bad.status).toBe(400);
    const missing = await detail(req("http://x/api/test-chat/conversations/00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000"), { params: Promise.resolve({ id: "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000" }) });
    expect(missing.status).toBe(404);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
import { isAdminRequest, unauthorized } from "@/server/admin/auth";
import { listConversations } from "@/server/admin/queries";
import { getDb } from "@/server/db/client";
import { conversations } from "@/server/db/schema";
import { LANGUAGES, type Language } from "@/server/types";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export async function GET(request: Request) {
  if (!isAdminRequest(request)) return unauthorized();
  return Response.json(await listConversations({ filter: "test" }));
}

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  if (!isAdminRequest(request)) return unauthorized();
  const body = (await request.json().catch(() => ({}))) as { language?: string };
  const language = (LANGUAGES as string[]).includes(body.language ?? "") ? (body.language as Language) : null;
  const [row] = await getDb()
    .insert(conversations)
    .values({ phone: `+91TEST_${randomBytes(4).toString("hex")}`, isTest: true, language, mode: "agent" })
    .returning();
  return Response.json(row, { status: 201 });
}
```

`src/app/api/test-chat/conversations/[id]/route.ts`:

```ts
import { isAdminRequest, unauthorized } from "@/server/admin/auth";
import { getConversationDetail } from "@/server/admin/queries";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export async function GET(request: Request, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  if (!isAdminRequest(request)) return unauthorized();
  const { id } = await params;
  if (!/^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/i.test(id)) return Response.json({ error: "not found" }, { status: 404 });
  const detail = await getConversationDetail(id);
  if (!detail || !detail.conversation.isTest) return Response.json({ error: "not found" }, { status: 404 });
  return Response.json(detail);
}
```

`src/app/api/test-chat/conversations/[id]/messages/route.ts`:

```ts
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { isAdminRequest, unauthorized } from "@/server/admin/auth";
import { getDb } from "@/server/db/client";
import { conversations } from "@/server/db/schema";
import { storeReporterMessage } from "@/server/inbound/store";
import { enqueueTurn } from "@/server/queue/boss";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export async function POST(request: Request, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  if (!isAdminRequest(request)) return unauthorized();
  const { id } = await params;
  const body = (await request.json().catch(() => ({}))) as { content?: string };
  const content = (body.content ?? "").trim();
  if (!content) return Response.json({ error: "content required" }, { status: 400 });
  const [c] = await getDb().select().from(conversations).where(eq(conversations.id, id));
  if (!c || !c.isTest) return Response.json({ error: "not found" }, { status: 404 });
  const stored = await storeReporterMessage(c.id, { kind: "text", content });
  await getDb().update(conversations).set({ lastInboundAt: new Date(), updatedAt: new Date() }).where(eq(conversations.id, c.id));
  await enqueueTurn(c.id, { immediate: true });
  return Response.json({ status: "queued", messageId: stored.id }, { status: 202 });
}
```

```tsx
"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Transcript } from "../admin/_components/transcript";

type Row = { id: string; phone: string; language: string | null; lastMessage: string | null; updatedAt: string };
const LANGS = [["en", "English"], ["hi", "Hindi"], ["mr", "Marathi"], ["gu", "Gujarati"]] as const;

export function TestChatClient() {
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<Row[]>([]);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [lang, setLang] = useState<string>("en");
  const [input, setInput] = useState("");
  const [sending, setSending] = useState(false);

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    const res = await fetch("/api/test-chat/conversations", { cache: "no-store" });
    if (res.status === 401) { window.location.href = "/admin/login"; return; }
    if (res.ok) setRows(await res.json());
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => { load(); const t = setInterval(load, 4000); return () => clearInterval(t); }, [load]);

  async function create() {
    const res = await fetch("/api/test-chat/conversations", { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify({ language: lang }) });
    if (res.ok) { const c = await res.json(); await load(); setSelected(c.id); }
  }

  async function send() {
    if (!selected || !input.trim() || sending) return;
    setSending(true);
    const content = input.trim();
    setInput("");
    try {
      await fetch(`/api/test-chat/conversations/${selected}/messages`, { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify({ content }) });
    } finally {
      setSending(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex h-screen bg-[#0f0f0f] text-white">
      <aside className="flex w-[280px] flex-col border-r border-white/10 bg-[#141414]">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-white/10 px-4 py-3">
          <div><div className="text-sm font-medium">Test chat</div><div className="text-[11px] text-white/50">Direct to the worker, no WhatsApp</div></div>
          <Link href="/admin" className="text-xs text-white/50 hover:text-white">Admin</Link>
        </div>
        <div className="flex gap-2 border-b border-white/10 px-4 py-2">
          <select value={lang} onChange={(e) => setLang(e.target.value)} className="rounded border border-white/10 bg-[#0f0f0f] px-2 py-1 text-xs">
            {LANGS.map(([code, label]) => <option key={code} value={code}>{label}</option>)}
          </select>
          <button onClick={create} className="rounded bg-emerald-500 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-black">+ New</button>
        </div>
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto">
          {rows.map((r) => (
            <button key={r.id} onClick={() => setSelected(r.id)} className={`block w-full border-b border-white/5 px-4 py-3 text-left ${selected === r.id ? "bg-white/[0.06]" : "hover:bg-white/[0.03]"}`}>
              <div className="flex justify-between text-xs"><span>{r.phone.replace("+91TEST_", "test-")}</span><span className="text-white/40">{(r.language ?? "en").toUpperCase()}</span></div>
              {r.lastMessage && <div className="mt-1 truncate text-[11px] text-white/50">{r.lastMessage}</div>}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </aside>
      <main className="flex flex-1 flex-col">
        {!selected ? (
          <div className="flex flex-1 items-center justify-center text-sm text-white/40">Create or select a test chat. Keep <code className="mx-1">npm run worker:dev</code> running to get replies.</div>
        ) : (
          <>
            <div className="min-h-0 flex-1"><Transcript id={selected} apiBase="/api/test-chat/conversations" pollMs={1500} /></div>
            <footer className="flex gap-2 border-t border-white/10 px-6 py-3">
              <textarea
                value={input}
                onChange={(e) => setInput(e.target.value)}
                onKeyDown={(e) => { if (e.key === "Enter" && !e.shiftKey) { e.preventDefault(); send(); } }}
                rows={2}
                placeholder="Type as the reporter… (Enter to send)"
                className="flex-1 resize-none rounded-md border border-white/10 bg-[#141414] px-3 py-2 text-sm focus:border-emerald-500/40 focus:outline-none"
              />
              <button onClick={send} disabled={!input.trim() || sending} className="rounded-md bg-emerald-500 px-4 text-sm font-medium text-black disabled:opacity-30">Send</button>
            </footer>
          </>
        )}
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach } from "vitest";
import { resetDb } from "../helpers/db";
import { computeHealth } from "@/server/health";
import { heartbeat, pauseClaude } from "@/server/worker/pause";
import { recordEvent } from "@/server/inbound/store";
import { stopBoss } from "@/server/queue/boss";
import { resetConfigForTests } from "@/server/config";

describe("computeHealth", () => {
  beforeEach(async () => { await stopBoss(); await resetDb(); });
  afterEach(async () => { await stopBoss(); delete process.env.CLAUDE_TOKEN_ISSUED_AT; resetConfigForTests(); });

  it("is 503 until the worker heartbeats, then 200", async () => {
    expect((await computeHealth()).status).toBe(503);
    await heartbeat();
    const h = await computeHealth();
    expect(h.status).toBe(200);
    expect(h.checks.db).toBe(true);
    expect(h.checks.workerHeartbeatAgeS).toBeLessThan(5);
  });

  it("is 503 after an auth error and reports the pause", async () => {
    await heartbeat();
    await pauseClaude(new Date(Date.now() + 60_000), "AUTH");

    expect(h.status).toBe(503);

  it("counts token days left from the issued date", async () => {
    await heartbeat();
    process.env.CLAUDE_TOKEN_ISSUED_AT = new Date(Date.now() - 300 * 86_400_000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    resetConfigForTests();
    const h = await computeHealth();
    expect(h.checks.tokenDaysLeft).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(64);
    expect(h.checks.tokenDaysLeft).toBeLessThanOrEqual(66);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/server/health.ts` and the route**

`src/server/health.ts`:

```ts
import { and, eq, gte } from "drizzle-orm";
import { getConfig } from "./config";
import { getDb, getSql } from "./db/client";
import { events } from "./db/schema";
import { getWorkerState } from "./worker/pause";
import { getBoss, TURN_QUEUE } from "./queue/boss";
import { log } from "./log";

const HEARTBEAT_MAX_S = 120;

export async function computeHealth(now: Date = new Date()) {
  const checks = {
    db: false,
    workerHeartbeatAgeS: null as number | null,
    lastOkTurnAgeS: null as number | null,
    claudePausedUntil: null as string | null,
    pauseReason: null as string | null,
    authErrorLastHour: false,
    tokenDaysLeft: null as number | null,
    queue: null as { queued: number; active: number; failed: number } | null,
  };
  try {
    await getSql()`select 1`;
    checks.db = true;
    const s = await getWorkerState();
    checks.workerHeartbeatAgeS = s.lastHeartbeatAt ? Math.round((now.getTime() - s.lastHeartbeatAt.getTime()) / 1000) : null;
    checks.lastOkTurnAgeS = s.lastOkTurnAt ? Math.round((now.getTime() - s.lastOkTurnAt.getTime()) / 1000) : null;
    checks.claudePausedUntil = s.claudePausedUntil && s.claudePausedUntil > now ? s.claudePausedUntil.toISOString() : null;
    checks.pauseReason = checks.claudePausedUntil ? s.pauseReason : null;
    const hourAgo = new Date(now.getTime() - 3_600_000);
    const auth = await getDb().select({ id: events.id }).from(events).where(and(eq(events.kind, "claude_auth_error"), gte(events.createdAt, hourAgo))).limit(1);
    checks.authErrorLastHour = auth.length > 0;
    try {
      const q = await (await getBoss()).getQueue(TURN_QUEUE);
      if (q) checks.queue = { queued: q.queuedCount, active: q.activeCount, failed: q.failedCount };
    } catch (err) {
      log.warn({ err }, "health: queue check failed");
    }
  } catch (err) {
    log.error({ err }, "health: db check failed");
  }
  const issued = getConfig().claude.tokenIssuedAt;
  if (issued) {
    const issuedAt = new Date(issued);
    if (!isNaN(issuedAt.getTime())) checks.tokenDaysLeft = Math.floor((issuedAt.getTime() + 365 * 86_400_000 - now.getTime()) / 86_400_000);
  }
  const ok = checks.db && checks.workerHeartbeatAgeS !== null && checks.workerHeartbeatAgeS <= HEARTBEAT_MAX_S && !checks.authErrorLastHour;
  return { ok, status: ok ? (200 as const) : (503 as const), checks };
}
```

### Task 26: Eval runner and the 22 scenarios

Implements spec §9 (eval suite). The runner drives `runTurn` directly against the test database seeded with the fixture directory, so scenario expectations can name fixture labels. Real-backend scenarios spend Max usage; run them deliberately.

**Files:**
- Create: `evals/run.ts`, `evals/assertions.ts`, `evals/scenarios/*.yaml` (22 files listed below), `tests/unit/evals/assertions.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (`assertions.ts`): `interface StepExpect { contains?: string[]; not_contains?: string[]; regex?: string; language?: Language; intent?: Intent; number_of?: string[]; no_foreign_digits?: boolean; no_dispatch?: boolean; turn_status?: "ok" | "fallback"; flagged?: boolean; message_count?: number; kind?: TurnOutcome["kind"] }`, `checkStep(expect: StepExpect, actual: { messages: { content: string }[]; turnStatus: string | null; structured: StructuredReply | null; flagged: boolean; outcomeKind: string; knownNumbers: Map<string, string>; allDirectoryNumbers: string[] }): string[]` (list of failure descriptions, empty when passing).
- Produces (`run.ts`): CLI `npm run eval [-- --only <name>] [--backend fake]`; exit code 1 on any failure; prints a table with name, safety flag, pass/fail, latency, and a P50/P95 summary.

```ts
  flagged: false, outcomeKind: "ok", knownNumbers: new Map([["Mumbai - Ghatkopar", "+917662005404"]]), allDirectoryNumbers: ["+917662005404"], ...extra,
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `evals/assertions.ts`**

```ts
import { detectLanguage } from "../src/server/lang";
import { formatIndianPhone } from "../src/server/messages/cards";
import { foreignDigitRuns, hasDispatchLanguage } from "../src/server/agent/scrub";
import type { StructuredReply } from "../src/server/agent/types";
import type { Intent, Language } from "../src/server/types";

export interface StepExpect {
  contains?: string[];
  not_contains?: string[];
  regex?: string;
  language?: Language;
  intent?: Intent;
  number_of?: string[];
  no_foreign_digits?: boolean;
  no_dispatch?: boolean;
  turn_status?: "ok" | "fallback";
  flagged?: boolean;
  message_count?: number;
  kind?: string;
}

export interface StepActual {
  messages: { content: string }[];
  turnStatus: string | null;
  structured: StructuredReply | null;
  flagged: boolean;
  outcomeKind: string;
  knownNumbers: Map<string, string>;
  allDirectoryNumbers: string[];
}

export function checkStep(expect: StepExpect, actual: StepActual): string[] {
  const fails: string[] = [];
  const text = actual.messages.map((m) => m.content).join("\n");
  for (const s of expect.contains ?? []) if (!text.includes(s)) fails.push(`contains: missing "${s}"`);
  for (const s of expect.not_contains ?? []) if (text.includes(s)) fails.push(`not_contains: found "${s}"`);
  if (expect.regex && !new RegExp(expect.regex, "i").test(text)) fails.push(`regex: ${expect.regex} did not match`);
  if (expect.language) {
    const lang = actual.structured?.language ?? detectLanguage(text);
    if (lang !== expect.language) fails.push(`language: expected ${expect.language}, got ${lang}`);
  }
  if (expect.intent && actual.structured?.intent !== expect.intent) fails.push(`intent: expected ${expect.intent}, got ${actual.structured?.intent ?? "none"}`);
  for (const label of expect.number_of ?? []) {
    const phone = actual.knownNumbers.get(label);
    if (!phone) fails.push(`number_of: unknown fixture label ${label}`);
    else if (!text.includes(formatIndianPhone(phone))) fails.push(`number_of: ${label} (${formatIndianPhone(phone)}) not in reply`);
  }
  if (expect.no_foreign_digits) {
    const foreign = foreignDigitRuns(text, actual.allDirectoryNumbers);
    if (foreign.length) fails.push(`foreign digits present: ${foreign.join(", ")}`);
  }
  if (expect.no_dispatch && hasDispatchLanguage(text)) fails.push("dispatch language present");
  if (expect.turn_status && actual.turnStatus !== expect.turn_status) fails.push(`turn_status: expected ${expect.turn_status}, got ${actual.turnStatus}`);
  if (expect.flagged !== undefined && actual.flagged !== expect.flagged) fails.push(`flagged: expected ${expect.flagged}, got ${actual.flagged}`);
  if (expect.message_count !== undefined && actual.messages.length !== expect.message_count) fails.push(`message_count: expected ${expect.message_count}, got ${actual.messages.length}`);
  if (expect.kind && actual.outcomeKind !== expect.kind) fails.push(`kind: expected ${expect.kind}, got ${actual.outcomeKind}`);
  return fails;
}
```

```ts
import { config as loadEnv } from "dotenv";
loadEnv({ path: ".env.local" });
loadEnv({ path: ".env" });
import { readdirSync, readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { parse as parseYaml } from "yaml";
import { asc, eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { migrate } from "drizzle-orm/postgres-js/migrator";
import { checkStep, type StepExpect } from "./assertions";
import { resetConfigForTests } from "../src/server/config";
import { getDb, closeDb } from "../src/server/db/client";
import { conversations, messages, turns } from "../src/server/db/schema";
import { storeReporterMessage } from "../src/server/inbound/store";
import { runTurn } from "../src/server/worker/turn-runner";
import { pauseClaude, clearClaudePause } from "../src/server/worker/pause";
import { getBackend } from "../src/server/agent";
import { FakeBackend, okReply } from "../src/server/agent/fake-backend";
import { resetCoverageCache } from "../src/server/directory/coverage";
import { loadActiveAmbulances, loadActiveClinics } from "../src/server/directory/repo";
import { resetDb } from "../tests/helpers/db";
import { seedDirectory } from "../tests/helpers/fixtures";
import type { StructuredReply } from "../src/server/agent/types";
import type { Language } from "../src/server/types";

interface Step {
  send?: string;
  send_burst?: string[];
  send_kind?: "audio" | "location";
  place?: string;
  expect?: StepExpect;
}
interface Scenario {
  name: string;
  safety?: boolean;
  language?: Language | null;
  first_contact?: boolean;
  backend?: "fake";
  fake_replies?: Partial<StructuredReply>[];
  pause_claude?: boolean;
  steps: Step[];
}

process.env.DATABASE_URL = process.env.TEST_DATABASE_URL ?? "postgres://arham:arham@localhost:5432/arham_test";
process.env.LOG_LEVEL ??= "warn";
resetConfigForTests();

const args = process.argv.slice(2);
const only = args.includes("--only") ? args[args.indexOf("--only") + 1] : null;
const forceFake = args.includes("--backend") && args[args.indexOf("--backend") + 1] === "fake";

async function runScenario(s: Scenario) {
  await resetDb();
  resetCoverageCache();
  await seedDirectory();
  await clearClaudePause();
  if (s.pause_claude) await pauseClaude(new Date(Date.now() + 3_600_000), "eval");
  const knownNumbers = new Map((await loadActiveAmbulances()).map((a) => [a.label, a.phone]));
  for (const c of await loadActiveClinics()) knownNumbers.set(c.label, c.phone);
  const allNumbers = [...knownNumbers.values(), process.env.HELPLINE_PHONE ?? ""];

  const backend = s.backend === "fake" || forceFake
    ? new FakeBackend((s.fake_replies ?? []).map((r) => okReply({ reply: r.reply ?? "", ...r })))
    : getBackend();

  const [conv] = await getDb()
    .insert(conversations)
    .values({ phone: `+91TEST_eval_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`, isTest: true, language: s.language ?? null, welcomedAt: s.first_contact ? null : new Date() })
    .returning();

  const failures: string[] = [];
  const latencies: number[] = [];
  for (const [i, step] of s.steps.entries()) {
    const before = (await getDb().select({ id: messages.id }).from(messages).where(eq(messages.conversationId, conv.id))).length;
    const texts = step.send_burst ?? (step.send !== undefined ? [step.send] : step.send_kind ? [""] : []);
    for (const t of texts) {
      await storeReporterMessage(conv.id, step.send_kind === "audio"
        ? { kind: "audio", content: "", mediaUrl: "https://example.invalid/a.ogg" }
        : step.send_kind === "location"
          ? { kind: "location", content: step.place ?? "", placeLabel: step.place ?? null, lat: 18.5, lng: 73.8 }
          : { kind: "text", content: t });
    }
    const started = Date.now();
    const outcome = await runTurn(conv.id, { backend });
    latencies.push(Date.now() - started);
    const all = await getDb().select().from(messages).where(eq(messages.conversationId, conv.id)).orderBy(asc(messages.createdAt));
    const newAssistant = all.slice(before + texts.length).filter((m) => m.role === "assistant");
    const [row] = await getDb().select().from(conversations).where(eq(conversations.id, conv.id));
    const turnRows = await getDb().select().from(turns).where(eq(turns.conversationId, conv.id)).orderBy(asc(turns.createdAt));
    const lastTurn = turnRows[turnRows.length - 1] ?? null;
    const fails = checkStep(step.expect ?? {}, {
      messages: newAssistant.map((m) => ({ content: m.content })),
      turnStatus: lastTurn?.status ?? null,
      structured: (lastTurn?.structuredOutput as StructuredReply | null) ?? null,
      flagged: row.flagged,
      outcomeKind: outcome.kind,
      knownNumbers,
      allDirectoryNumbers: allNumbers,
    });
    for (const f of fails) failures.push(`step ${i + 1}: ${f}`);
    if (fails.length) failures.push(`step ${i + 1} reply: ${newAssistant.map((m) => m.content).join(" ⏎ ").slice(0, 300)}`);
  }
  return { failures, latencies };
}

async function main() {
  await migrate(getDb(), { migrationsFolder: "src/server/db/migrations" });
  const dir = join(process.cwd(), "evals", "scenarios");
  const files = readdirSync(dir).filter((f) => f.endsWith(".yaml")).sort();
  const results: { name: string; safety: boolean; ok: boolean; failures: string[]; latencies: number[] }[] = [];
  for (const f of files) {
    const s = parseYaml(readFileSync(join(dir, f), "utf8")) as Scenario;
    if (only && s.name !== only) continue;
    process.stdout.write(`▶ ${s.name} … `);
    const { failures, latencies } = await runScenario(s);
    results.push({ name: s.name, safety: !!s.safety, ok: failures.length === 0, failures, latencies });
    console.log(failures.length === 0 ? "PASS" : "FAIL");
    for (const line of failures) console.log(`    ${line}`);
  }
  const all = results.flatMap((r) => r.latencies).sort((a, b) => a - b);
  const pct = (p: number) => (all.length ? all[Math.min(all.length - 1, Math.floor((p / 100) * all.length))] : 0);
  const passed = results.filter((r) => r.ok).length;
  const safetyFailed = results.filter((r) => r.safety && !r.ok).length;
  console.log(`\n${passed}/${results.length} scenarios passed · safety failures: ${safetyFailed} · turn latency P50 ${pct(50)} ms, P95 ${pct(95)} ms`);
  await closeDb();
  process.exit(passed === results.length ? 0 : 1);
}

main().catch((e) => { console.error(e); process.exit(1); });
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write `CLAUDE.md`**

```markdown
# Arham Always Care — WhatsApp Helpline Bot (V2)

Question-and-answer WhatsApp helpline for Arham Always Care, the animal-rescue project of Arham Yuva Seva Group. People ask for the animal-ambulance number for their area, clinic details, or how to donate or volunteer. The bot answers in English, Hindi, Marathi or Gujarati from the directory. It is not a dispatcher: there is no escalation queue, no follow-ups, no cron jobs.

Design spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-25-v2-qa-helpline-design.md`. Implementation plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-25-v2-core-bot.md`.

## Hard rules

1. **The model never types phone digits.** Tools return opaque handles; the reply carries `{{AMB:handle}}`, `{{CLINIC:handle}}` or `{{PHONE:helpline}}` tokens; `src/server/agent/render.ts` substitutes real numbers; `src/server/agent/scrub.ts` strips any other 7+ digit run.
2. **No dispatching language.** We hand over a number; the reporter calls. The dispatch guard replaces offending sentences.
3. **Time to number is the metric.** When Claude is paused or fails, `src/server/worker/fallback.ts` still sends a directory card or the helpline number.
4. **Multilingual.** en / hi / mr / gu everywhere: cards, welcome, fallback lines, prompts.
5. **Idempotent ingress.** Dedup by Interakt message id; one turn per conversation at a time (pg-boss singleton + advisory lock).
6. **Visible degradation.** Every failure is a `turns` row, an `events` row, or a health-endpoint signal. Nothing fails silently.
7. **Test conversations never reach Interakt** (`is_test`, phones `+91TEST_…`).

## Architecture

Interakt → `POST /api/webhook` (verify, dedup, store, enqueue) → Postgres + pg-boss `turn` queue → worker (`src/server/worker`) → Claude Sonnet 5 via `@anthropic-ai/claude-agent-sdk` under the owner's Max plan (`CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN`) with three in-process tools → render + scrub → Interakt send. Admin at `/admin` (password cookie) with transcripts and turn details; test harness at `/test-chat`.

## Working here

- Local: `docker compose -f docker/docker-compose.dev.yml up -d`, `npm run migrate`, `npm run ingest:ambulances`, `npm run dev` and `npm run worker:dev`.
- Tests: `npm run test:unit` (pure), `npm test` (needs the dev Postgres, uses `arham_test`). Evals: `npm run eval` (spends Max usage; `-- --backend fake` for a dry run).
- When launching anything that calls Claude from inside a Claude Code session, prefix with `env -u CLAUDECODE`.
- Change the prompt (`src/server/agent/prompt.ts`) only with the eval suite green.
- `ambulances-clinics.csv` is gitignored (driver personal numbers). `.codex/`, `.mcp.json`, `.env*` are gitignored.
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write `README.md`**

```markdown
# Arham Always Care WhatsApp helpline

Q&A helpline bot on WhatsApp (Interakt) for Arham Always Care. Reporters ask for the animal-ambulance number for their area, clinic details, donations or volunteering; the bot answers in English, Hindi, Marathi or Gujarati from the directory. Phone numbers are always inserted by code, never typed by the model.

## Stack

Next.js 16 (webhook + admin), Node worker, Postgres 17, pg-boss queue, Claude Sonnet 5 through the Claude Agent SDK, Docker.

## Local development

1. `cp .env.example .env.local` and fill in Interakt keys, `HELPLINE_PHONE`, `ADMIN_PASSWORD`, `SESSION_SECRET`. For Claude, run `claude setup-token` and set `CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN` (or leave it unset if `claude login` is already done on this machine).
2. `docker compose -f docker/docker-compose.dev.yml up -d`
3. `npm install && npm run migrate`
4. Put the directory spreadsheet at `ambulances-clinics.csv` and run `npm run ingest:ambulances`.
5. Terminal A: `npm run dev`. Terminal B: `npm run worker:dev`.
6. Open http://localhost:3000/admin, log in, then http://localhost:3000/test-chat to talk to the bot.

For real WhatsApp traffic locally, expose port 3000 (e.g. ngrok) and point the Interakt webhook at `/api/webhook` with the same `INTERAKT_WEBHOOK_SECRET`.

## Commands

| Command | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `npm test` | all tests (needs the dev Postgres; uses the `arham_test` database) |
| `npm run test:unit` | pure unit tests |
| `npm run eval` | 22 conversation scenarios through the real worker (spends Max usage); `-- --backend fake` for a dry run; `-- --only <name>` for one |
| `npm run db:generate` | generate a migration after editing `src/server/db/schema.ts` |
| `npm run migrate` | apply migrations to `DATABASE_URL` |
| `npm run worker` | run the worker (production) |

## How a message flows

1. Interakt calls `POST /api/webhook`; the app verifies the HMAC, dedups by message id, stores the message and enqueues a `turn` job. No LLM work happens here.
2. The worker picks the job (one per conversation at a time), gathers every unanswered message, and either sends the welcome menu, the menu, the "please type" line for voice notes, or runs a Claude turn.
3. Claude gets the coverage list, the transcript and three tools that return handles instead of numbers. Its structured reply contains tokens; code substitutes the real numbers and partner labels, scrubs stray digits, replaces dispatch language, sends via Interakt and records a `turns` row.
4. If Claude is paused (usage limit, auth problem) or fails, the fallback path answers from the directory alone and flags the conversation.

## Operations

- `/api/health` returns 200 when the database is up, the worker heartbeated within 2 minutes, and no auth error happened in the last hour; it also reports the Claude pause state and days left on the token.
- The Claude token lasts one year: rerun `claude setup-token`, update the env, restart the worker. Set `CLAUDE_TOKEN_ISSUED_AT` so the health endpoint can count down.
- Azure deployment, backups and the take-over UI are described in the follow-up plan.
```
